### [1] [ARTA: Graph Combinatorial Optimization for Robust Interbeat Interval Estimation from Wearable Electrocardiogram Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00011)
*Shovito Barua Soumma,Asiful Arefeen,Hassan Ghasemzadeh*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: ARTA是一个基于DAG与束搜索动态规划的免训练组合框架，融合多检测器候选，输出生理合理的逐拍序列，显著降低运动伪迹下的HR/IBI误差且计算极轻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴ECG连续监测需准确HR与IBI，但运动伪迹产生类QRS假峰并掩盖真实R峰；传统检测器与局部区间修复规则逐次独立处理检测结果，连续多拍被污染时易失效，缺乏全局时序一致性建模。

Method: 将多个ECG检测器的候选峰作为节点构建有向无环图，边仅连接生理可行的RR区间；节点置信度由检测器一致性与局部ECG质量构成，转移代价依历史IBI一致性与软心率先验而定；用束搜索动态规划选取全局低代价一致路径，抑制假检并借助辅助候选恢复漏检。

Result: IEEE SP Cup运动伪迹数据集（-2~12 dB）上，HR MAE为1.79 bpm，较最强单检测器基线降低54%，92.38%窗口在±5 bpm内，R峰F1为0.95，IBI MAE 6.52 ms；欧洲ST-T库完成跨数据集迁移评估；无参考标注的可靠性分析考察条件性能。单线程CPU约27倍实时，峰值内存<2.1 MB。

Conclusion: 以图搜索实现全局时序对齐的ARTA在强伪迹下优于单检测器与局部修复方法，且单通道、免训练、可解释、轻量，适于可穿戴与长程动态ECG场景。

Abstract: Accurate heart rate (HR) and interbeat interval (IBI) estimation from wearable electrocardiogram (ECG) is essential for continuous physiological monitoring, but motion artifacts can create false QRS-like peaks and obscure true R-peaks. Conventional detectors and local interval-repair rules process detections independently and may become unreliable when several consecutive beats are corrupted. We present ARTA (Artifact-Robust Temporal Alignment), a graph-based combinatorial framework that estimates the heartbeat sequence through constrained temporal optimization. ARTA combines candidates from multiple ECG detectors, represents them as nodes in a directed acyclic graph and connects candidates through physiologically plausible RR intervals. Candidate confidence uses detector agreement and local ECG quality, while history-dependent transition costs capture recent IBI consistency and a soft heart-rate prior. Beam-search dynamic programming selects a coherent low-cost path across the recording, suppressing false detections and recovering missed beats when plausible auxiliary candidates are available. On the IEEE Signal Processing Cup exercise dataset with electrode-motion artifacts from -2 to 12 dB, ARTA achieved an HR mean absolute error of 1.79 beats per minute, 54% lower than the strongest single-detector baseline, with 92.38% of windows within +/-5 beats per minute, an R-peak F1 score of 0.95, and an IBI mean absolute error of 6.52 ms. Cross-dataset evaluation on the European ST-T Database further assessed transfer to long clinical ambulatory recordings and a label-free reliability analysis examined conditional performance without using reference annotations for segment selection. ARTA is single-channel, training-free, interpretable and computationally lightweight, operating approximately 27 times faster than real time with peak memory below 2.1 MB in a single-thread CPU simulation.

</details>


### [2] [Dataset and Experimental Testbed for Deep Learning-Based Bluetooth Device Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00332)
*Haytham Albousayri,Bechir Hamdaoui*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文针对AI赋能BLE网络的泛化性问题，构建了一个大规模、多场景采集的BLE原始IQ数据集，并基于该数据集对域鲁棒射频指纹识别（RFFP）的先进方法开展了基准评测案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型已可嵌入BLE物理层完成环境感知、定位、RFFP与信号去噪等任务，但它们在训练配置下表现良好，一旦部署到未见环境（不同房间、温度条件、载波频率）便显著失效。要解决该问题，需要在未见配置下对模型与算法进行严格的基准评测，以衡量其泛化性与鲁棒性，而当前缺乏相应的数据基础。

Method: 论文作出两项贡献：一是构建全面、大规模的BLE无线数据集，采集31台不同商用设备的原始IQ传输数据，覆盖不同日期、地点、环境、温度、接收机硬件及频点；二是基于该数据集开展案例研究，评测域鲁棒RFFP的SOTA技术。

Result: 摘要未给出具体量化结果，仅说明数据集涵盖31台商用设备及多维度采集条件（日期、位置、环境、温度、接收硬件、频点），并据此完成了对域鲁棒RFFP先进方法的评估。

Conclusion: 大规模、多域偏移的BLE IQ数据集为评估模型泛化与鲁棒性提供了基准平台，有助于提升真实场景下AI（尤其在RFFP任务中）的可靠性。

Abstract: AI-enabled wireless networks have emerged as vital components of future-generation wireless communication systems. Among various wireless architectures, Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) networks have attracted significant research interest due to their widespread adoption and low cost. In these networks, deep learning (DL) models can be integrated directly into the physical-layer infrastructure to perform diverse tasks, including environment sensing, localization, radio frequency fingerprinting (RFFP), and signal denoising. While highly effective under their original training configurations, these DL models often fail significantly when deployed in unseen environments---such as different rooms, varying temperature conditions, or altered carrier frequencies. Addressing this limitation requires rigorous benchmarking of models and algorithms against unseen configurations to evaluate their generalization and robustness. To bridge this gap, this paper makes two key contributions: i) we introduce a comprehensive, large-scale wireless BLE dataset consisting of raw IQ transmissions from 31 distinct commercial devices captured across diverse days, locations, environments, temperatures, receiver hardware, and frequency channels; ii) we present a case study evaluating state-of-the-art techniques for domain-robust RFFP to advance real-world AI reliability.

</details>


### [3] [Wideband Channel Measurements in the Lower FR3 Band (6-12 GHz) for Industrial Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00426)
*Faizan Rauf,Adam Umra,Oways Alsoloh,Aydin Sezgin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文在工业车间开展6–12 GHz宽带信道测量（135个LOS点、30个被机器人遮挡的NLOS点），校验3GPP InF模型在FR3频段的适用性，发现LOS平均路损吻合但阴影衰落被高估，机器人遮挡损耗随频率增大（10.3→13.0 dB），并显著抬升方向性时延扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FR3（7.125–24.25 GHz）是6G关键候选频段，但工业环境信道实测稀缺；3GPP InF模型基于全向天线开发，尚未在FR3宽带范围内得到验证。

Method: 在工业生产大厅开展6–12 GHz宽带测量：LOS场景135个位置（1–18 m），NLOS场景30个位于工业机器人后方位置（2.5–16.5 m）；分析路损指数、close-in拟合与3GPP InF-LOS对比、阴影衰落、遮挡附加损耗及方向性/全向时延扩展。

Result: LOS路损指数1.87–2.12，close-in拟合与3GPP InF-LOS偏差约2 dB内；阴影衰落仅1.4–1.7 dB，远低于模型4.3 dB。NLOS下机器人附加损耗在6.5–11.5 GHz由10.3增至13.0 dB，与绕射一致；遮挡直射径使平均方向性时延扩展由约5 ns升至25 ns，接近全向值。

Conclusion: 3GPP InF模型能较好刻画LOS平均路损，但高估了波束对齐FR3链路的衰落变异性；FR3遮挡损耗具频率依赖性。数据集与处理代码公开。

Abstract: The upper mid-band, also known as Frequency Range 3 (FR3), spans 7.125-24.25 GHz and is a key candidate band for sixth-generation (6G) wireless communications. However, channel measurements in industrial environments remain scarce, and the 3GPP indoor-factory (InF) channel model, developed for omnidirectional antennas, has yet to be validated across the FR3 band. This paper presents a wideband channel measurement campaign in an industrial production hall, covering 135 line-of-sight (LOS) positions at 1-18 m and 30 non-line-of-sight (NLOS) positions behind an industrial robot at 2.5-16.5 m across 6-12 GHz. In LOS, the path-loss exponent ranged from 1.87 to 2.12, and the close-in fits agreed with the 3GPP InF-LOS model to within approximately 2 dB. However, shadow fading was only 1.4-1.7 dB, compared with the model's 4.3 dB. In NLOS, the robot caused an excess loss that increased from 10.3 to 13.0 dB between 6.5 and 11.5 GHz, consistent with diffraction. Blocking the direct path raised the mean directional delay spread from approximately 5 to 25 ns, close to omnidirectional values. These results indicate that the 3GPP InF model captures mean LOS path loss well but overestimates the variability of beam-aligned FR3 links, and that blockage loss in FR3 is frequency dependent. The dataset and processing code are publicly available.

</details>


### [4] [Sense Less, Miss Less: Evidence-Driven Sensing Schedules for ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00439)
*Seyed Bagher Hashemi Natanzi,Bo Tang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于CuSum变化检测的证据驱动感知调度策略：无运动迹象时暂停感知、一旦疑似运动则连续感知；在相同监测预算、低于每分钟一次虚警条件下，200 ms内的肢体动作检出数为周期感知的1.5–4.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC中每次感知传输都会占用本可用于数据传输的资源，而长期无运动时周期性感知浪费资源；反之，若感知间隔过长，静止人员短暂的手势可能完整落在两次传输之间而被漏检。因此需要在有限监测预算下以少量探测换取高灵敏的事件捕获。

Method: 以数据高效的累积和（CuSum）变化检测为核心构建"证据驱动"调度：测量表明无运动时暂停感知，证据提示运动时转入连续感知；方法适配OFDM感知场景，处理感知请求被数据业务阻塞、测量延迟到达等问题；并采用带关节肢体运动的铰接人体模型进行仿真评估，与周期感知及调优的双速率触发方案对比。

Result: 在无墙面反射仿真中，相同监测预算、虚警率低于每分钟一次时，200 ms窗口内检出肢体运动数为周期感知的1.5–4.2倍；调优的双速率触发性能相近；在含墙面反射的仿真房间中，不重新调参、不匹配代价的情况下相对周期感知的增益依然保持。

Conclusion: 在小预算条件下，应由测量证据而非固定周期决定何时感知；CuSum式证据驱动调度能在严格虚警约束下显著提高短时肢体动作的捕获率，并对多径环境与实现非理想性具有稳健性。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) lets a base station detect human motion with the same signals it uses for data, but every sensing transmission takes resources from users. Sensing rarely while nothing moves saves resources, yet a brief hand gesture by an otherwise still person can then start and end between two transmissions and go unnoticed. We propose an evidence-driven schedule, based on data-efficient cumulative-sum (CuSum) change detection, that pauses sensing while measurements indicate no motion and senses continuously once they suggest motion. It is adapted to orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) sensing, where requests can be blocked by data traffic and measurements arrive late, and evaluated with an articulated human model of limb motion. In simulations without wall reflections, with the same monitoring budget and below one false alarm per minute, it detects 1.5--4.2 times as many limb movements within 200~ms as periodic sensing. A tuned two-rate trigger performs similarly, and the gain over periodic sensing persists, without retuning or cost matching, in a simulated room with wall reflections. Under a small budget, measurements should decide when to sense.

</details>


### [5] [FESTiVE: Self-calibrated Spatial-temporal Gradient Characterization for Non-cartesian Imaging on High Performance Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00472)
*Zachary Shah,Daniel R. Abraham,Nan Wang,Xiaozhi Cao,Adam B. Kerr,Kawin Setsompop*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出FESTiVE：用并行成像（时变GRAPPA映射 + 隐式神经表示）替代外部磁场监测硬件，从k空间数据中隐式建模并显式恢复时空梯度场不完美性，实现高性能梯度系统上的高分辨非笛卡尔成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度不完美性破坏长读出成像（如螺旋、EPI）在高性能梯度系统上的编码收益；目前唯一定量手段是外部磁场监测硬件（如field camera），成本高、通用性受限，且在高阶/强梯度场景可能受探针退相位限制。

Method: 以快速k-t标定扫描建立时变GRAPPA问题，将受时变梯度误差污染的k空间数据映射到干净参考；用隐式神经表示紧凑参数化该时空映射；结合线圈灵敏度在图像域显式恢复时空场分布。方法学上属于数据驱动的高阶场表征，替代高阶场监测。

Result: 体模中，与Skope field camera在多种非笛卡尔2D采集策略与梯度规格下得到可比的场测量；活体中，在仅头部MAGNUS 3T上于高性能扩散螺旋序列（高阶相位误差显著）获得SOTA图像质量；7T Siemens TerraX上实现300μm螺旋成像，而传统field camera方法已低于探针退相位极限。

Conclusion: FESTiVE证明可用并行成像而非场监测硬件来表征梯度不完美性，从而在高性能梯度系统上实现稳健的高分辨非笛卡尔成像，具备替代外部硬件监测的潜力。

Abstract: Gradient imperfections disrupt the encoding benefits of long-readout imaging applications on high-performance systems. Currently, external field monitoring hardware provides the only reliable solution for general spatial-temporal gradient characterization. We present FESTiVE, a data-driven alternative to higher-order field monitoring. FESTiVE leverages parallel imaging to both implicitly model and explicitly recover field imperfections. Using a fast k-t mapping calibration scan, FESTiVE formulates a time-varying GRAPPA problem which maps k-space data corrupted by a time-varying gradient imperfection against a clean reference. This mapping is represented compactly with an implicit neural representation, from which the spatial-temporal fields are recovered in image space using coil sensitivities. In phantom, FESTiVE produces comparable field measurements to a Skope field camera across multiple non-cartesian 2D acquisition strategies and gradient specifications. In vivo, FESTiVE achieves state-of-the-art image quality for a high-performance diffusion spiral protocol on the head-only MAGNUS 3T, where higher-order phase imperfections are significant. At 7T, on the head-only Siemens TerraX system, FESTiVE enables 300μm spiral imaging where conventional field camera-based approaches fall below probe dephasing limits. In conclusion, FESTiVE enables robust high-resolution non-cartesian imaging on high-performance gradients, characterizing gradient imperfections using parallel imaging rather than field monitoring hardware.

</details>


### [6] [RayAR: Autoregressive Dominant Path Generation for Low-Overhead MIMO Beam Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00560)
*Junxi Huang,Xiucheng Wang,Nan Cheng,Kailong Wang,Jiang Guo,Yuxiang Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: RayAR：利用城市几何的自回归主导路径生成方法，通过降低探测开销实现高效波束对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIMO波束对齐需在有限测量开销下选择合适收发波束对。3GPP R18虽研究基于部分测量的波束预测，但测量本身仍带来探测开销。几何信息可借助主导路径的离开/到达方向聚焦探测，而识别这些路径需处理耦合的绕射交互。

Method: 提出RayAR自回归方法：从城市几何生成主导传播路径。以路径前缀、局部绕射几何与接收机可达性为条件对候选路径排序，每次选择后更新上下文，直至接收机可见。由完整路径的离开/到达方向对波束对排序，从少量候选中选择接收功率最高的波束对进行探测。

Result: 在3GPP R18波束管理指标下，相较穷举扫描减少96.88%探测开销，并在同等预算下取得最高最优波束对命中率。真实测量显示仿真训练的RayAR无需微调即取得最低的离开/到达角误差，平均到达方向误差9.07°。

Conclusion: 几何引导的自回归路径生成可显著降低波束对齐的探测开销并提升角域精度，仿真训练模型具备良好真实场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Beam alignment in multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems requires selecting suitable transmit and receive beam pairs with limited measurement overhead. Although 3GPP Release 18 investigates beam prediction from partial measurements, collecting these measurements still incurs probing overhead. Geometry can focus this probing through the departure and arrival directions of dominant paths, whose identification requires accounting for coupled diffraction interactions. To address this challenge, we propose RayAR, an autoregressive method that generates dominant propagation paths from urban geometry. RayAR conditions candidate ranking on the path prefix, local diffraction geometry, and receiver reachability, updating this context after each selection until the receiver becomes visible. The completed path's departure and arrival directions rank beam pairs for probing, allowing the pair with the highest received power to be selected from a small candidate set. Under 3GPP Release 18 beam-management metrics, RayAR reduces probing by 96.88\% relative to exhaustive sweeping and achieves the highest optimal-pair hit rate among compared methods at the same budget. Real-world measurements further demonstrate the accuracy of this geometric guidance, with simulation-trained RayAR achieving the lowest departure and arrival angular errors among compared methods without fine-tuning and a mean arrival-direction error of 9.07$^\circ$.

</details>


### [7] [Synergy-Aware Symbol Allocation for Distributed Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00648)
*Fan Gao,Youzheng Wang,Zhijin Qin,Xiaoming Tao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出"协同信用"分布式JSCC框架，以可微分的均衡后矩阵Rényi协同信用作为替代奖励，引导各终端保留跨终端互补语义，实现无额外开销的独立编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态感知与协同感知中，单终端仅获部分视角，完整任务语义需在接收端融合多终端互补观测才显现；但上行预算紧张，现有任务导向JSCC与分布式IB方法按终端逐一评估率失真权衡，低估跨终端协同特征并过早剪枝。

Method: 构造可微分的"均衡后矩阵Rényi协同信用"，作为互补任务证据经无线信道存活程度的替代奖励，并将其梯度回传至本地符号门控；部署时移除信用分支并冻结二值符号掩码，各终端独立编码，无需终端间信令或额外推理开销。

Result: 在parity信源上恢复出解析的符号选择阈值；相较无信用门控基线，AV-MNIST载荷符号减少28.4%、PAMAP2减少32.9%；在3GPP TDL-C衰落与导频辅助信道估计下、-6 dB时精度提升6.2个点。

Conclusion: 以协同信用替代逐终端率失真评估，可在紧约束上行下抑制互补语义的过早剪枝，兼顾通信开销降低与低信噪比下的协同感知精度，且部署零额外开销。

Abstract: Distributed semantic communication enables spatially separated terminals to support collaborative inference over wireless links for 6G edge intelligence. In multimodal sensing and cooperative perception, a single terminal captures only a partial view, and full task semantics emerge only when complementary observations from multiple terminals are fused at the receiver. However, under tight uplink budgets, existing task-oriented joint source--channel coding (JSCC) and distributed information bottleneck (IB) methods evaluate rate--distortion trade-offs per terminal, tending to undervalue cross-terminal synergistic features and prune them prematurely. To address this issue, we propose a synergy-credited distributed JSCC (\method{}) framework that introduces a differentiable, post-equalization matrix Rényi synergy credit as a surrogate to reward complementary task evidence surviving the wireless channel and backpropagates its gradients to local symbol gates. At deployment, the credit branch is removed and binary symbol masks are frozen, allowing each terminal to encode independently without inter-terminal signaling or extra inference overhead. Experiments show that \method{} recovers the analytical symbol-selection threshold on parity sources and reduces payload symbols by 28.4\% on AV-MNIST and 32.9\% on PAMAP2 over an uncredited gated baseline, while achieving a 6.2-point accuracy gain at $-6$~dB under 3GPP TDL-C fading with pilot-aided channel estimation for low-overhead wireless cooperative sensing.

</details>


### [8] [Robust 3DMA GNSS Positioning in Urban Areas Using Conservative Candidate Set Determination Based on GNSS Shadow and Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00696)
*Sanghyun Kim,Jiwon Seo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种保守候选集确定策略，融合AI分类器两个最可能类别的候选区域，以提升ZSRM在卫星信号误分类下的定位鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境中建筑物遮挡与反射导致GNSS定位性能下降，3DMA类方法中的ZSRM依赖预测的阴影/反射区域确定候选接收机位置集，但仅采用AI分类器最可能信号接收状态时会因信号误分类而定位失败。

Method: 不再只使用AI分类器输出的最可能信号接收类别，而是将概率最高的两个类别所对应的候选区域进行合并，从而更保守地确定候选接收机位置集，降低误分类导致的失败风险。

Result: 基于真实城市GNSS数据评估：RF、GBDT、SVM分类器下定位成功率均提升至100%；现有ZSRM单分类器输出仅为8.2%–30.1%，一致投票ZSRM为96.6%。虽然保留额外候选区域使定位边界增大，但仍小于一致投票ZSRM的边界。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持较小定位边界的同时显著提升ZSRM在城市GNSS环境下对卫星信号误分类的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In urban environments, global navigation satellite system (GNSS) positioning is often degraded by signal blockage and reflection caused by surrounding buildings. To address this problem, various three-dimensional mapping-aided (3DMA) GNSS techniques have been proposed. However, ZSRM remains vulnerable to satellite signal misclassification, which may lead to positioning failure. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a conservative candidate set determination strategy for robust ZSRM positioning. Instead of using only the most probable signal reception condition from an artificial intelligence (AI)-based classifier, the proposed method combines the candidate regions associated with the two most probable classes. This allows the candidate receiver position set to be determined more conservatively, reducing the risk of positioning failure under signal misclassification. The proposed method was evaluated using real-world urban GNSS data. Experimental results show that the proposed method improved the positioning success rate to 100% for RF, GBDT, and SVM classifiers, compared with 8.2%-30.1% for the existing ZSRM algorithm using individual classifier outputs and 96.6% for ZSRM with unanimous voting. Although the proposed method increased the position bounds by retaining additional candidate regions, it produced smaller position bounds than ZSRM with unanimous voting. These results demonstrate that the proposed method substantially enhances the robustness of ZSRM under satellite signal misclassification in urban GNSS environments.

This article first explores the capabilities and applications of cooperative embodied agents. The article concludes with open challenges and future research directions for reliable and efficient embodied cooperative radio mapping in complex environments.

TL;DR: 研究了underlay认知无线电环境反向散射（CR-AmBC）系统在保护主网络约束下的性能最大化问题，提出联合优化发射功率与反射系数的交替优化框架。

Abstract: The integration of cognitive radio (CR) technology with backscatter communication offers a promising solution for developing spectrum-efficient and low-power wireless networks. The objective is to maximize the performance of the CR-AmBC system while protecting the PN from interference. Differing from prior work, our formulation jointly accounts for: (i) interference from the PN on the CR receivers, (ii) interference from the backscatter device (BD) on the PN receiver, and (iii) imperfect successive interference cancellation (i-SIC) at the secondary destination. We propose a joint transmit power and reflection coefficient optimization framework that incorporates the minimum data rate requirements, the PN interference constraint, and i-SIC for decoding the BD signal. The nonconvex problem is decomposed into two subproblems solved via alternating optimization: (a) a closed-form expression for the feasible transmit power given a fixed reflection coefficient, and (b) an efficient one-dimensional search for the reflection coefficient. We prove that the power update is optimal, reducing the joint problem to a one-dimensional search, with monotone convergence and $\mathcal{O}(KN_β)$ complexity for $K$ iterations and $N_β$ search points. We present a comprehensive simulation-based performance evaluation and the results demonstrate that the proposed joint transmission power and reflection coefficient optimization provides an effective strategy to maximize the performance of the CR-AmBC system without violating the PN protection constraints.

</details>

*Zoran Hadzi-Velkov,Slavche Pejoski,Jia Guo,Marija Poposka,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对PASS近场波束聚焦对用户位移敏感、影响NOMA中SIC可解码性的问题，本文提出在有限服务区内联合优化天线放置与功率分配的鲁棒PASS-NOMA设计，包含针对准静态用户的最坏情况设计和针对遍历码字的遍历设计，并刻画可达速率域。结果表明除强用户低速率情形外，NOMA在共享放置下均优于OMA；在遍历情形下该优势甚至可对抗逐用户重配置天线的OMA。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PASS通过介质波导上的辐射点实现近场波束聚焦，但相干球面波叠加使信道对用户微小位移高度敏感。在功率域NOMA中，SIC要求弱用户层在强用户处仍可解码，这一敏感性因此成为关键瓶颈，需要空间鲁棒的设计。

Method: 在有界服务区上联合优化天线放置与功率分割，预设解码顺序并引入显式SIC可行性约束；分别提出面向准静态用户的最坏情况设计与面向跨越代表性服务区位置的码字的遍历设计，并刻画二者的可达速率域。

Result: 数值结果表明：尽管SIC在两个服务区均需解码弱用户，在共享放置下NOMA在两种鲁棒模型下均优于OMA，仅当强用户以低速率被服务时例外；在遍历情形下，即使OMA采用逐用户天线重配置，NOMA的优势仍可保持。

Conclusion: 空间鲁棒的PASS-NOMA设计可在用户位移敏感性与SIC可行性约束下有效利用NOMA增益，且其相对OMA的优势在共享放置下普遍成立，并在遍历情形下对更灵活的天线重配置OMA仍具鲁棒性。

Abstract: Pinching-antenna systems (PASS) enable near-field beam focusing using radiating points along a dielectric waveguide, but coherent spherical-wave superposition makes their channels sensitive to small user displacements. This sensitivity is critical for power-domain non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA), where successive interference cancellation (SIC) requires the weak-user layer to remain decodable at the strong user. We develop spatially robust PASS-NOMA designs that optimize antenna placement and power splitting over bounded service areas (SAs), with a preset decoding order and explicit SIC-feasibility constraints. We propose a worst-case design for quasi-static users and an ergodic design for codewords spanning representative SA positions, and characterize their achievable rate regions. Numerical results show that, despite SIC requiring weak-user decoding in both SAs, NOMA outperforms orthogonal multiple access (OMA) with a shared placement under both robustness models except when the strong user is served at low rate. In the ergodic regime, this advantage can persist even against OMA with per-user antenna reconfiguration.

</details>

*Yewen Cao,Fen Hou,Yulin Shao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对Rank-2 DFT-s-OFDM提出CLASS方案，通过在DFT扩展前选择性交换两层星座符号来降低PAPR，并利用循环分配模式与共享基波合成以有限IDFT预算实现波形分集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rank-2 DFT-s-OFDM在6G上行中重获关注，除空间复用增益外还提供降低PAPR的额外自由度；如何低成本地挖掘该自由度以提升波形分集、降低最差层PAPR是本文动机。

Method: 在DFT扩展前选择性交换两层星座符号（保持信息不变、重塑波形）；提出Cyclic Layer Assignment with Shared-basis Synthesis (CLASS)：用精心设计的循环分配模式生成多样候选波形，并复用少量波形分量避免逐个合成；在最小基波约束下、等分组可行时证明循环设计在最大化波形分集上的最优性。

Conclusion: CLASS以极低的合成开销有效利用Rank-2 DFT-s-OFDM的层间符号交换自由度，实现显著PAPR降低并具理论最优性保证，为6G上行PAPR抑制提供高效方案。

Abstract: Rank-2 discrete Fourier transform-spread orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (DFT-s-OFDM) is attracting renewed interest for 6G uplink, offering not only spatial multiplexing gains but also an additional degree of freedom for peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR) reduction. We exploit this freedom by selectively exchanging constellation symbols between the two layers before DFT spreading, preserving the transmitted information while reshaping the resulting waveforms. Building on this idea, we propose Cyclic Layer Assignment with Shared-basis Synthesis (CLASS). CLASS employs carefully designed cyclic assignment patterns to generate diverse waveform candidates, while reusing a small number of waveform components to avoid synthesizing each candidate separately. We further establish the optimality of the cyclic design in maximizing waveform diversity under a minimum-basis constraint when equal group sizes are feasible. With only six inverse DFTs, CLASS explores 16 assignments and reduces the 99th-percentile worst-layer PAPR by 1.41 dB relative to fixed assignment, outperforming representative selected mapping (SLM) and partial transmit sequences (PTS) schemes under the same synthesis budget.

</details>


### [13] [Harnessing Inter-Waveguide Evanescent Coupling in Pinching-Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00867)
*Dengke Wei,Runxin Zhang,Yulin Shao,Shaodan Ma*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文将多波导PASS中相邻介质波导间的倏逝波耦合由“串扰”重新诠释为额外的模拟波束成形自由度，通过耦合感知的波导部署、PA布放与数字预编码联合优化提升多热点通信总速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多波导PASS常把各波导视为独立信号传输路径，并将波导间倏逝场重叠引起的能量交换视为有害串扰而加以抑制（如强制最小间距）。作者希望挖掘这一被忽视的物理效应，将其转化为可利用的信道重构资源。

Method: 建立基于物理的端到端信道模型刻画耦合与分布式pinching天线顺序抽取的交互；推导耦合感知的波导间距设计准则与可达速率上界；针对多热点通信提出波导部署、PA位置与数字预编码的联合优化算法。

Abstract: In multi-waveguide pinching-antenna systems (PASS), waveguides are typically modeled as independent signal-transport paths. However, when dielectric waveguides are closely spaced, their evanescent electromagnetic fields overlap, allowing guided signals to exchange energy before radiation. While such inter-waveguide coupling is conventionally regarded as undesirable crosstalk, this paper reveals an opportunity to harness it as an additional analog beamforming resource. The key insight is that coupling, through its interaction with sequential signal extraction by distributed pinching antennas (PAs), can reshape coherent signal combining at the receivers without increasing the available signal power. Consequently, stronger coupling does not necessarily yield better performance; rather, the coupling-induced phase relationships determine whether the radiated signals combine constructively or destructively. To characterize and exploit this mechanism, we establish a physics-based end-to-end channel model, derive coupling-aware waveguide-spacing principles and achievable-rate bounds, and develop joint waveguide deployment, PA placement, and digital precoding algorithms for multi-hotspot communications. Numerical results demonstrate substantial sum-rate improvements over conventional fixed-deployment and optimized minimum-spacing PASS benchmarks. These findings establish inter-waveguide evanescent coupling as a new physical degree of freedom for PASS channel reconfiguration.

</details>

Abstract: As Wireless Communication evolves towards 5G and beyond, it satisfies Quality of Service (QoS) requirements of users by utilizing beam forming, adaptive transmission and multi antenna communication techniques. It is essential to have accurate channel information at Base station (BS) to provide satisfactory QoS to users. Traditional channel estimation approach involves transmitting pilots from User Equipments (UEs) and estimating the channel at BS, increases computational complexity as it is periodic process. It also increases communication complexity as number of antennas at BS increases which leads to increase in pilots. In this paper, we consider channel vector prediction using Machine learning approach at 7GHz. In this work, we generated data using ray tracing mechanism and used this generated data to train Machine learning models. We Considered XG Boost regression and Multilayer perceptron regression model.

### [15] [Denoising Multiuser Interference Enabled Iterative AI Receiver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00910)
*Xiangzhao Qin,Sha Hu,Jing Qian,Hao Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对有限字母符号下多用户干扰非高斯、协方差不足以刻画似然的问题，提出迭代式AI接收机，联合迭代精化干扰消除、信道估计与期望用户检测；仿真表明其逼近可达性能界并显著优于准ML基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限字母符号导致的聚合干扰加噪声非高斯，仅凭协方差无法给出贝叶斯检测所需似然；MMSE-IRC仅在二阶统计量已知的线性估计中最优，一般无法达到贝叶斯MMSE估计，故需非线性/学习方法利用干扰的结构化信息。

Method: 采用迭代结构，每轮包含：干扰去噪网络依据残差观测、协方差信息及期望用户软判决不确定性重建接收干扰并消除；利用前次软判决在期望用户流间做SIC，并在数据位置得到LS信道估计，再由信道估计网络结合前次信道估计精化；MMSE-IRC预处理输出有效信道矩阵与软符号估计，送入Transformer-ResNet-PIC检测器产生符号概率供下一轮使用；作者并把这些结构化输入与松弛贝叶斯检测问题的似然梯度相联系。

Result: 链路级仿真显示，对多用户干扰进行去噪使所提迭代AI接收机逼近可达理论性能界，并显著优于基于LMMSE信道估计与MMSE-IRC预处理的传统准ML接收机。

Conclusion: 以软判决跨迭代耦合干扰消除、信道估计与检测三阶段，可将非高斯多用户干扰的统计结构加以利用，突破MMSE-IRC的线性/二阶统计局限，获得接近理论界的检测性能。

Abstract: When interfering users transmit finite-alphabet symbols, the aggregate of multiuser interference and thermal noise in a multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) receiver is generally non-Gaussian. Its covariance alone does not specify the likelihood required for Bayesian detection. Conventional minimum mean-square error interference rejection combining (MMSE-IRC) is optimal among linear estimators with known second-order statistics, but it does not generally attain the Bayesian MMSE estimate in this setting. In this article, we propose an iterative artificial intelligence (AI) receiver that jointly refines interference cancellation, channel estimation, and desired-user detection. At each iteration, an interference denoising network reconstructs the received interference from the residual observation, covariance information, and uncertainty in the desired-user soft decisions. After cancellation, the preceding soft decisions support successive interference cancellation among desired-user streams and yield least squares (LS) channel estimates at data positions. A channel estimation network refines these estimates using the preceding channel estimate. Finally, MMSE-IRC preprocessing provides an effective channel matrix and soft symbol estimates to a Transformer-ResNet-PIC detector, which produces symbol probabilities for the next iteration. We also relate these structured detector inputs to the likelihood gradient of a relaxed Bayesian detection problem. The resulting receiver uses soft decisions to couple the three processing stages across iterations. Link-level simulations show that denoising multiuser interference enables the proposed iterative AI receiver to approach the attainable theoretical performance bound. The proposed receiver substantially outperforms the conventional quasi-ML receiver based on LMMSE channel estimation and MMSE-IRC preprocessing.

</details>


### [16] [Channel Estimation for Stacked Intelligent Metasurfaces with Mutual Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01114)
*Chandan Kumar Sheemar,Giovanni Iacovelli,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文指出堆叠智能超表面(SIM)信道估计中长期采用的级联模型忽略了互耦与层间反射，在互耦一致的多端口模型下重新研究导频估计问题。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 在SIM信道估计中必须采用耦合一致的多端口模型；所提LS/LMMSE估计器与梯度调度设计在误差与和速率上显著优于级联模型方法。

Abstract: Channel estimation for stacked intelligent metasurfaces (SIMs) has relied on the cascade model, which ignores mutual coupling and inter-layer reflection. Under a coupling-consistent multiport model, we show that the pilots are linear in the unknown channel segments and derive least-squares and linear minimum-mean-square-error estimators, an identifiability condition and an exact error decomposition for the reactance training schedule, and a gradient-based schedule design. Cascade-based estimation floors above $0$~dB normalized error at practical layer spacings and collapses the sum rate below that of the bare feed, whereas the proposed estimators retain about $80\%$ of the perfect-channel-knowledge sum rate.

</details>


### [17] [3D Gaussian Splatting: A New Paradigm for Wireless Environment Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01122)
*Jinya Zhang,Jiajia Guo,Chao-Kai Wen,Shi Jin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述了3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）作为无线环境表征新范式，提出统一框架，并探讨其在通信与感知任务中的适用性、部署考量及未来方向。

Conclusion: 3DGS为无线环境表征提供了有前景的新范式，可支撑广泛通信与感知任务，但需在跨场景泛化、近场建模、数据采集与生命周期管理等方向进一步研究以实现落地。

Abstract: Wireless channels are shaped by the surrounding propagation environment, making explicit environment representation an effective approach for characterizing channels. By combining explicit environment modeling with learnable radio frequency characteristics, 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) emerges as a new paradigm for wireless environment representation. This article establishes a systematic perspective on 3DGS-enabled wireless communications in terms of environment representation. We first clarify the rationale for extending 3DGS from visual scene modeling to wireless communications and formulate a unified framework for wireless environment representation. Building on this framework, we further investigate the applicability of 3DGS-based representations to a broad range of communication and sensing tasks, and characterize key considerations for practical deployment. Finally, emerging directions are highlighted, including cross-scene generalization, near-field channel modeling, real-world data collection, and lifecycle management.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星单基站JCAS须全双工工作，而SI功率比目标回波高80–100 dB，若直接采用覆盖整个时延范围的线性消除器，会把相干回波一并吸收，严重压缩雷达SINR；同时ADC量化在强SI下易饱和。因此需要一套利用SI短支撑与回波毫秒级返回之间时间分离的理论与设计框架。

Method: （1）将模拟与数字消除器的支撑限制在几个符号的短SI区间，证明其无最优性损失且实践必要；（2）推导支撑含目标时延时最小二乘消除器吸收回波的闭式SINR损失因子（零多普勒穿越船只约18 dB）；（3）将线性消除器无法重建的SI建模为空间白噪声并计入ADC量化，闭式给出雷达SINR上限、抽头预算下的最小预ADC SI功率、完全消除所需预算及最优设计由通信转向感知的感知权重阈值；（4）对K用户、Q目标，以各自半双工基准归一化的通信与感知SINR加权和为目标，对波束成形器、感知协方差、合并器与消除器采用闭式更新的交替算法求解。

Result: 仿真验证了各闭式表达式：联合设计相较仅在接收端消除SI的顺序设计，归一化目标最多提升13%；在4比特ADC下，下行与雷达SINR提升超过10 dB，四目标中第10百分位最差目标SINR亦提升超过10 dB；而忽略SI会导致ADC过载。

Conclusion: SI与回波的时延支撑分离可作为LEO单基站全双工JCAS的核心设计原则：限定短支撑消除器既无损最优性又能避免回波自吸收，配合闭式性能界与交替优化可实现通信—感知的显式权衡，并在低精度ADC条件下保持鲁棒性能。

Abstract: Monostatic joint communication and sensing (JCAS) from a low-Earth-orbit (LEO) satellite requires full-duplex (FD) operation, with self-interference (SI) $80$--$100$~dB stronger than the ground echo. The echo returns milliseconds after transmission, whereas the onboard coupling spans a few symbols. We develop a theory and a design that exploit this separation. Restricting the analog and digital cancellers to the short SI support is without loss of optimality and essential in practice: a least-squares canceller whose support contains the target delay absorbs the coherent echo and reduces the radar SINR by a closed-form factor, about $18$~dB for a vessel at its zero-Doppler crossing. Modeling the SI that no linear canceller can reconstruct as spatially white and accounting for ADC quantization, we derive in closed form a ceiling on the radar SINR, the minimum pre-ADC SI power under a canceller tap budget, the budget for full cancellation, and the sensing weight at which the optimal design changes over from communication to sensing. We then maximize a weighted sum of the communication and sensing SINRs, each normalized by its half-duplex benchmark, over the beamformers, sensing covariance, combiners, and cancellers for $K$ users and $Q$ targets, using an alternating algorithm with closed-form updates. Simulations confirm the closed forms. The joint design gains up to $13\%$ in the normalized objective over a sequential design that cancels the SI only at the receiver, and more than $10$~dB of downlink and radar SINR with $4$-bit ADCs and in the 10th-percentile worst-target SINR of four targets, whereas ignoring the SI overloads the ADCs.

</details>

*Aman Khalid,Muhammad Sohaib Aslam,Ahmed Naeem,Seyit Karatepe,Muhammet Kirik,Huseyin Arslan*

TL;DR: 面向星地共享sub-6GHz频谱的跨层干扰，提出双时间尺度框架：SINR感知自适应流量卸载（SAATO）用滤波SINR与负载感知期望吞吐更新用户关联并设迟滞带，联合跨层分式规划（Joint-FP）优化资源分配，显著改善吞吐与中断并抑制乒乓切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面网络与低轨卫星共享sub-6GHz频谱时产生双向跨层干扰：地面传输恶化卫星链路接收，卫星传输损害地面链路性能；而面向独立频段设计的传统基于RSRP的卸载不建模该跨层干扰，导致全频谱重叠时大量用户处于低于0.5 Mbps的中断状态。

Method: 构建地面—非地面一体化网络的双时间尺度框架：快/慢时间尺度上，SAATO基于滤波后的SINR与负载感知期望吞吐更新用户关联，仅在迟滞带内才触发切换；随后Joint-FP对给定关联做联合跨层资源分配。在等分资源模型下，所得决策度量近似比例公平效用的增量，误差随负载衰减；并证明和速率效用下的联合分配为NP-hard，Joint-FP接受的效用序列非降且收敛。

Result: 全频谱重叠时，基于RSRP的卸载使44%用户样本低于0.5 Mbps（中断），而具备瞬时信道知识的SAATO+Joint-FP无中断；在半重叠与全重叠下相较仅功率控制（不卸载）分别提升几何平均吞吐8%与2%；且将层间切换限制在每用户每分钟不超过0.05次。

Conclusion: 将跨层干扰显式纳入关联与资源分配的联合设计，可在星地频谱共享下兼顾吞吐提升、中断消除与切换稳定性；双时间尺度分解与迟滞机制是控制复杂度与切换抖动的有效手段。

Abstract: Sharing the sub-6~GHz spectrum between terrestrial networks and low Earth orbit satellites introduces cross-tier interference in both directions, as terrestrial transmissions can degrade satellite-link reception, while satellite transmissions can impair the performance of terrestrial links. Conventional reference signal received power (RSRP)-based offloading, designed for separate frequency bands, does not account for this cross-tier interference. We study offloading and resource allocation in an integrated terrestrial and non-terrestrial network through a two-timescale framework in which signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR)-aware adaptive traffic offloading (SAATO) updates user associations from filtered SINR and load-aware expected throughput, switching a user only across a hysteresis band, while joint cross-tier fractional programming (Joint-FP) optimizes resource allocation for the resulting associations. Under an equal-share resource allocation model, the resulting decision metric approximates the change in proportional-fair utility with an error that decays with load. We prove that joint allocation under the sum-rate utility is nondeterministic polynomial-time (NP)-hard and that the utility sequence accepted by Joint-FP is non-decreasing and convergent. At full spectral overlap, RSRP-based offloading leaves 44\% of user samples below 0.5~Mbps (outage), whereas SAATO with Joint-FP, given instantaneous channel knowledge, leaves none and increases geometric-mean throughput by 8\% and 2\% under half and full spectral overlap, respectively, relative to power control without offloading. It also limits tier switching to at most 0.05 switches per user per minute.

</details>

Abstract: A method of joint device authentication and gesture classification using low-cost passive transponders and 802.11 signals of opportunity from commodity hardware is presented for tracking objects in cluttered environments. The approach detects nonlinear responses of diode-based radio-frequency (RF) transponders by estimating the mean time-varying phase across all subcarrier channel state information (CSI) obtained from standard preambles. These unique responses are enabled by passive transponders utilizing 2.4 GHz meander-line monopole antennas with capacitive top-hat loading and a low-barrier Schottky diode, allowing for low-power omnidirectional sensing and complex gesture recognition across multiple unique devices. The capture system uses a wireless access point to illuminate the transponder with a standard preamble. This signal is modulated by the diode's nonlinear response and transmitted to a single ESP32 receiver to calculate the channel response. Using the modulated CSI, device identification via RF fingerprinting achieves >95% accuracy in both static and dynamic conditions using simple machine learning models. For dynamically moving transponders, a time-varying velocity profile is calculated using a novel phase sanitation approach that significantly reduces the impacts of the non-cooperative network. This enables high-accuracy estimation of micro-motions on a single receiver, achieving a theoretical limit of 9.75 m/s using an amplitude-invariant binary comparator form of the sanitized waveform. The concept is experimentally validated using lightweight CNN-LSTM deep-learning models applied to a diverse dataset of five transponders and three motion profiles. This experimental validation demonstrated a classification accuracy of 98% in both gesture classification and device identification tasks.

</details>

Result: 仿真表明联合轨迹与调度设计带来显著性能增益；所提系统在有限天线移动速度下可逼近瞬时定位的性能；SCA目标值收敛，博弈调度算法具备有限终止性与稳定性。

Abstract: Finite antenna movement speed introduces repositioning delays that can limit the performance gains of pinching-antenna systems. In this paper, a trajectory based scheme is proposed for a time division multiple access (TDMA) based pinching-antenna system, allowing transmission during both movement and stationary operation. A discrete-time model is developed to characterize periodic antenna trajectories and antenna-user service association under practical movement constraints. For a single-waveguide scenario, the expected average achievable rate is derived. The rate loss relative to instantaneous positioning and the rate improvement over a fixed antenna are further analyzed, with an upper bound on the former and a lower bound on the latter established. To improve fairness among users, a minimum average rate maximization problem is formulated through joint trajectory design and user scheduling. To address the coupling between these variables, an alternating optimization (AO) framework is adopted. Specifically, a successive convex approximation (SCA) based algorithm is developed using concave rate lower bounds, while a game based scheduling algorithm employs an ordered user-rate vector as the common utility. The convergence of the SCA objective value and the finite termination and stability of the scheduling algorithm are established. Simulation results demonstrate the gains achieved by the joint design and show that the proposed system can approach instantaneous-positioning performance under finite antenna movement speed.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a pinching-antenna system (PASS)-assisted integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) framework for joint downlink communication and target positioning. The dielectric waveguides enable flexible signal radiation through activated pinching antennas (PAs), while the reflected echo signals are collected by a sensing receiver. A unified waveform is adopted to support both data transmission and sensing-echo processing. In particular, a two-stage procedure is developed for target localization using time-sum-of-arrival (TSoA) measurements, where the signal propagation distances are first estimated through de-chirp processing and beat frequency extraction. Then, the target position is obtained based on TSoA measurements using the weighted linear least squares(WLLS) technique. To characterize the fundamental limits of the PASS-assisted ISAC system, a Cramér-Rao lower bound (CRB) is derived, which captures the impact of PA activation. Furthermore, the PA activation problem is formulated to jointly optimize the communication spectral efficiency (SE) and sensing performance. To address this, a low-complexity heuristic greedy selection algorithm is proposed to determine the activated PA pattern. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed scheme outperforms random PA selection, achieving gains of up to 81% improvement in communication SE.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变环境下杂波特性变化使回波分布偏离训练分布，导致检测器可靠性下降；而部署阶段缺乏真值标签，无法直接评估性能，亟需准确的免标签性能估计手段。同时发现基于置信度的估计器在分布偏移下会高估性能，因其未考虑伪标签分布与真实标签分布的差异。

Method: 提出概率最优传输（POT）：计算检测器在无标签部署回波上的输出概率分布与训练数据标签分布之间的Wasserstein距离以度量部署性能；进一步提出阈值化概率最优传输（TPOT），通过阈值机制抑制异常传输代价对Wasserstein距离估计的影响。

Result: 在公开实测雷达数据集上，于不同数据分布偏移程度的部署场景中，POT与TPOT均稳定优于代表性免标签基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于最优传输的分布对齐度量可有效实现雷达目标检测器的免标签性能估计，阈值化处理进一步提升鲁棒性，为部署期性能监控与退化识别提供可行方案。

Abstract: Deep-learning-based radar target detectors can achieve strong performance in complex sensing environments. However, in time-varying environments, changes in clutter characteristics may cause the distribution of echoes to deviate from that of the training data, thereby degrading detector reliability. Timely identification of such degradation requires an accurate assessment of the deployed detector's performance; however, ground-truth labels for radar echoes are typically unavailable during deployment. This paper investigates label-free performance estimation for deployed deep learning-based radar target detectors. We first show that confidence-based estimators tend to overestimate detection performance under distribution shift because they fail to account for the discrepancy between the detector-induced pseudo-label distribution and the true label distribution. To address this limitation, we propose probabilistic optimal transport (POT), which assesses deployment performance by computing the Wasserstein distance between the detector's output probability distribution on unlabeled deployment echoes and the label distribution in the training data. We further develop thresholded probabilistic optimal transport (TPOT), which suppresses the influence of anomalous transport costs on the Wasserstein-distance-based estimate, thereby improving estimation robustness. Experiments on a publicly measured radar dataset demonstrate that POT and TPOT consistently outperform representative label-free baselines across deployment scenarios with varying degrees of data distribution shift.

</details>


### [25] [Joint Subcarrier and Power Allocation for Multi-User OFDM ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01470)
*Jiayue Huang,Ning Wei,Min Qiu,Aimin Tang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究下行多用户OFDM ISAC系统的联合子载波分配与功率分配，以最小化时延CRLB为目标，提出锥松弛引导的固定-优化（CRG-FO）算法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OFDM ISAC系统中通信与感知的资源共享是核心挑战，多用户场景下可利用信道分集提升资源利用率，但联合子载波与功率分配问题具有混合整数特性，难以高效求解。

Method: 以最小化时延CRLB为目标，约束包括RoI内归一化ISL、每用户速率及总发射功率。先对固定子载波分配导出感知功率分配的凸形式，再构建等价的混合整数锥规划；提出CRG-FO算法，融合连续松弛、固定子载波下的功率重优化与受限邻域搜索。

Result: 仿真表明，在各ISL阈值、速率负载与用户配置下，联合分配相比均匀子载波与功率分配均降低距离CRLB；其中均匀感知支撑上的功率优化贡献了大部分增益，自适应子载波分配仅带来有限额外提升。

Conclusion: 联合子载波与功率分配可有效改善多用户OFDM ISAC的感知精度，且功率优化是主要增益来源，子载波自适应分配收益边际，为实际资源分配设计提供参考。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) is envisioned as a key enabling technology for 6G networks. Efficient resource sharing between communication and sensing is a fundamental challenge in orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) ISAC systems, particularly in multi-user scenarios, where channel diversity can be exploited to improve resource utilization. This paper investigates joint subcarrier assignment and power allocation for a downlink multi-user OFDM ISAC system, with the objective of minimizing the delay Cramér-Rao lower bound (CRLB) subject to constraints on the normalized integrated sidelobe level (ISL) over the region of interest (RoI), per-user data rates, and total transmit power. We first derive a convex formulation for sensing power allocation given fixed subcarrier assignments, and then develop an equivalent mixed-integer conic formulation of the joint optimization problem. To obtain computationally tractable solutions, we propose a conic relaxation-guided fix-and-optimize (CRG-FO) algorithm that integrates continuous relaxation, power re-optimization under fixed subcarrier assignments, and restricted neighborhood search. Simulation results demonstrate that joint subcarrier and power allocation reduces the distance CRLB relative to uniform subcarrier and power allocation across the tested ISL thresholds, rate loads, and user configurations. Power optimization on uniform sensing supports captures most of this gain, while adaptive subcarrier assignment provides only modest additional improvement.

</details>


### [26] [Low-Latency Joint Source-Channel Coding with Multiple Constant Curvature Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01476)
*Anders M. Buvarp,Stefan Werner*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出常曲率曲线多码（C3M），将C3T扩展为多条分支曲线并联的模拟联合信源信道编码，在固定各向同性AWGN下改善有限维短块性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C3T将标量信源映射到功率球面上单一曲线，几何自由度有限，短块长下单曲线映射的SDR受限；需在保持低块长模拟编码结构的前提下提升有限维端到端性能。

Method: 以最优C3T曲线为起始分支，基于管密度、曲线长度与分支分类可靠性设计几何与分类感知的分支分配规则，顺序选取附加分支以规避穷举搜索；推导混合输出分布、后验、MAP/MMSE估计器及互信息分解（分支识别项+沿曲线项）；采用基于接收环境向量傅里叶特征提升的NORM式分支条件谱解码器。

Result: 四维与六维数值实验表明，增加候选分支曲线可提升有限维端到端信号失真比；在+10 dB SNR下，二、三分支C3M系统优于单曲线基线。

Conclusion: 多曲线模拟映射为低块长联合信源信道编码提供了可行的有限维设计路径，且顺序分支选择降低了多曲线组合搜索复杂度。

Abstract: We propose constant-curvature curve multicode (C3M), a multi-branch extension of constant-curvature curve tube (C3T) analog joint source-channel coding for one-shot transmission over fixed isotropic AWGN channels. Instead of mapping a scalar source to a single curve on the power sphere, C3M partitions the source interval across a union of constant-speed branch curves in even-dimensional Euclidean space. This construction increases the available geometric degrees of freedom while preserving a low block length analog encoding structure. We derive the induced mixture output distribution, posterior representation, MAP and MMSE estimators, and mutual-information decomposition into branch-identification and along-curve components. We then introduce geometry- and classification-aware branch allocation rules based on tube density, curve length, and branch-classification reliability. The resulting design procedure fixes the optimal C3T curve as a starting branch and sequentially selects additional branches, thereby reducing the combinatorial cost of exhaustive multi-curve search. We use NORM-inspired branch-conditioned spectral decoders based on Fourier-feature lifting of the received ambient vector. Numerical experiments in four and six dimensions show that adding candidate branch curves improves the finite-dimensional end-to-end signal-to-distortion ratio, with selected two- and three-candidate C3M systems outperforming the one-curve baseline at +10 dB SNR. The results show that multi-curve analog mappings provide a practical finite-dimensional design route for low block length joint source-channel coding.

</details>


### [27] [Spectral-Convolutional Factorized Transformer for Joint Multipath Parameter Estimation: Applications to mmWave Positioning and Radio SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01491)
*Yaya Etiabi,Wessam Ajib,Halima Elbiaze*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出谱-卷积因子化Transformer（SCFT），从含噪复信道估计与波束对描述中联合估计多径分量的时延、AoA、AoD，LoS/NLoS判别与几何参数估计一体化，显著提升下游定位与无线SLAM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G向AI原生、环境感知演进，定位与建图成为关键服务。现有学习型信道估计多面向通信目标（如重建信道），未显式估计时延、AoA、AoD等多径几何参数，而这些参数是定位与无线SLAM等下游任务所需的核心几何信息。

Method: SCFT由三部分构成：（1）局部谱卷积，用于提取信道频域/空域局部结构特征；（2）置换等变（permutation-equivariant）波束集注意力，处理无序波束对描述；（3）基于query的Transformer解码器，以集合预测方式检测并分类LoS/NLoS路径，同时回归各路径的观测时延、AoA与AoD。

Result: 在真实射线追踪数据上，SCFT的GOSPA误差较最强基线ResNet降低13.4%；在下游任务中，单快照定位位置RMSE降低18.5%，无线SLAM位置RMSE降低65.0%。

Conclusion: 显式恢复多径几何至关重要：MPC估计精度的提升可有效转化为下游定位与无线SLAM任务的大幅增益，为AI原生的通感一体化系统提供可行路径。

Abstract: Future 6G wireless networks are expected to evolve toward AI-native systems that support intelligent, environment-aware services, including positioning and mapping. Current research has already integrated AI into several aspects of communication, particularly wireless channel estimation. However, most learning-based channel estimators focus on communication-oriented objectives rather than explicitly estimating multipath parameters such as delay, angle of arrival (AoA), and angle of departure (AoD). These parameters provide essential geometric information for downstream tasks including positioning and radio simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). In this work, we propose a Spectral-Convolutional Factorized Transformer (SCFT) for joint multipath component (MPC) estimation from noisy complex channel estimates and physical beam-pair descriptors. SCFT combines local spectral convolution, permutation-equivariant beam-set attention, and a query-based Transformer decoder to detect and classify line-of-sight (LoS) and non-line-of-sight (NLoS) paths and estimate their observed delays, AoAs, and AoDs. Experiments on realistic ray-tracing data show that SCFT reduces the generalized optimal sub-pattern assignment (GOSPA) error by 13.4% compared with ResNet, the strongest competing baseline. We further demonstrate this advantage in independent snapshot positioning and radio SLAM, where SCFT reduces position RMSE by 18.5% and 65.0%, respectively, relative to ResNet. These findings highlight the importance of explicitly recovering multipath geometry, as improvements in MPC estimation can translate into substantial gains in downstream tasks.

</details>


### [28] [AI-Aided and AI-Oriented Integrated Sensing, Communication, and Computation: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01521)
*Yaya Etiabi,Wessam Ajib,El Mehdi Amhoud,Halima Elbiaze*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该综述从"AI辅助ISCC"与"面向AI的ISCC"双重视角系统梳理集成感知、通信与计算（ISCC）的研究进展，并探讨AI与ISCC的协同设计、架构、指标与评估实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G追求"万物智联"，需将智能嵌入通信基础设施；而感知、通信、计算三类功能竞争共享资源且目标冲突，须从独立优化转向统一联合设计。同时AI既是ISCC优化的使能工具，其自身负载需求又反过来影响ISCC系统设计，亟需系统性梳理这一双向关系。

Method: 综述性研究：提出双重视角分类框架——(i) AI-aided ISCC（学习类方法支撑感知-通信-计算联合设计与优化）；(ii) AI-oriented ISCC（三功能联合配置以支撑学习、推理与分布式智能）；并进一步分析AI与ISCC的co-design，即AI需求与ISCC约束共同决定系统与模型决策；随后对设计方法、系统架构、性能指标与评估实践进行比较性综合。

Result: 形成对ISCC设计方法、系统架构、性能度量与评估实践的对比性综合，并将这些进展与不同应用域的具体需求相关联，指出面向可扩展、自适应、可信AI-ISCC系统的开放研究问题。

Conclusion: ISCC应作为统一系统进行联合设计而非独立优化；AI与ISCC存在双向耦合，需在系统与模型层面协同设计；未来研究方向为构建可扩展、自适应且可信的AI-ISCC体系。

Abstract: The evolution from 5G to 6G networks marks a transition toward the "intelligence of everything." While 5G enables enhanced connectivity, 6G aspires to embed intelligence directly into communication infrastructures, creating systems that are adaptive, context aware, and capable of autonomous decision making. Realizing such systems depends on harmonizing three fundamental functions that compete for shared network resources and often pursue conflicting goals: (i) sensing for environmental and contextual information acquisition, (ii) communication for reliable and timely information exchange, and (iii) computation for information processing and decision making. This harmonization necessitates shifting from independent optimization to their joint design as a unified system, a paradigm known as Integrated Sensing, Communication, and Computation (ISCC). In parallel, AI has emerged both as an enabling tool for ISCC optimization and as a workload whose requirements influence ISCC system design. This survey comprehensively examines ISCC from this dual perspective by distinguishing between AI-aided ISCC, in which learning-based methods support the joint design and optimization of sensing, communication, and computation, and AI-oriented ISCC, in which these functions are jointly configured to support learning, inference, and distributed intelligence. The survey further examines the co-design of AI and ISCC, where AI requirements and ISCC constraints jointly shape system and model decisions. It further provides a comparative synthesis of the resulting design approaches, system architectures, performance metrics, and evaluation practices, relates these developments to application-specific requirements across different domains, and identifies open research problems toward scalable, adaptive, and trustworthy AI-ISCC systems.

</details>


### [29] [Assessing Temporal Dispersion of Time-Varying Channel at 38 GHz for Different Reference Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01685)
*Jarosław Wojtuń,Bartosz Czaja,Tomasz Grasza,Rafał Przesmycki,Jan M. Kelner,Cezary Ziółkowski,Radek Zavorka,Josef Vychodil,Tomas Mikulasek,Jiri Blumenstein,Ales Prokes,Aniruddha Chandra*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种参考波形（MATLAB数值生成、传导方式测量、暗室测量）对38 GHz时变无线信道时域色散估计的影响，发现PDP多径结构相似、平均时延与RMS时延扩展差异较小，说明在所研究条件下估计结果对参考波形选择不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考波形直接影响信道冲激响应/PDP的反卷积估计，但其选择对时域色散参数估计结果的影响缺乏定量评估，本文旨在量化该敏感性。

Method: 在38 GHz动态道路环境中开展同一组宽带信道测量，分别采用三种参考波形（MATLAB数值生成、传导连接测量、暗室测量记录）进行处理，计算并对比PDP、平均时延与RMS时延扩展。

Result: 三种参考波形在时域与频谱特性上存在明显差异，但所得PDP的多径结构相似，平均时延与RMS时延扩展的估计值差异相对较小。

Conclusion: 在所研究的测量条件下，时域色散参数估计对参考波形选择敏感性有限，实测或数值生成的参考波形均可获得一致结论。

Abstract: This paper investigates the influence of reference waveform selection on temporal dispersion estimation in a time-varying radio channel at 38 GHz. Three reference waveforms-numerically generated in MATLAB, measured through a conducted connection, and recorded in an anechoic chamber -are compared using the same wideband channel measurements conducted in a dynamic road environment. Their effects on the estimated power delay profiles (PDPs), mean delay, and root mean square (RMS) delay spread are evaluated. Despite noticeable differences in the time-domain and spectral characteristics of the reference waveforms, the resulting PDPs exhibit similar multipath structures, while the estimated temporal dispersion parameters show relatively small differences. The results indicate limited sensitivity to reference waveform selection under the investigated measurement conditions.

</details>


### [30] [Domain-Aware Hierarchical Federated Learning for UAV Swarms Across Heterogeneous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01812)
*Taha Mahmood,Mudassar Shakeel,Ahmed Naeem,El Mehdi Amhoud,Rizwan Ahmad,Salman Ghafoor*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出领域感知分层联邦学习DA-HFL，面向无人机集群在异构动态环境下的高效训练，融合移动性聚类、Fisher加权合并、领域对抗共享主干与环境专属头，并给出理论分析与仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层联邦学习虽能在无人机集群中实现通信高效的训练，但在环境动态变化与异构条件下性能下降，缺乏对领域差异与移动性的建模。

Method: DA-HFL框架：基于移动性信息的聚类、簇级聚合、云端Fisher加权合并、领域对抗共享主干网络、环境特定头部、门控推理与本地自适应；并建立任务加权优化模型，纳入能耗、时延与簇头资格约束。

Result: 理论证明资源可行性、条件性主干平稳性界、Fisher加权合并的二次最优性及通信/计算复杂度界；在四类异构环境仿真中收敛更快、环境切换期精度更高、簇更稳定纯净，能耗仅略高于资源感知HFL；消融实验验证各组件贡献。

Conclusion: DA-HFL通过领域感知与移动性聚类有效缓解异构动态环境下的性能退化，在精度、收敛与聚类质量上优于基线，代价为适度能耗开销。

Abstract: Hierarchical federated learning (HFL) enables communication-efficient training in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms, but performance degrades under changing and heterogeneous environments. This paper proposes domain-aware HFL (DA-HFL), integrating mobility-informed clustering, cluster-level aggregation, cloud-based Fisher-weighted merging, a domain-adversarial shared backbone, environment-specific heads, and gated inference with local adaptation. A mission-weighted optimization formulation incorporates energy, latency, and cluster-head eligibility constraints. Theoretical analysis establishes resource feasibility, a conditional backbone-stationarity bound, the quadratic optimality of Fisher-weighted merging, and communication and computation complexity bounds. Simulations across four heterogeneous environments demonstrate faster convergence, improved accuracy during environmental transitions, and more stable, purer clusters than the evaluated baselines, with modest energy overhead over resource-aware HFL. Ablation studies validate the contributions of the proposed components.

</details>


### [31] [FADE: A Flow-Based Approach for Multichannel Tail Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01849)
*Parmida Valiahdi,Niloofar Mehrnia,Mustafa Furkan Beker,Ubeydullah Erdemir,Ibrahim Hokelek,Sinem Coleri*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于流的角密度估计方法FADE，在MEVT框架下无参数化表征多接收机极端事件的联合尾部依赖，在UAV实测数据上将联合CDF拟合MAE最多降低22.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: URLLC对罕见低接收功率事件敏感，多接收机分集增益在同时深衰落时显著削弱；传统多元极值理论（MEVT）方法依赖受限的参数假设或离散依赖表示，难以刻画复杂的多元尾部行为。

Method: FADE将多元极值变换为标准极值表示（角密度形式），再用基于流的密度估计器从变换后数据中学习依赖结构，不预设参数形式。

Result: 在UAV为发射机、两个地面接收机的实测数据上评估：与SOTA的Logistic和最大经验似然（MEL）方法相比，FADE与经验联合CDF更吻合，MAE最多降低22.4%，同时改善分布一致性并降低绝对偏差。

Conclusion: 流式生成模型可作为MEVT中灵活、非参数的尾部依赖建模工具，提升URLLC场景下多接收机联合统计特性的刻画精度。

Abstract: Ultra-Reliable Low-Latency Communication (URLLC) is vulnerable to rare low-received-power events, and the spatial-diversity benefits of multiple receivers can be significantly reduced when they experience simultaneous deep fading. Multivariate Extreme Value Theory (MEVT) provides a principled framework for characterizing such joint tail behavior. However, conventional MEVT methods often rely on restrictive parametric assumptions or discrete representations of the dependence structure, which may not fully capture complex multivariate tail behavior. This paper proposes Flow-based Angular Density Estimation (FADE), a flow-based approach for characterizing the dependence between extreme events across receivers within the MEVT framework. FADE transforms multivariate extremes into a standard extreme-value representation and uses a flow-based density estimator to learn the dependence structure from the transformed data without imposing a predefined parametric form. The proposed framework is evaluated using experimentally collected data from a UAV communication system, in which the UAV serves as the transmitter and communicates with two ground receivers. FADE provides closer agreement with the empirical joint CDF, achieving reductions of up to 22.4% in MAE compared with the state-of-the-art Logistic and Maximum Empirical Likelihood (MEL) methods, while also improving distributional agreement and reducing absolute bias.

</details>


### [32] [Ray-Tracing-Parameter-Based Lightweight Hybrid Precoding via Cascade XGBoost for Wideband Massive MIMO-OFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01880)
*Po-Heng Chou,Wei-Chen Lin,Wan-Jen Huang,Walid Saad,Ronald Y. Chang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出XGB-SVD轻量级混合预编码框架，用级联XGBoost将射线追踪路径参数直接映射为模拟波束码字索引，数字预编码/合并器由降维等效信道经SVD得到；在五个DeepMIMO场景中优于DNN基线并接近SOMP，且在线与训练开销更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合预编码可降低宽带大规模MIMO-OFDM系统的射频硬件负担，但传统优化方法与基于DNN的方法在线复杂度高，需要一种低开销的替代方案。

Method: XGB-SVD框架：以级联XGBoost捕获波束选择的顺序依赖性与收发传播相关性，将紧凑的射线追踪路径参数直接映射为模拟波束成形码字索引；数字预编码器与合并器则由降维后的等效信道通过SVD求得。

Abstract: We consider the peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR) reduction challenge of orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) systems utilizing tone reservation (TR) under an over-the-air computing constraint, such that the signals placed in the reserved tones simultaneously enable distributed computation at the receiver. A key contribution is the decomposition of the reserved-tone vector into an orthogonal pair of components: one satisfying the sum-computation constraint and the other providing the remaining degrees of freedom for PAPR reduction. This decomposition reveals that the proposed method and the benchmark sacrifices the same number of degrees of freedom. However, the proposed design distributes the computational payload across all reserved tones, yielding superior robustness against multi-user interference and quantization noise. Analytical and simulation results confirm that the proposed method matches the benchmark in PAPR reduction while outperforming it in computation accuracy, particularly in multi-user scenarios.

</details>

Abstract: Multipath channel models for Low-Earth-orbit (LEO) non-terrestrial network (NTN) must contend with time variability due to rapid satellite orbital motion that in turn impacts local (near terrestrial) scattering near the ground terminal (GT) receiver. While 3GPP-defined NTN channel model pipelines TR 38.811, 38.901 incorporate elevation-dependence into statistical channel generators, these are inherited largely from 2-D terrestrial propagation and are largely insufficient. Further, they do not prescribe any integrated workflow that is broadly available for generating joint delay, Doppler/phase and component multipath power profiles as a function of satellite location over a pass. This work addresses this gap by developing a geometry-based stochastic channel model driven by prescribed slant range, elevation, and azimuth over a LEO pass. A ground scene generates wall-reflected, ground-reflected, and roof-edge-diffracted components, from which joint delay-Doppler statistics and temporal channel impulse response (CIR) evolution are obtained. The resulting locally wide-sense stationary tapped-delay-line CIR enables analysis of key statistics such as the $K$-factor of the taps, multipath delay and Doppler spreads as a function of elevation and scene parameters. Depending on the scenarios (urban and rural), our model-based results show that with omnidirectional antennas, the LoS probability, delay-spread and $K$-factor trends broadly match 3GPP NTN models in TR 38.811 as a function of elevation. Beamforming instead produces a mid-elevation $K$-factor peak, which is not predicted by TR 38.811 and 6G NTN. Doppler spreads are smaller than those from our previous model and QuaDriGa because the proposed model accounts for path existence. Stationarity durations for CIR are reported and generally decrease with elevation, but with local increases at mid-elevation due to path geometry effects.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Most Doppler-based LEO positioning studies rely on tracked satellite signals or raw in-phase/quadrature (IQ) samples that preserve signal-level structures for acquisition, Doppler tracking, or satellite association. In contrast, passive software-defined radio (SDR) monitoring systems often retain only time-frequency power scans, in which LEO Doppler traces appear as unlabeled ridges without raw IQ samples or identifiable signal structures. The proposed method was evaluated using a three-hour subset of open Starlink/OneWeb monitoring data collected in the United Kingdom. The clock-offset search selected an effective offset of +19 s, for which 56 of 69 segments had at least one slope-compatible candidate, compared with 19 segments at zero shift. Using the resulting satellite associations, the batch positioning error over the full three-hour period was 104.2 m, whereas the RMSE-based and slope-based simple-segment baselines failed to converge near the reference receiver location.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Wi-Fi sensing applications require unambiguous angle-of-arrival (AoA) estimation over the full azimuth plane. Such undersampling produces strong grating lobes. To overcome this hardware bottleneck, we first establish a generalized array manifold incorporating element radiation patterns, revealing the suitability and degradation mechanisms of directional UCAs. Then we propose an angularly staggered beam-switched UCA architecture that leverages the spatial complementarity. Numerical evaluations demonstrate that the proposed scheme effectively mitigates grating-lobe ambiguities without increasing the number of RF chains, significantly reducing AoA estimation outliers and improving omnidirectional tracking performance.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中反向传播要求返回端口"看到"前向端口的信道，终端移动会破坏互易性，导致梯度符号反转。

Method: 射线追踪芝加哥街道场景仿真；流体天线按最近端口返回；由平面角谱二阶矩推导平均相关性符号的证书，化为三变量、无穷约束的紧线性规划；静态高斯模型下证明直线观测无法区分角谱与其镜像；知识地图通过虚拟源迁移勘测路径并拟合因果信道历史；与几何规则及多层感知机对比。

Result: 行人位移约半波长、街面基站：0.7波长间距下20%梯度反转，0.9波长下49%；四最近端口中至少一个对齐者除2%与9%外均成立。知识地图将0.9波长反转率从49%降至27%，对最近端口的增益在所有测试误差下保持显著，对几何规则的增益在每步5°相位漂移与2 mm位置误差下显著、下一档不显著。无勘测时五城市十基站：最近端口37%反转，基于基站方向与仰角的规则20.5%。MLP未以预设裕度超越物理规则。

Abstract: Over-the-air backpropagation returns a loss gradient through the reciprocal channel, which requires the return port to see the channel of the forward port. If the terminal moves in between, a fluid antenna system can return through the port nearest to the forward position. In ray-traced Chicago streets, with a pedestrian displacement of about half a wavelength and a street-level base station, this rule reverses 20 percent of the gradients at a port spacing of 0.7 wavelengths and 49 percent at 0.9 wavelengths, while one of the four nearest ports stays aligned in all but 2 and 9 percent. We derive a certificate for the sign of the mean correlation from the second moment of a planar angular spectrum. It reduces to a linear program with three variables and infinitely many constraints, is tight, and holds over a region of second moments. In a static Gaussian model, observations collected along a line cannot tell an angular spectrum from its mirror image, so the sign of the cross moment must come from other information. At unsurveyed positions of the same city, a knowledge map that transports surveyed paths through virtual sources and fits them to the causal channel history lowers the reversal rate at 0.9 wavelengths from 49 to 27 percent. Its gain over the nearest port survived every tested error, while its gain over a geometry rule stayed significant at the tested single-error levels of 5 degrees of phase drift per step and 2 mm of position error but not at the next tested levels. Without any survey, across five cities and ten base stations, the nearest port reverses 37 percent of the returns at 0.9 wavelengths and a rule based on the direction and elevation of the base station 20.5 percent. The multilayer perceptrons we tested did not beat these physical rules by the margin fixed in advance in the primary settings.

</details>


### [40] [Which Chains to Calibrate for Physical Backpropagation: Per-User Factors Versus Base-Station Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02390)
*Xianzhong Li,Yuzhi Yang,Tianhao Guo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 在物理反向传播中，互易校准的优先级可能反转：相同量级的用户侧幅相误差往往比基站侧链路误差更损害回传梯度（尤其经由“选错用户”惩罚），基站链路误差则表现为每天线缩放、无法靠增大阵列消除。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理反向传播依赖时分双工上行回传损失导数，使基站无需信道重建即可获得下行梯度；传统互易校准以基站链路为优先（因列归一化迫零把用户因子化为流旋转）。本文质疑该优先级，考察在何种条件下用户侧误差反超基站侧误差。

Method: 理想基站链路、无噪声下分析每用户幅度误差如何把回传场变为加权和速率梯度；以损失分解区分“选择惩罚”（服务了错误的用户）与幅度/相位误差贡献；比较用户侧与基站链路误差（3 dB、25°；用户 1 dB、10°）、不同天线规模与 SNR（30 dB、10 dB）、匹配/失配导频、聚集信道、80 用户及 256 天线，并提出截断单回波估计器。

Result: 两种最优解均实现迫零全服务时损失与阵列规模无关；否则选择惩罚在六种配置中的五种占幅度所致损失的 86%–94%；用户相位误差主要拖慢迭代。基站链路误差按天线缩放回传场，3 dB/25° 使单用户损失 1.3 dB SNR 且不随阵列增大消除。80 用户、双侧误差与失配导频下，256 天线聚集信道上 oracle 用户级与基站级校准分别残留 6.2% 与 12.3% 损失。该排序在 30 dB SNR 成立，在 1 dB/10° 用户误差下反转。

Conclusion: 校准优先级并非固定：误差量级与类型决定主导项，用户侧幅度误差（选择惩罚主导）与基站链路的不可消除 SNR 损失各有特征。截断单回波估计器距 oracle 用户级校准仅 2.4 个百分点，但在 10 dB SNR 时劣于不校准，需按 SNR 与误差结构自适应选择校准策略。

Abstract: In physical backpropagation, users return loss derivatives over the time-division duplex uplink, giving the base station the downlink gradient without channel reconstruction. Reciprocity calibration conventionally prioritizes base-station chains, as column-normalized zero-forcing reduces a per-user factor to a stream rotation. This priority can reverse. With ideal base-station chains and no noise, per-user amplitude errors make the returned field a weighted sum-rate gradient. With zero-forcing full service at both optima, the loss is independent of array size. Otherwise the selection penalty, serving the wrong users, carries 86 to 94 percent of the amplitude-induced loss in five of six configurations. Per-user phase errors mainly slow the iteration. Base-station chain errors scale the returned field per antenna, and at 3 dB and 25 degrees they cost one user 1.3 dB of signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) that no array size removes. With 80 users, such errors on both sides, and mismatched pilots, oracle per-user and base-station calibration leave 6.2 and 12.3 percent loss at 256 antennas on clustered channels. This ordering holds at 30 dB SNR and reverses for 1 dB, 10 degree user errors. A capped one-echo estimator comes within 2.4 percentage points of oracle per-user calibration but underperforms no calibration at 10 dB SNR.

</details>

*Shenghan Luo,Yin Xu,Cixiao Zhang,Yang Wang,Yujie Cao,Zhuoran Xiao,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: MirrorComm 是首个基于世界模型的通信高效协同感知框架，通过收发端镜像世界模型预测当前观测、仅传输预测误差更新，在 OPV2V 上实现最高约 66 倍的带宽降低且性能与 SOTA 相当。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同感知需大量带宽交换感知信息；现有压缩方法多为逐帧范式，忽略时间可预测性，反复传输可由历史上下文推断的信息。

Method: 发射端与接收端部署镜像世界模型，基于共享的已重建观测历史预测发射端当前观测；发射端将预测与真实观测比较，仅选择性地传输用于纠正预测误差的观测更新。

Conclusion: 利用时间可预测性进行预测误差驱动传输可显著降低协同感知带宽开销，世界模型镜像结构是有效的通信压缩范式。

Abstract: Cooperative perception (CP) requires substantial communication bandwidth to exchange sensing information among connected agents. A variety of compression techniques have been proposed to reduce transmission costs while retaining task-relevant information. However, most existing methods follow a frame-wise compression paradigm, overlooking temporal predictability and repeatedly transmitting information that could be inferred from historical context. To address these limitations, we propose MirrorComm, the first world model-based communication-efficient CP framework. Specifically, mirrored world models at the transmitter and receiver predict the transmitter's current observation from a shared history of its reconstructed observations. The transmitter compares this prediction with its current observation and selectively transmits observation updates to correct prediction errors, reducing repeated transmission of predictable information. Experiments on the OPV2V dataset across two downstream tasks including object detection and semantic segmentation show that MirrorComm reduces communication bandwidth requirements by up to approximately 66-fold while maintaining comparable performance to a state-of-the-art (SOTA) method. Ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of the individual components.

</details>

Abstract: Iterative receivers for coded multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) links estimate the channel from pilots, decoded data, and a prior. In short packets with few pilots and at low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), the prior matters. An expectation propagation (EP) turbo receiver needs the channel's mean and covariance given an anisotropic Gaussian message from the pilots and decoder. A diffusion prior's denoiser, however, returns only a mean, and only for a channel in isotropic noise. We replace only the receiver's channel-prior module with a learned diffusion prior. At an isotropic query formed from the message, one network gives both moments: its output is the mean, its Jacobian the covariance. They form a matrix-valued EP site, the Gaussian factor representing the prior. We show why an indefinite Jacobian can make the receiver diverge, and we floor its eigenvalues. In coded block error rate (BLER), the diffusion prior fails less than a Gaussian mixture model (GMM) prior learned from the same channels. It does so on a controlled sparse specular model and a 3GPP model, at every tested receive-array and training-set size. On the former, scalar-site controls, with one variance instead of the covariance, keep this advantage, so the gain comes from the diffusion prior.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2X是连接与自动驾驶的关键支撑，但无线信道开放使其易受物理层攻击，干扰（jamming）是最直观且严重的威胁之一；现有接收机通常依赖干扰机信道或空间方位先验，难以适应实际V2X环境。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication plays a crucial role in enabling connected and autonomous driving by supporting the reliable exchange of safety-critical information among vehicles and infrastructure. However, due to the open nature of wireless channels, V2X systems are vulnerable to various physical-layer attacks, among which jamming is one of the most intuitive and severe threats. In this paper, we propose a vehicle speed-aware jammer-resilient reception framework for multi-user multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) V2X systems. The proposed method exploits the fundamental difference in Doppler characteristics between stationary jammers and moving vehicles. By transforming the received signal into the Doppler domain, the receiver identifies low-Doppler components associated with static interference and suppresses them through Doppler-domain filtering. Notably, the proposed approach does not require prior knowledge of the jammer channel or its spatial direction, making it suitable for practical V2X environments. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively mitigates strong jamming signals and significantly improves the achievable sum-rate compared with conventional receivers.

</details>

*Geneva Ecola,Jida Zhang,Joseph M. Kahn,Zerina Kapetanovic*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 所搭建系统达到kbps量级数据率，较以往演示提升逾两个数量级；基于极化码的编码方案可达到理论推导的容量预期。

Abstract: Thermal noise communication systems transmit information by modulating thermal radiation, eliminating the need for a dedicated signal power source and making them attractive solutions for low-power sensor networks. Their efficiency enables long-lived, unobtrusive operation, which is valuable in the Internet of Things, electromagnetic interference-sensitive environments, and RFID tag systems. However, a limited understanding of the wireless channel model and efficient transmission schemes has hindered their practical deployment. Here, we present a wireless channel model that elucidates how systems operating at ambient temperature can modulate thermal noise to convey information. Our results reveal a thermal noise backscatter mechanism that enables significantly higher data rates than previously achieved. We designed and built a thermal noise communication system leveraging this mechanism to achieve data rates in the kilobits-per-second range - over two orders of magnitude higher than prior demonstrations. We also derive the system capacity and show that using polar codes, we are able to achieve this expected capacity. Our channel model and transmission scheme pave the way for new strategies in thermal noise sensing and communication, advancing practical low-power networking for next-generation wireless applications.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出可移动方向图可重构天线(MPRA)与三混合预编码框架，联合优化预编码器、天线位置与辐射方向图状态，实现能效提升。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Conventional hybrid precoding is confined to the digital/analog domains, leaving the spatial and radiation-pattern degrees-of-freedoms (DoFs) largely untapped. To unlock these, we propose a movable pattern-reconfigurable antenna (MPRA) and an energy-efficient tri-hybrid framework that jointly optimizes the precoders, antenna positions, and radiation-pattern states. We develop a hardware-inspired power model capturing the energy and transmission-time costs of antenna reconfiguration. The resulting mixed discrete-continuous fractional problem is solved via an alternating-optimization framework combining fractional programming, Riemannian optimization, and switching-aware pattern optimization. Simulations demonstrate near-exhaustive-search performance and consistent energy-efficiency (EE) gains, validating the complementary benefits of antenna movement, pattern reconfiguration, and tri-hybrid precoding under hardware-inspired power and latency costs.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable distributed acoustic surveillance system for real-time gunshot detection and localiza tion in large-area, GNSS-challenged environments. The system consists of multiple low-cost MEMS microphone array units (MAUs) deployed as distributed anchors. Each MAU performs edge-level sound-event detection using a lightweight Sound De tection and Echo Suppression (SDES) algorithm, followed by a TinyML-based classifier for gunshot identification. For detected gunshots, an enhanced Generalized Cross-Correlation (eGCC) al gorithm estimates intra-array time differences of arrival (TDoAs)
  with improved robustness under low signal-to-noise ratio and re verberant conditions. Angle-of-arrival (AoA) estimates obtained from individual MAUs are fused through a modified SCO-EV localization framework to determine the gunshot position in a global coordinate system. A misplacement calibration (MC)
  algorithm compensates for platform orientation errors, improv ing angular consistency and localization accuracy in practical field conditions. Extensive experiments conducted over coverage areas ranging from 4,985 m2 to 49,063 m2 demonstrate reliable operation across varying spatial scales. The proposed framework achieves a Matthews Correlation Coefficient (MCC) of 0.70 for gunshot recognition and average localization errors below 6.5 m over 25,000 m2 , while maintaining errors under 15 m at larger scales. The results demonstrate the practical potential of the proposed system for distributed gunshot surveillance in GNSS challenged large areas.

*Jean-Pierre Delmas,Habti Abeida*

TL;DR: 本文将 Whittle 公式推广到周期平稳实值及（非）圆复值复合高斯过程，并用于分析信源谱特性对 DOA 估计 CRB 的影响。

  <summary>Details</summary>
