Conclusion: 所提出的推广显著拓宽了 Whittle 理论框架的适用范围，为周期平稳与复值 CGP 的参数估计性能界分析提供了统一工具，并揭示信源谱特性对 DOA 估计 CRB 的作用规律。

Abstract: The classical Whittle formula provides a closed-form expression for the asymptotic Fisher information matrix (FIM) of stationary, zero-mean Gaussian processes (GPs) with finite-dimensional parameterizations, expressed in terms of their power spectra. While this result was recently extended to nonzero-mean, multidimensional, real-valued stationary compound-Gaussian processes (CGPs), its applicability remains limited. In this paper, we significantly broaden this theoretical framework with two primary contributions. First, we extend the Whittle formula to purely cyclostationary real-valued CGPs with parameterized cyclic spectra, and further generalize it to circular and noncircular complex-valued multidimensional stationary CGPs, encompassing both purely and almost cyclostationary CGPs. Second, we apply these novel Whittle-type formulas to evaluate the impact of source spectral characteristics on the Cramer-Rao bound (CRB) for direction-of-arrival (DOA) estimation, particularly in scenarios involving frequency offsets.

</details>


### [48] [How Temporal Dispersion of Radio Channels Depends on Signal Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03048)
*Jarosław Wojtuń,Tomasz Grasza,Cezary Ziółkowski,Jan M. Kelner,Aniruddha Chandra,Radek Zavorka,Josef Vychodil,Jiri Blumenstein,Tomas Mikulasek,Ales Prokes*

Abstract: The degree of temporal dispersion of a radio channel is the basis for assessing its transmission capabilities. These parameters provide the basis for assessing the degree of dispersion of the received signal. The measurement results show that both the mean and RMS values of the delay spread depend on the distance between the transmitter and receiver as well as the bandwidth of the transmitted signals. The function description of these relationships (model) is determined based on a statistical analysis of the obtained measurement data obtained in a selected indoor corridor under line-of-sight conditions.

</details>


### [49] [Resilient Cell-Free MIMO ISAC Under Uncertainties: An Impairment-Aware Graph Neural Network Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03054)
*Oways Alsoloh,Robert-Jeron Reifert,Aymen Khaleel,Stefan Roth,Aydin Sezgin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出损伤感知的异构图神经网络框架，以CSI误差水平与杂波协方差为边特征，单模型可同时适应CSI误差与杂波两类损伤，实现无蜂窝MIMO ISAC的鲁棒波束成形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无蜂窝MIMO ISAC的波束成形设计需在CSI不确定性和杂波干扰下保持可靠，而单一模型难以同时适应两类损伤。

Method: 构建异构图神经网络，将CSI误差水平与杂波协方差作为边特征输入；以最小用户速率的低分位数为训练目标，采用杂波感知的SINR（信号对杂波加噪声比），并通过归一化Tchebycheff标量化平衡感知与通信目标。

Result: 在仅感知与仅通信设置下均优于对比基线；视工作点不同，单一GNN模型在同时服务两种功能时可达各基线速率的约97%；并可泛化到未见过的CSI误差水平与杂波数目。

Conclusion: 损伤感知异构GNN能统一处理CSI误差与杂波损伤，实现鲁棒且可泛化的ISAC波束成形，提升系统韧性。

Abstract: Beamforming designs for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) in cell-free multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems face the challenge to stay reliable under uncertainties from both channel state information (CSI) as well as clutter. To this end, this paper proposes an impairment-aware heterogeneous graph neural network (GNN) framework for beamforming that obtains the CSI error level and the clutter covariance as edge features, which enables a single trained model to adapt to both impairments. In particular, the GNN is trained on a low quantile of the minimum user rate with a clutter-aware signal-to-clutter-plus-noise ratio, balanced by a normalized Tchebycheff scalarization. Simulation results show that the proposed GNN framework outperforms the considered baselines in sensing-only and communication-only settings. Depending on the operating point, a single GNN model attains approximately 97\% of each baseline's rate while serving both functionalities at once. The model further generalizes to unseen CSI error levels and clutter counts and renders the ISAC system resilient.

</details>


### [50] [Joint Data Association and Tracking for Surveillance Radar Using Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03314)
*Anders Malthe Westerkam,Troels Pedersen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种变分贝叶斯方法，联合推断数据关联变量与航迹后验，用于雷达杂波环境下单目标跟踪，性能优于伯努利滤波与卡尔曼滤波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机增多使受限空域安防需求上升，雷达是常用传感器，高斯过程跟踪是新兴手段；但雷达虚警普遍、弱目标跟踪尤甚，数据关联困难。

Method: 采用变分贝叶斯近似，对关联变量与目标航迹的联合后验进行推断，构建联合数据关联与跟踪算法（单目标、高斯基跟踪框架）。

Result: 在较宽的检测概率区间内，所提算法优于伯努利滤波（现有先进数据关联跟踪算法）及经典卡尔曼滤波。

Conclusion: 以变分推理统一处理关联不确定性与航迹估计，可有效应对雷达虚警下的单目标跟踪，具备优于基线方法的稳健性。

Abstract: The growing presence of unmanned aerial vehicles or drones has increased the necessity of developing methods to detect and track them to secure restricted airspaces. This can be done using radars which is a common sensor around e.g. airports, an emerging method to track targets is by using Gaussian processes. However, false alarms is ever present in radars especially if tracking weak targets complicating the data association task.
  We propose a joint data association and tracking algorithm for a single target using a variational Bayesian approach to approximate the full posterior of both the association variable as well as the track. We show that the proposed algorithm outperforms a state of the art data association and tracking algorithm in the form of a Bernoulli filter, as well as the classical Kalman filter, for a large range of detection probabilities.

</details>


### [51] [Multi-User Precoding with Stacked Intelligent Metasurfaces via Neural Reparameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03398)
*Yongqiang Zhang,Qurrat-Ul-Ain Nadeem*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种QoS约束感知的神经重参数化框架，用于堆叠智能超表面（SIM）辅助下行预编码，将级联波传播结构嵌入模型驱动可微架构，把相移配置与功率分配重参数化为结构神经网络参数，实现免离线训练的实时预编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIM可增强多用户系统频谱效率，但在QoS约束下联合优化发射功率与多层相移构成大规模非凸问题，交替优化等传统数值方法计算负担重、可扩展性差。

Method: 将SIM级联波传播结构嵌入模型驱动的可微架构，对相移配置和发射功率分配进行神经重参数化，使参数化结果天然满足原问题可行性约束；不依赖离线训练的"信道到预编码"映射，而是直接更新重参数化优化变量，对任意信道实现实时求解。

Result: 仿真表明，该方法在QoS约束下取得更优的和速率性能，同时相比传统数值优化方法显著提升计算效率。

Conclusion: 模型驱动神经重参数化可在保证可行性的前提下兼顾性能与效率，为SIM辅助无线系统的实时预编码提供有效途径。

Abstract: Stacked intelligent metasurfaces (SIMs) enable wave-domain signal processing, and provide a promising architecture for enhancing spectral efficiency in multi-user wireless communication systems. However, jointly optimizing transmit power and multi-layer phase shifts in SIM-assisted wireless systems under quality-of-service (QoS) constraints results in a large-scale nonconvex problem, for which conventional numerical optimization-based methods, such as alternating optimization (AO), suffer from high computational burden and limited scalability. In this paper, we develop a QoS constraint-aware neural reparameterization framework for SIM-assisted downlink precoding. By embedding the cascaded wave-propagation structure of the SIM into a model-based differentiable architecture, both the phase shift configuration and transmit power allocation are reparameterized as parameters of a structured neural model that inherently satisfies feasibility constraints of the original problem. The resulting formulation enables efficient optimization that does not rely on a channel-to-precoding mapping learned through offline training. Instead, it directly updates the reparameterized optimization variables to realize real-time precoding for any channel realization. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior sum rate performance under QoS constraints, while significantly improving computational efficiency compared with conventional numerical optimization approaches.

</details>


### [52] [GIST: Location-Dependent Uplink Path Structure Transfer for FDD Downlink Channel Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03482)
*Jinghan Zhang,Xitao Gong,Qi Wang,Mate Boban,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出GIST方法：用高斯基元将每条传播路径建模为随UE位置变化的时延/方向/增益预测器，仅用上行实测数据训练，从而以更少导频辅助FDD大规模MIMO下行信道估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDD大规模MIMO下行信道估计的导频开销随天线数与子载波数增长而急剧上升；上下行共享的路径时延与方向先验可降低开销，但需从UE位置预测路径结构以迁移该先验。

Method: GIST用高斯基元表示单条传播路径，由UE相对于基元中心的位置预测该路径的时延、方向与功率增益；再以该先验为条件，对下行导频做LMMSE估计得到预测路径的复增益。模型仅用基站侧实测的上行信道训练。

Result: 在射线追踪数据集与实测数据集上均优于所有基线，以更少的导频子载波取得更低的下行估计误差；并对UE位置误差鲁棒，存储与计算开销低。

Conclusion: 以位置为条件的路径结构迁移可作为FDD大规模MIMO下行信道估计的高效先验，兼顾低导频开销、鲁棒性与低资源需求。

Abstract: A key challenge of downlink (DL) channel estimation in frequency division duplex (FDD) massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems is the pilot overhead, which grows with the numbers of antennas and subcarriers. However, a prior on path delays and directions shared by the uplink (UL) and DL channels can reduce this overhead. A model trained only on UL channels measured at a base station (BS) can predict the path structure (delays, directions and power gains) from the location of a user equipment (UE) and thereby assist DL channel estimation. In this paper, we propose Gaussian-Informed Structure Transfer (GIST), in which each Gaussian primitive models one propagation path, whose delay, directions and power gain are predicted from the UE location relative to the primitive center. The complex gains of the predicted paths are estimated from DL pilots by linear minimum mean square error estimation with this prior. Experiments on a ray-traced and a measured dataset show that GIST outperforms all baselines, reaching a lower DL estimation error with fewer pilot subcarriers. Further experiments demonstrate its robustness to UE location errors, its low storage and computational requirements.

</details>


### [53] [Single-visit screening for intracochlear tissue growth validated against multi-visit electrical measurement changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03644)
*Minh Q. Vu,Erin L. Bratu,Jared A. Rybarczyk,Jack H. Noble*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于单次SCINSEV测量、以顶端拟合的患者特异性前向模型预测底端电压的算法，用于检测耳蜗植入电极周围显著组织生长(TG)的征象，无需术中基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电极周围组织生长会升高阻抗，但CT上难以可靠显示；常规记录的非刺激电极电压(SCINSEV)蕴含此信息，然而单次记录缺乏患者特异性参照。

Method: 假设TG集中于底端近插入处、顶端基本不受影响；由CT构建患者特异性前向模型，用顶端测量拟合并预测底端值；以失配分数T衡量各底端触点相对顶端失配的有符号相对偏差（考虑顶端拟合质量与噪声）；相邻两个底端触点越阈即判为阳性。与无模型参照T-delta（底端相对顶端SCINSEV自术中以来持续至近两次访视的变化）比较。

Result: T在无生长耳中假阳性率3%；在68耳中与T-delta一致率81%（敏感度71%、特异度86%、p=6e-6）；200次患者级两折划分中，留出集一致率中位数78%，假阳性率5.1%。

Conclusion: 顶端拟合的患者特异性模型对底端SCINSEV预测足够准确，可在单次访视、无术中基线条件下标记显著TG征象，且检测结果与持续的多访视底端-顶端变化这一替代真值高度一致。

Abstract: Purpose: Tissue growth (TG) around cochlear implant electrodes raises impedance but is not reliably visible on CT scans. Routinely recorded stimulus current-induced non-stimulating electrode voltages (SCINSEVs) carry this information, but a single recording has no patient-specific reference. We present an algorithm for detecting signs of significant TG from a single SCINSEV measurement. Methods: TG is assumed to concentrate basally near the electrode insertion site, leaving the apical region mostly unaffected. A patient-specific forward model built from CT is fitted to the apical measurements and predicts the basal ones. The misfit score, T, measures each basal contact's signed relative misfit against the apical misfit, accounting for apical fit quality and measurement noise. An ear is flagged when two adjacent basal contacts cross a predetermined threshold. We compare T with T-delta, a model-free reference, tracking basal relative to apical SCINSEV change since the intra-operative recording, persisting over the last two visits. Results: T had a false-positive rate of 3% on growth-free ears. It agreed with T-delta in 81% of 68 ears (sensitivity 71%; specificity 86%; p = 6e-6). Over 200 patient-level two-fold splits, median held-out agreement was 78% at a false-positive rate of 5.1%. Conclusion: An apically fitted patient-specific model predicts basal SCINSEVs well enough to flag signs of significant TG at a single visit, without an intra-operative baseline. Detections largely agree with persistent multi-visit basal-versus-apical change, a surrogate ground truth for significant TG.

</details>

### [54] [The covering radius of RM(1,9) is precisely 242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00070)
*Xiang-Dong Hou,Evelyn Sabo,Freeman Slaughter*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过计算机辅助证明给出 ρ(1,9)=242，解决了 Reed-Muller 码 RM(1,9) 覆盖半径的长期公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定 RM(1,9) 的覆盖半径 ρ(1,9) 是长期未决问题，已知仅局限于 242≤ρ(1,9)≤244 的区间，需要确定其精确值。

Method: 借助 GL(9,2) 下 RM(3,9)/RM(2,9) 中 349 个轨道的分类，进行计算机辅助搜索与证明；并提供可复现的 notebook（Verify_RM(1,9).ipynb）包含全部数据并重算每项检查。

Result: 证明 ρ(1,9)=242，将上界由 244 改进至 242，从而确定其精确值。

Conclusion: RM(1,9) 的覆盖半径精确等于 242，解决了这一长期公开问题；证明为计算机辅助且可通过公开仓库中的数据与 notebook 复现验证。

Abstract: Let $\text{RM}(r,n)$ be the $r$th order Reed-Mullet code of length $2^n$, and let $ρ(r,n)$ denote its covering radius. It is known that $242\leρ(1,9)\le 244$, and the determination of $ρ(1,9)$ is a long-standing open question. We give a computer-assisted proof that $ρ(1,9)=242$, leveraging the classification of the $349$ orbits in $\text{RM}(3,9)/\text{RM}(2,9)$ under $\text{GL}(9,2)$ [1,3]. The notebook to verify the computer search portion, Verify_RM(1,9).ipynb, contains all data and recomputes each check, and may be found in the repository https://github.com/FreemanSlaughter/Covering-Radius.

</details>


### [55] [Applying Changes to LDPC Codes by Using 3-Induced Matchings and Eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00082)
*Alireza Eshraghi Dehaghani,Sara Saeedi Madani,Dariush Kiani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将用于减少LDPC码Tanner图中短环的2-switch操作推广到3-induced matching，给出围长≥6的二部图中存在3-induced matching的充要条件，并据此在保持二部性与度序列不变的前提下选取最优变换以减少6环数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作利用2-switch操作减少LDPC码Tanner图中的短环；但在2-induced matching上该操作只有唯一选择，灵活性与优化空间有限，因此作者希望借助3-induced matching获得更多可行的图变换方案，同时保证二部性和度序列不变。

Method: 以二部图的3-induced matching为核心工具，利用子图的特征值来构造/判定3-induced matching；给出围长至少为6的二部图中3-induced matching存在的充要条件，并在算法步骤中加入可检验的条件以确保子图至少含有一个3-induced matching；在所有候选变换中按减少长度为6的环数量进行最优选择。

Result: 建立了围长≥6二部图中3-induced matching存在的充要条件；证明3-induced matching可在保持二部性、度序列不变的前提下提供更多变换选择；给出选取最优变换以最小化6环数的方法，并报告了关于"检查保证3-induced matching存在条件所在步骤"的数值结果。

Conclusion: 3-induced matching为Tanner图的环削减提供了比2-switch更灵活且安全的变换框架，既能严格保持二部性与度序列，又能在多方案中择优降低6环数量，对LDPC码的围长/短环优化具有实用价值。

Abstract: The 2-switch operation for reducing the number of short cycles in Tanner graphs of LDPC codes has been utilized in some works. If this operation is appropriately applied to a 2-induced matching in a bipartite graph, then it surely preserves the bipartiteness of the graph. With a 2-induced matching, we have only one choice for applying this type of change to the graph. Using 3-induced matchings creates more potential and flexibility to apply certain changes to the Tanner graph. We present a necessary and sufficient condition for the existence of a 3-induced matching in bipartite graphs with girth at least 6. A 3-induced matching helps us to have more choices for applying a change to a bipartite graph while surely preserving the bipartiteness of the graph and keeping the degree sequences unchanged. Among all changes that we consider, we select the best change for reducing the number of cycles of length 6. The eigenvalues of subgraphs are used to reach a 3-induced matching, and a condition in some steps can be checked to ensure that the subgraph has at least one 3-induced matching. We present some numerical results regarding the steps at which the condition that guarantees the existence of a 3-induced matching is checked.

</details>


### [56] [HideOut - User Protection Against Localization with Throughput Guarantees in Mobile Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00088)
*Navidreza Asadi,Michele Guardiani,Wolfgang Kellerer,Jafar Aco Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: HideOut 是一种客户端防御机制，通过对上报的 CSI 施加受速率可行集约束的梯度式定位混淆，在保持下行频谱效率（SE）损失在 1% 预算内的同时，将以 CSI 为基础的神经定位误差从约 1–2 m 提升到 72–85 m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝网络中 CSI 被明文上报且调度公开，攻击者捕获后可用训练好的 AI 模型轻易定位 UE，构成严重隐私漏洞；而差分隐私等加性扰动类传统隐私增强手段可能损害下行通信质量，因此需要一种兼顾定位隐私与通信质量的防御方法。

Method: HideOut 将下行 SE 作为严格约束纳入优化：在 MRT 波束成形下，证明用户选定的 SE 损失预算可转化为上报信道向量的角度速率可行约束；随后执行基于梯度的定位混淆，并在每次更新后将扰动投影到该速率可行集上。该方法在四种基于 CSI 的定位架构、白盒与黑盒两种假设下评估。

Result: 跨模型评估显示，HideOut 将定位误差（混淆度）从约 1–2 m 提升至 72–85 m，同时下行 SE 损失保持在 1% 预算以内。

Conclusion: 当对抗扰动被赋予无线链路的感知（link-aware）时，基于 CSI 的定位可被显著削弱且通信质量损失可忽略，表明 HideOut 是一种有效的客户端隐私保护方案。

Abstract: Channel state information (CSI) in cellular networks is primarily used to maintain quality of service (QoS), yet it leaks privacy-sensitive information, in particular the user equipment's (UE) location. CSI is reported in plaintext on a publicly available schedule; thus, capturing it on the air and using a well trained AI model to localize the UE is straightforward. This simplicity in the attack execution makes it a serious privacy vulnerability. Using conventional privacy enhancing techniques, such as additive perturbation from differential privacy, to protect location data can potentially degrade the downlink communication quality. We present HideOut, a client-side defense mechanism that preserves the user's location from neural-based localization methods, while maintaining their downlink Spectral Efficiency (SE). Unlike generic adversarial attacks, HideOut treats downlink SE as a strict constraint in an optimization setting. Under maximum ratio transmission (MRT) beamforming, we show that a user-selected SE loss budget can be converted into an angular rate-feasible constraint on the reported channel vector. HideOut then performs gradient-based localization obfuscation while projecting each update onto this rate-feasible set. We evaluate HideOut against four CSI-based localization architectures under both white-box and black-box assumptions. Across models, HideOut increases localization ambiguity (error) from approximately $1--2$ m to $72--85$ m while keeping downlink SE loss within a $1\%$ budget. These results show that CSI-based localization can be substantially degraded at negligible communication quality loss when adversarial perturbations are made aware of the wireless link.

</details>


### [57] [Energy-Efficient UAV Covert Communications via Metaheuristic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00090)
*Nguyen Minh Quan,Tran Thanh Thu,Nguyen The Ngoc Khue,Ninh Thi Thanh Tam,Trinh Van Chien*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 研究多主動勾結看守者且位置信息不完美条件下，UAV隐蔽通信系统的3D轨迹与功率联合优化，以最大化隐蔽能量效率（CEE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信系统对传输不可检测性（隐蔽性）需求上升，而UAV高机动性可提升隐蔽通信性能；现有工作多考虑单一或位置精确的看守者，缺乏多主动勾结且位置不完美场景下的能效研究。

Method: 建立UAV服务合法接收机、存在多个主动且勾结看守者（位置信息不完美）的隐蔽通信模型；以能耗为约束、隐蔽性为鲁棒约束，联合优化UAV三维轨迹与发射功率以最大化CEE；将非凸问题用遗传算法（GA）与粒子群优化（PSO）求解，并与BCD-SCA基准对比。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提GA与PSO方法在满足隐蔽性约束的同时提升了隐蔽能量效率，性能与BCD-SCA基准具有可比性/优势（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 元启发式算法可有效处理带鲁棒隐蔽约束的非凸联合优化问题，为不完美位置信息下多看守者UAV隐蔽通信的能效设计提供可行方案。

Abstract: Wireless communication systems increasingly require not only confidentiality but also transmission undetectability, motivating covert communications. This paper studies an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) enabled covert communication system where a UAV serves a legitimate receiver in the presence of multiple active and colluding wardens with imperfect location information. We maximize covert energy efficiency (CEE) by jointly optimizing the UAV's 3D trajectory and transmit power while accounting for energy. To address the resulting non-convex problem with robust covertness constraints, we develop Genetic Algorithm (GA) and Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO)-based metaheuristic algorithms and compare them with a Block Coordinate Descent with Successive Convex Approximation (BCD-SCA) benchmark. Numerical results show that the proposed methods improve CEE while satisfying covertness constraints.

</details>


### [58] [Comments on "Multidimensional Costas Arrays and Their Periodicity"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00098)
*Shiv Munagala*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文给出一个阶为6、域与像均为二维的四维周期Costas阵列，从而否定了Rubio–Torres猜想，并说明其鸽巢界是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rubio与Torres猜想：定义在两个整数点盒之间双射上的多维Costas阵列，若域维数不少于像维数，则其周期延拓在每个窗口中都保持Costas性质，仅当阶数为由域维数确定的2的幂。本文旨在检验并反驳该猜想。

Method: 构造一个四维周期Costas阵列，阶为6，域为二维、像为二维；通过逐一检验周期延拓的36个窗口来验证其Costas性质，并用计算机及Lean证明助手进行形式化确认。

Result: 该阶6阵列在所有36个窗口中均为Costas，反例成立；且该阵列恰好取等Rubio–Torres用于非存在性结果的鸽巢上界，说明该界是紧的。

Conclusion: Rubio–Torres猜想为假；鸽巢界紧，不能作为更强的非存在性判据。

Abstract: Rubio and Torres conjectured that a multidimensional Costas array, defined by a bijection between two boxes of integer points whose domain has at least as many dimensions as its image, can stay Costas in every window of its periodic extension only if its order is a power of two fixed by the dimension of the domain. We give a four-dimensional periodic Costas array of order $6$, with a two-dimensional domain and a two-dimensional image, so the conjecture is false. The verification reduces to $36$ windows. It has been confirmed by computer and in the Lean proof assistant. The array also meets with equality the pigeonhole bound that Rubio and Torres use for their non-existence results, so that bound is tight.

</details>

*Chandra Nair,Aref Namayandeh,Zhaobang Zhu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文构造反例否定了非交换情形下的Gyarmati-Matolcsi-Ruzsa猜想，反例来自等价的极大耦合熵不等式，具体给出$D_4^{14}$中的显式基数反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gyarmati-Matolcsi-Ruzsa猜想在交换群情形已有研究，其非交换推广是否成立尚不明确，本文旨在检验该推广的正确性。

Method: 建立该猜想与一个极大耦合熵不等式之间的等价关系，并在维数更低的群中寻找该熵不等式的反例，进而转化为显式的基数（cardinality）反例。

Result: 在$D_4^{14}$（8阶二面体群的14次直积）中给出显式基数反例，从而推翻非交换Gyarmati-Matolcsi-Ruzsa猜想。

Conclusion: 非交换Gyarmati-Matolcsi-Ruzsa猜想一般不成立；相应的极大耦合熵不等式亦不普遍成立。

Abstract: We provide a counterexample to the non-abelian Gyarmati-Matolcsi-Ruzsa conjecture. The construction comes from an equivalent maximal-coupling entropy inequality, for which a counterexample can be found in a lower-dimensional group. We give an explicit cardinality counterexample in $D_4^{14}$, the fourteenth direct power of the dihedral group of order $8$.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond Typical Strings: Sampling the Complexity Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00162)
*Abdurrahman Hadi Erturk,Joshua Sharkey,Nora S Martin,Kamal Dingle,Ard A Louis*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出嵌套中性集估计器(NNSE)用于稀有事件蒙特卡洛采样，估计定长二进制串复杂度分布P(K)，并引入均匀复杂度加权(UCW)，在数据集无关条件下比较LZ76、LZ78、CTW、BDM与块熵等复杂度度量的全谱行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀随机采样几乎完全被高复杂度串占据，低与中等复杂度串（多为指数级稀有）难以被直接采样访问，导致复杂度度量的全谱行为、尤其是低中复杂度区间的差异长期未被考察。

Method: 将NNSE（一种蒙特卡洛稀有事件采样方法）适配于估计定长二进制串的复杂度分布P(K)，可覆盖长度达数百的串并对指数稀有区域进行采样；同时提出均匀复杂度加权(UCW)，对不同复杂度层级而非单个串赋予等权重，形成数据集无关的比较框架，并应用于LZ76、LZ78、CTW、BDM与块熵五种度量。

Result: NNSE能够重构完整的复杂度景观，包括直接采样不可及的低复杂度区域；基于UCW的采样揭示各度量在低与中等复杂度串上存在显著差异，而这些差异在传统均匀采样下基本被掩盖。

Conclusion: NNSE结合UCW提供了跨全谱、数据集无关的复杂度度量比较框架，弥补了均匀采样偏倚，凸显低中复杂度区间各度量行为的分歧，为复杂度度量的校准与选择提供新依据。

Abstract: Uniform random sampling of strings is overwhelmingly dominated by high-complexity strings, leaving the much smaller set of low- and intermediate-complexity strings largely unexplored. Here, we adapt the nested neutral set estimator (NNSE), a Monte Carlo rare-event sampling method, to estimate the distribution P(K) of complexity values for binary strings of fixed length. NNSE allows us to explore the full complexity landscape, including exponentially rare low-complexity regions that are inaccessible to direct sampling, for string lengths up to several hundred. We apply the method to several widely used complexity measures: Lempel-Ziv 76 (LZ76), Lempel-Ziv 78 (LZ78), context tree weighting (CTW), the block decomposition method (BDM), and block entropy. We further introduce uniform complexity weighting (UCW), which gives equal weight to different complexity levels rather than to individual strings. This approach provides a dataset-independent framework for comparing complexity measures across their full range and reveals substantial differences between measures for low- and intermediate-complexity strings that are largely hidden by conventional uniform sampling. Using the NNSE to perform UCW sampling allows us to compare complexity measures across their full range in a dataset-independent way. This reveals differences in their behavior for low- and intermediate-complexity strings that are practically inaccessible to conventional uniform sampling.

</details>

*Jiang Chang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文计算了二进制常权码的Polak界，得到A_4(19,6,8)≈673.04001，结合精确有理数证书证明A(19,6,8)≤673，将Brouwer表中上界693改进为673。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制常权码A(n,d,w)的精确值难以确定，Brouwer表中n=19,d=6,w=8的上界693可能不紧，需要借助LP型松弛界（Polak界）加以收紧。

Method: 计算Polak界（基于Delsarte线性规划A_4阶矩约束）在参数(19,6,8)处的值，构造精确有理数证书，并用独立程序以精确有理算术验证证书正确性。

Result: Polak界数值为673.04001，精确证书给出A_4(19,6,8)≤673.0400143064802474039375132665301315202504，从而A(19,6,8)≤673；证书经独立程序精确算术验证通过。

Conclusion: A(19,6,8)的上界由693改进至673，说明Polak高阶LP界在该参数下强于原记录界；浮点结果经精确有理证明保障严格性。

Abstract: We compute Polak's bound $A_4(19,6,8)$ for binary constant weight codes, obtaining the numerical value $673.04001$. An exact rational certificate gives $A_4(19,6,8)\le 673.0400143064802474039375132665301315202504$, and hence $A(19,6,8)\le 673$. This improves the upper bound $693$ listed in Brouwer's table. The certificate is verified by a separate program using exact rational arithmetic.

</details>


### [62] [Equal Hamming Weights at Fixed Root Order and Applications to Constacyclic MDS Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00637)
*Li Zhu,Hongfeng Wu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 研究分圆多项式 Φ_n 在 F_q 上所有不可约因子是否具有相同系数 Hamming 权，用分圆轨道系数给出有限有效的分类，并将其应用于 MDS 常循环码的结构限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分圆多项式因子系数的稀疏性与一致性是有限域结构中的基本问题，且与不可约生成元下的 MDS 常循环码的存在性密切相关；已有结果多依赖 p 的大小或长度/字母表假设，缺少统一、可计算的判据。

Method: 以 Φ_n 在 F_q 上的分圆轨道及其零特征轨道多项式的支撑为工具，作有限有效分类；对剩余特征，将扩域指数归约为 ord_n(p) 的因子，并用多项式 gcd 次数表示的整数方差恒等式判定等权；由同一系数数据导出完整权分布。

Result: 除有限多个可计算特征外，每个因子都具有其零特征轨道多项式的支撑，p>2^{φ(n)} 即可保证；剩余情形由 ord_n(p) 的因子与 gcd 次数方差判据完全决定；证明含不可约生成元的 MDS 常循环码迫使同根序的所有不可约多项式满支撑，得到统一 gcd 与范数障碍，并给出短乘子推广、素数阶三次族及显式二次剩余排除。

Conclusion: Φ_n 因子的等权性可由有限轨道系数数据完全判定，并给出可计算的权分布；该框架把等权条件转化为 MDS 常循环码的整数障碍，统一并放宽了以往对字母表与长度的限制。

Abstract: For a fixed positive integer $n>1$, we study the prime powers $q$ coprime to $n$ for which all monic irreducible factors of $Φ_n$ over $\F_q$ have the same coefficient Hamming weight. Cyclotomic orbit coefficients give a finite, effective classification of these fields. Outside finitely many computable characteristics, every factor has the support of its characteristic-zero orbit polynomial; the uniform condition $p>2^{\varphi(n)}$ suffices. In each remaining characteristic, extension exponents reduce to divisors of $\ord_n(p)$, and an integer variance identity expressed by polynomial gcd degrees decides equal weight. The same coefficient data determine the full weight distribution. As an application, we prove that an MDS constacyclic code with an irreducible generator forces every irreducible polynomial of the same root order to have full support, including factors associated with different constacyclic constants. This yields a uniform gcd and norm obstruction. We develop its short-multiplier extension, give a prime-order cubic family, and establish an explicit quadratic-residue exclusion without a prime-square alphabet or a near-square length hypothesis.

</details>


### [63] [Spatial Token Interleaving and Two-Pass BP Decoding for Semantic Image Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00697)
*Yifan Feng,Chentao Yue,Haiyao Yu,Gaoyang Pang,Xinyu Chen,Hao Chang,Branka Vucetic,Yonghui Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向图像传输的语义纠错编码（SECC），采用空间token交织与两遍置信传播（BP）调度，在5G NR LDPC块上联合VQ tokenizer与自回归模型Emu3-Gen，显著降低token错误率并超越深度联合信源信道编码基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义纠错编码（SECC）仅面向文本，尚缺乏针对图像传输的设计；而图像经VQ tokenizer后以光栅顺序分块传输时，单个BP解码失败的LDPC块会破坏连续多行token，错误呈空间聚集，不利于纠错。

Method: 发射端用VQ tokenizer将图像转为token，按5G NR的多个LDPC码块传输，并采用无需额外传输比特的交织器重排token，使相邻token落入不同码块。接收端采用两遍BP：第一遍BP后，将BP给出的token概率与自回归模型Emu3-Gen的预测融合完成token恢复；第二遍以融合分布作为信息比特先验，对每个码块再次BP解码。

Result: 在Kodak24、每像素0.5信道使用、SNR为0.15 dB条件下，第二遍BP消除token恢复后残留token错误的92%，交织使最终token错误率降低88%；在中高SNR下，所提接收机在相同信道使用数下PSNR较深度联合信源信道编码基线至少高1.2 dB。

Conclusion: 将空间token交织与两遍BP调度引入图像SECC，可有效解耦码块级失败与token空间相关性，并充分利用生成模型先验，实现优于深度JSCC基线的图像传输性能。

Abstract: Semantic error control coding (SECC) can correct the residual errors of a standard channel code with a foundation model that supplies a prior on the source content, but existing SECC schemes are designed for text. In this paper, we develop SECC for image transmission with spatial token interleaving and a two-pass belief propagation (BP) schedule. At the transmitter, a vector-quantized (VQ) tokenizer converts an image into tokens, which are transmitted in multiple low-density parity-check (LDPC) code blocks of fifth-generation New Radio (5G NR). In raster order, a block that BP fails to decode corrupts consecutive token rows, so the interleaver reorders the tokens such that adjacent tokens are carried by different blocks, without additional transmitted bits. At the receiver, after the first BP pass, token recovery fuses the token probabilities given by BP with the predictions of the autoregressive model Emu3-Gen, and the second BP pass decodes each block again with the fused distributions as priors on the information bits. On Kodak24 at 0.5 channel uses per pixel and a signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of 0.15 dB, the second pass removes 92% of the token errors left by token recovery, and interleaving reduces the final token error rate by 88%. At medium to high SNR, the proposed receiver exceeds deep joint source--channel coding baselines with the same channel uses by at least 1.2 dB in peak signal-to-noise ratio.

</details>


### [64] [Noise Stability on the Hypercube: Order $7/8$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00868)
*Lei Yu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究离散超立方体上平衡布尔函数的α-稳定性 E[(T_ρf)^α]（0<α<1），证明独裁函数在所有维度和所有相关度下最小化7/8-稳定性，并由此推出α∈[7/8,1)时最小化、α∈(1,2)时最大化α-稳定性，以及最大化单边Shannon熵稳定性，解决Li–Médard猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡布尔函数上的稳定性量（α-稳定性、熵稳定性）与噪声稳定性、Brascamp–Lieb型不等式及Courtade–Kumar猜想密切相关。此前Li与Médard提出猜想：独裁函数应最小化某类稳定性；但缺乏对所有维度和所有相关度都成立的统一刻画。本文旨在给出一个可证明的临界指数（7/8）作为突破口，从而系统解决该猜想并强化平衡情形下的Courtade–Kumar结果。

Method: 以布尔噪声算子T_ρ为工具，引入并利用非对称Hellinger核估计，针对α=7/8这一临界指数建立变分/泛函不等式；通过在超立方体上对该核估计做展开与极值分析，证明独裁函数为全局极小元，并在内部相关度0<ρ<1处证明极小元的唯一性；再借助稳定性关于α的单调/凸性插值，将7/8处的结论推广到α∈[7/8,1)与α∈(1,2)两区间，并处理单边Shannon熵稳定性的极限情形。

Result: 证明：(1) 在每个维度和每个0≤ρ≤1下，独裁函数最小化7/8-稳定性；(2) 在内部相关度处独裁函数是唯一极小元；(3) 因而对任意7/8≤α<1，独裁函数最小化α-稳定性；对任意1<α<2，独裁函数最大化α-稳定性；(4) 独裁函数最大化单边Shannon熵稳定性 E[T_ρf log T_ρf]。

Conclusion: 本文以7/8为临界指数给出统一的极值刻画，解决了Li–Médard猜想，并在平衡布尔函数设定下强化了Courtade–Kumar猜想。结果揭示了Hellinger核估计在布尔函数稳定性极值问题中的有效性，为α-稳定性与熵稳定性的极值分类提供了完整框架。

Abstract: For a balanced Boolean function $f$ on the discrete hypercube, we study the $α$-stability $\mathbb{E}[(T_ρf)^α]$, where $T_ρ$ is the Boolean noise operator and $0<α<1$. Using the asymmetric Hellinger kernel estimates recently established by OpenAI, we prove that dictators minimize the $7/8$-stability in every dimension and at every correlation $0\leρ\le1$. At interior correlations they are the only minimizers. As a consequence, dictators minimize the $α$-stability for every $7/8\leα<1$, maximize the $α$-stability for every $1<α<2$, and maximize the one-sided Shannon entropy stability $\mathbb{E}[T_ρf\log T_ρf]$. This resolves a conjecture of Li and Médard and strengthens the Courtade--Kumar conjecture in the balanced setting.

</details>


### [65] [Closed-Form Gaussian Mixture Representations of d-Dimensional Triangulated Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00922)
*Ali Darijani,Benedikt Stratmann,Uwe D. Hanebeck,Ratan Bahadur Thapa,Jürgen Beyerer,Nehal Afifi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文从有限单纯形剖分出发，以KL散度最小化将每个单纯形上的均匀PDF替换为高斯PDF（信息投影），并给出GM表示对原始域均匀分布全局KL散度的与几何无关、仅依赖维数的尖锐上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种闭式的、由单纯形剖分构造的高斯混合（GM）表示来逼近任意有限体积区域上的均匀分布，并定量刻画逼近误差。

Method: 在每个单纯形上以最小化KL散度的方式选取高斯PDF（信息投影）；利用单纯形间的仿射同构、KL散度的联合凸性与双射不变性、Dirichlet PDF恒等式及单位分解论证。

Result: 得到全局KL散度（GM PDF与原域均匀PDF之间）的一个仅依赖维数、与几何无关的尖锐上界；数值算例验证构造并展示实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该闭式GM构造可有效逼近剖分域上的均匀分布，其逼近误差具有维度依赖的尖锐理论保证。

Abstract: We derive a Gaussian Mixture (GM) representation of a full-dimensional subset of Euclidean space with finite volume from a finite simplicial triangulation in closed form. On each simplex, we replace the uniform probability density function (PDF) with a Gaussian PDF that minimizes the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence, arriving at the information projection onto the family of Gaussian PDFs. By exploiting affine isomorphisms between simplices, the joint convexity and bijection invariance properties of the KL divergence, identities for Dirichlet PDFs, and a partition-of-unity argument, we establish a dimension-dependent but geometry-independent sharp upper bound on the global KL divergence between the resulting GM PDF and the uniform PDF on the original domain. Numerical examples illustrate the construction and demonstrate its potential in practical applications.

</details>


### [66] [A density theorem for non-standard irreducible cyclic codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00925)
*Tao Feng,Henk D. L. Hollmann,Weicong Li,Qing Xiang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 证明了Berger–Charpin猜想在固定特征下的密度形式：对每个素数p，允许非标准不可约循环码的长度集在特征p下自然密度为零，从而几乎所有长度下这类码的置换自同构仅为循环移位与Frobenius映射生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berger–Charpin猜想断言非退化不可约循环码除少数例外（非标准码）外，其置换自同构群仅由循环移位与Frobenius生成。本文旨在给出该猜想的定量版本，从"逐例验证"转向"几乎处处成立"的密度陈述。

Method: 采用Feng–Hollmann–Li–Xiang关于非标准不可约循环码的分类；给出该文中派生构造（deriving constructions）的标准形（normal form）；结合Wiertelak关于乘法阶的定理（采用Moree回忆的形式）作解析数论估计。

Result: 对每个固定素数p，存在非标准不可约循环码（在特征p的某个有限域上）的长度之自然密度为0；等价地，对几乎每个与p互素的长度，特征p任意有限域上的此类码只有由循环移位和Frobenius生成的置换自同构。

Conclusion: Berger–Charpin猜想在固定特征下以密度意义几乎处处成立；结果将代数分类与乘法阶的解析估计相结合，给出了猜想的定量化表述。

Abstract: We prove a fixed-characteristic density form of the Berger-Charpin conjecture for non-degenerate irreducible cyclic codes: for each prime $p$, the lengths admitting such a non-standard code over some finite field of characteristic $p$ have natural density zero. Thus, for almost every length coprime to $p$, every such code over every finite field of characteristic $p$ has only the permutation automorphisms generated by the cyclic shift and the Frobenius map. The proof uses the Feng--Hollmann--Li--Xiang classification for non-standard irreducible cyclic codes, a normal form for the deriving constructions in that paper, and a theorem by Wiertelak on multiplicative orders, in the form recalled by Moree.

</details>


### [67] [Shared Randomness and Parallel Rounds in Permutation Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01028)
*Haoxiang Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究并行输出轮次下采样均匀置换所需的共享随机性。作者刻画了在每轮固定密度ρ下，任意o(n)共享预算的最优总变差误差等于条件边缘最优，而固定改进需线性代价；精确采样在R=Θ(n)轮时最优成本为nΦ(R/n)+O(log n)，并给出仅用初始种子加至多一次二元共享选择即可达到该率的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究并行环境下生成均匀随机置换的共享随机性成本，明确共享随机比特预算与采样精度（总变差误差）之间的定量权衡关系，并探讨精确采样的最优共享成本随轮数R的变化规律。

Method: 设定模型：同轮提交的坐标在完整历史与当前共享选择条件下独立，先前输出公开，本地计算与私有随机免费；按共享选择的条件支撑集大小取对数计费，并沿最坏执行路径累加。利用稀疏单射的熵不等式控制与目标分布的重叠以证明近似下界（逆定理），并构造基于共享标签划分、复用首阶段输出暴露后保留的随机性的正向上界协议。

Result: 在每轮固定密度ρ∈(0,1)下，任意o(n)共享预算的最优极限总变差误差等于条件边缘最优，即使允许任意本地核与自适应协调；实现固定改进需线性成本。R=Θ(n)轮的精确采样最优成本为nΦ(R/n)+O(log n)，其中Φ线性插值Φ(1/q)=log q；达到该率的协议仅需初始种子和至多一次额外二元共享选择。当⌈n/2⌉≤R≤n时，有限最优值为log C(2(n-R), n-R)，仅由初始种子即可达到。

Conclusion: 并行置换采样中，低于线性量级的共享随机性无法带来优于条件边缘最优的误差，跨越该阈值需线性代价；精确采样的最优共享成本被精确刻画，且高效的初始种子加少量二元选择方案即可达到渐近最优，揭示首阶段随机性可被后续阶段复用。

Abstract: We study the shared randomness needed to sample a uniform permutation in parallel output rounds. Coordinates committed in the same round are independent conditional on the complete history and the current shared choice. Previous outputs are public; local computation and private randomness are free. We charge each shared choice by the logarithm of its conditional support size and sum these costs along the worst execution path. At every fixed round density $ρ\in(0,1)$, the optimal limiting total-variation error under any $o(n)$ shared budget matches the conditional-marginal optimum, even with arbitrary local kernels and adaptive coordination. A fixed improvement requires linear cost. For exact sampling in $R=Θ(n)$ rounds, the optimum is $nΦ(R/n)+O(\log n)$, where $Φ$ linearly interpolates $Φ(1/q)=\log q$. A protocol attaining this rate uses an initial seed and at most one further binary shared choice. When $\lceil n/2\rceil\le R\le n$, the finite optimum is $\log\binom{2(n-R)}{n-R}$, attained by an initial seed alone. The approximate converse uses an entropy inequality for sparse injections to control overlap with the target. The exact construction reuses randomness retained in a shared label partition after the first-stage outputs are exposed.

</details>

*Haobo Ma,Wenlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文在均匀传输下确定了2-删除与2-插入信道的最大输入熵接收字，并给出正嵌入直方图与3-错误极值字的精确计算与猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在删除/插入信道中，最大化输入熵的接收字（即让各可能发送字等可能的接收字）对应最优编码的输入分布，因此刻画这些"极大字"对构造删除纠正码的容量与码设计至关重要；此前仅对单错误情形有完整结论。

Method: 对2-删除：按游程组织嵌入计数恒等式，结合严格凸性估计并分组讨论双位置的删除掩码；对2-插入：证明长度≥5时交替字仍为极大字并穷举小长度情形；对插入半径一般情形给出交替接收字的完整正嵌入直方图，并确定其任意长度的3-删除直方图；再以精确整数比较认证接收长度≤12的3-删除极大字与≤18的3-插入极大字。

Result: 2-删除的极大字在每个正接收长度下恰为两个交替字；2-插入在接收长度≥5时同样恰为两个交替字，长度<5的全部情形与确切最大值被列出；辅助计算给出了3-删除/3-插入的极大字清单及精确小长度例外。

Conclusion: 交替字是2-删除信道所有长度、2-插入信道长度≥5时的唯一最大熵接收字；基于精确计算的3-错误情形呈现清晰规律并仅在小长度处出现例外，可表述为精确的3-错误猜想。

Abstract: Under uniform transmission, we determine the received binary words that maximize input entropy for two-deletion and two-insertion channels. For two deletions the maximizers are exactly the two alternating words at every positive received length. For two insertions the same conclusion holds from received length five onward; we give all smaller cases and exact maximal values. The proof combines an embedding-count identity organized by runs with strict convexity estimates, and a grouping of two-position deletion masks. For every insertion radius we give the complete positive embedding histogram of an alternating received word. We also determine its three-deletion histogram for every length, and certify all three-deletion maximizers through received length twelve and all three-insertion maximizers through length eighteen by exact integer comparison. These computations give precise three-error conjectures with their small-length exceptions.

</details>


### [69] [Digital Twin-Assisted Fast Beam Alignment for mmWave MIMO Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01107)
*Muhammad Kamran Saeed,Ashfaq Khokhar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出一种融合数字孪生辅助预测、区域定位、加权集成学习与可解释性驱动的波束剪枝的时延感知波束管理框架，以低开销实现高精度毫米波波束预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波通信依赖方向性波束扫描完成初始接入，天线阵列规模增大与部署密集化导致时延和信令开销显著上升；基于机器学习的波束预测虽可降低开销，但依赖能准确反映部署环境的训练数据，实际中难以获取。

Method: 构建统一的时延感知波束管理架构：数字孪生离线生成站点特定射线追踪信道知识，实现无需详尽实测的环境感知波束预测，支持初始接入与波束跟踪；采用基分类器与专家分类器经可靠性加权融合的集成学习提升精度与鲁棒性；利用XAI归因剪枝将波束码本缩减为最具信息量的少量波束子集。为刻画DT与真实环境的分布失配，网络在非理想数字孪生数据上训练、在真实世界surrogate数据上评估。

Result: 所提方法取得96.80%的top-1与98.36%的top-3波束预测准确率，相对穷举扫描将近一个数量级地降低空口探测开销，并优于已有卷积网络与深度神经网络基线。

Conclusion: 数字孪生辅助的集成预测结合可解释性波束剪枝，可在训练数据不完美的现实条件下有效降低毫米波波束管理时延与开销，是一种可落地的统一框架。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) communication relies on directional beam sweeping for initial access, which incurs substantial latency and signaling overhead as antenna arrays grow large and deployments become denser. Machine-learning-based beam prediction can reduce this overhead, but its effectiveness depends on training data that accurately reflects the deployment environment, a requirement that is difficult to meet in practice. This paper presents a latency-aware beam management architecture that integrates digital-twin-assisted prediction, regionbased localization, weighted ensemble learning, and explainability-driven beam pruning into a unified framework. A digital twin (DT) generates site-specific ray-traced channel knowledge offline, enabling environment-aware beam prediction without exhaustive real-world measurements and supporting both initial access and beam tracking. The proposed ensemble learning scheme combines a base classifier and a specialist classifier through reliability-weighted fusion to improve prediction accuracy and robustness, while explainable artificial intelligence (XAI)-based attribution pruning reduces the beam codebook to a small subset of the most informative beams for beam allocation. To capture the distributional mismatch between the DT and real-world environments: the network was trained on imperfect digital-twin data but evaluated on real-world surrobase data. Results show that the proposed method achieves 96.80% top-1 and 98.36% top-3 beam prediction accuracy, while reducing over-the-air sounding overhead by nearly an order of magnitude relative to exhaustive sweeping, outperforming convolutional and deep neural network baselines from prior work.

</details>


### [70] [Sub Terahertz LEO Satellite Communication: Is Doppler Shift a Major Challenge?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01264)
*Seongjoon Kang,Parisa Ramezani,Ki Won Sung,Vitaly Petrov*

TL;DR: 本文研究OFDM传输下多普勒频移对亚太赫兹LEO卫星通信的影响，构建综合评估框架并比较三种工程方案，结论是多普勒效应虽不可忽略，但仅需少量低复杂度措施（甚至一次5分钟过境内的频移估计）即可消除大部分负面影响，不构成关键设计难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚太赫兹频段与LEO高移动性叠加会使多普勒频移显著增大，需量化其对星地链路的影响严重程度，并判断这是否构成系统设计的关键挑战。

Method: 针对OFDM体制的亚太赫兹LEO星地链路，建立同时计入多普勒引起与非多普勒引起的主要信号损伤的联合评估框架；在此基础上对比三种应对多普勒频移的工程方案。

Result: 完全忽略多普勒效应会显著损害系统性能与可靠性；而采用少量低复杂度措施即可消除大部分负面影响，甚至在单次约5分钟过境期间仅做一次多普勒频移估计，便可在整个连接周期内保持高性能。

Conclusion: 多普勒频移在亚太赫兹LEO卫星通信中需加以考虑，但易于补偿，不应被视为此类新兴系统的关键设计瓶颈。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the impact of Doppler frequency shift on sub-terahertz low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite communications using OFDM transmission. To comprehensively evaluate the severity of the Doppler shift impact on sub-terahertz satellite-to-ground links, we first put together an evaluation framework that jointly accounts for major Doppler-induced and non-Doppler-induced signal impairments in such systems. We then compare and contrast three possible engineering approaches to deal with Doppler shift in such a communication system. Our study concludes that (i)~while just ignoring the Doppler effect in such high-frequency and high-mobility systems substantially compromises the performance and reliability, (ii)~most of the negative effects can be eliminated by implementing only a small set of low-complexity measures. We particularly show that even a single Doppler shift estimation over a 5-min-long satellite pass over the ground user location may be sufficient to ensure high performance throughout the entire connectivity period. Hence, while Doppler shift is an important effect to account for in sub-terahertz LEO satellite communications, it should not constitute a critical design challenge for these emerging systems.

</details>


### [71] [Polynomial-Time Minimax Prediction for Stationary Renewal Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01430)
*Xinyu Chen,Yiming Ma*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文提出一个多项式时间预测器，对任意未知有限均值间隔律的平稳二值更新过程达到 n^(-1/2) 阶的极小极大下一符号KL风险；核心贡献是基于经典类型均匀更新码与删失前缀边缘的精确动态规划，将计算降至 O(n^3) 算术工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平稳二值更新过程的极小极大下一符号KL风险已知为 n^(-1/2) 阶，但既有预测器在计算上不可行：需要枚举间隔直方图或二值补全。本文的动机是解决计算可行性问题，而非改进统计速率。

Method: 采用经典的类型均匀更新码（type-uniform renewal code），并设计精确动态规划计算删失（censored）前缀边缘概率，避免枚举间隔直方图与二值补全；结合既有的预测归约，得到风险上界 [log M_(n+1)+log(n+2)]/n（M_N 为更新类型数）。共享后缀更新将总计算量降为 O(n^3) 算术运算，并具多项式比特复杂度。

Result: 所提预测器在 O(n^3) 算术工作量与多项式比特复杂度下达到风险上界 [log M_(n+1)+log(n+2)]/n，从而保持 n^(-1/2) 的极小极大阶；且无需尾部分布、混合性、已知均值或支撑有界等假设。

Conclusion: 统计速率及其下界属已有结果，本文的推进在于计算层面：给出可多项式时间实现的预测器，使已知的极小极大最优阶在无额外结构假设下切实可达。

Abstract: The minimax next-symbol Kullback-Leibler risk for stationary binary renewal processes is known to have order n^(-1/2). We give a polynomial-time predictor attaining this order for an arbitrary unknown finite-mean interarrival law. The construction uses a classical type-uniform renewal code. Its contribution is an exact dynamic program for censored prefix marginals, avoiding enumeration of gap histograms or binary completions. Combined with an established prediction reduction, it gives risk at most [log M_(n+1)+log(n+2)]/n, where M_N counts renewal types. Shared suffix updates reduce the total computation to O(n^3) arithmetic work and polynomial bit complexity, without tail, mixing, known-mean, or bounded-support assumptions. The statistical rate and its lower bound are prior results; the proposed advance concerns computation.

</details>


### [72] [Secrecy Sum-Rate Maximization for RSMA-Enabled Pinching-Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01544)
*Code Kebe,Saeid Pakravan,Wessam Ajib*

TL;DR: 研究单波导pinching天线PASS下行RSMA系统中的保密和速率最大化，联合优化RSMA功率分配、安全公共速率分配与天线位置，采用交替优化求解并验证增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在外部窃听者的单波导pinching天线辅助RSMA下行系统中，如何通过联合设计RSMA资源分配与pinching天线位置来提升保密和速率，尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建以发射功率、每用户保密速率和PA布放为约束的SSR最大化非凸问题；采用交替优化框架，将RSMA资源分配用逐次凸近似（SCA）处理，PA位置优化采用保可行性的投影梯度上升法。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提设计在平均SSR上持续优于对比基准方案，体现了RSMA资源与PA位置联合优化对安全PASS传输的收益。

Conclusion: 联合优化RSMA资源分配与pinching天线位置可有效提升单波导PASS系统的保密和速率性能。

Abstract: This paper investigates secrecy sum-rate (SSR) maximization in a downlink rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA)-enabled single-waveguide pinching-antenna system (PASS) with an external eavesdropper. The RSMA power allocation, secure common-rate allocation, and pinching-antenna (PA) positions are jointly optimized subject to transmit-power, per-user secrecy-rate, and PA-placement constraints. To tackle the resulting nonconvex problem, an alternating-optimization framework is developed, combining successive convex approximation for RSMA resource allocation with feasibility-preserving projected gradient ascent for PA-position optimization. Numerical results show that the proposed design consistently outperforms the considered benchmarks in terms of average SSR, demonstrating the benefits of joint RSMA resource and PA-position optimization for secure PASS transmission.

</details>


### [73] [MDS Algebraic Geometry Codes of Square-Root Length on Hyperelliptic Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01546)
*Chuangqiang Hu,Guorui Ma*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 对每个固定亏格 g≥2 与 0<ρ<1，对所有充分大的奇平方 q，构造出参数为 [n, ⌊ρn⌋, n−⌊ρn⌋+1]_q、长度 n=√q+1+O_g(q^{1/4}) 的 MDS 代数几何码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDS 码达到 Singleton 界，是最优码；除平凡构造外，长 MDS 码的显式构造稀少。作者希望在亏格 ≥2 的曲线上获得带全 Riemann–Roch 空间、长度接近 q-ary MDS 上界 √q+1 的 MDS AG 码，并刻画其与 GRS 码的非等价性。

Method: 在亏格 g 的超椭圆曲线上取全 Riemann–Roch 空间构造 AG 码；选取有理点使长度 n=√q+1+O_g(q^{1/4})（子族精确为 n=√q+2gq^{1/4}+1），使维数达 ⌊ρn⌋ 且最小距离取得 Singleton 界 n−⌊ρn⌋+1，从而为 MDS；再通过与 GRS 码的参数/单项等价判据比较。

Result: 得到无穷族 MDS AG 码，参数 [n, ⌊ρn⌋, n−⌊ρn⌋+1]_q，q 为充分大奇平方；存在长度精确为 n=√q+2gq^{1/4}+1 的无穷子族；当 q 充分大时这些码与 GRS 码非单项等价。

Conclusion: 亏格≥2 的超椭圆曲线可系统产生接近最优长度、且本质区别于 GRS 的新型 MDS 码，为显式 MDS 码构造提供了新来源。

Abstract: For every fixed genus $g\geq2$ and every $0<ρ<1$, we construct maximum distance separable (MDS) algebraic geometry codes over $\F_q$ for all sufficiently large odd squares $q$. Their parameters are $\bigl[n,\lfloorρn\rfloor,n-\lfloorρn\rfloor+1\bigr]_q$ with $n=\sqrt q+1+O_g(q^{1/4})$. The codes use full Riemann--Roch spaces on genus-$g$ hyperelliptic curves. They are not monomially equivalent to generalized Reed--Solomon (GRS) codes when $q$ is sufficiently large. An infinite subfamily has exact length $n=\sqrt q+2gq^{1/4}+1$.

</details>


### [74] [Second-Order Asymptotics for the Scalar Gaussian Broadcast Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01583)
*Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文刻画了标量高斯广播信道容量域弯曲边界上的二阶编码区域；在平均与最大错误准则下均成立，且允许接收机噪声相关。该二阶区域在每个内点处为半空间。可达性由带随块长变化功率分配的球面叠加编码给出；主要创新在逆定理：利用Ornstein–Uhlenbeck熵耗散与高斯泛函不等式构造辅助观测，建立输出分布的条件相对熵界，再用强泛函表示引理选取确定性分组，进而完成信息谱逆定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯广播信道的一阶容量域在弯曲边界附近的可达速率对之间存在权衡，有限块长下的精确刻画（二阶编码区域）此前主要局限于退化消息集的多址信道等情形。本文旨在把Scarlett与作者在多址信道上的二阶半空间刻画推广到标量高斯广播信道，并覆盖平均与最大错误准则以及接收机噪声相关的一般情形。

Method: 可达性：块长相关的功率分配结合球面叠加编码。逆定理：借助Ornstein–Uhlenbeck熵耗散与高斯泛函不等式，经由加性高斯噪声信道构造辅助观测，推导以该观测为条件的信道输出分布之间的定量相对熵界；再用Li–El Gamal强泛函表示引理对其独立随机种子取实现，得到保留的接收机1消息集的确定性划分。用分量标签充当容量刻画中辅助随机变量的角色，同时提供二阶尺度所需的分布控制；据此构造辅助输出测度，使似然比产生所需抵消与正态近似，以评估信息谱逆定理。

Result: 在容量域弯曲边界的每个内点，二阶编码区域是一个半空间；该结论在平均错误与最大错误两种准则下均成立，并允许两接收机噪声之间存在相关性。这与Scarlett与作者关于退化消息集高斯多址信道的结论形式一致。

Conclusion: 标量高斯广播信道弯曲边界上的二阶行为可由半空间精确刻画，其关键在于通过辅助观测、相对熵界与确定性分组实现的信息谱逆定理；分量标签兼具辅助随机变量与二阶分布控制的双重作用。

Abstract: This paper characterizes the second-order coding region of the scalar Gaussian broadcast channel along the curved boundary of its capacity region. The characterization holds under both average and maximal error criteria and allows correlation between the receiver noises. At each interior point of this boundary, the second-order region is a halfspace, as in the work of Scarlett and the author on Gaussian multiple-access channels with degraded message sets. Achievability follows from spherical superposition coding with a blocklength-dependent power allocation. The main innovation lies in the converse. Using Ornstein--Uhlenbeck entropy dissipation and Gaussian functional inequalities, we construct an auxiliary observation through an additive Gaussian noise channel and establish quantitative relative entropy bounds for the channel output distributions conditioned on this observation. The strong functional representation lemma of Li and El Gamal then allows us to select a realization of its independent random seed to obtain a deterministic partition of the retained receiver-1 message set. The component label plays a role analogous to the auxiliary random variable in the capacity characterization, while providing the distributional control needed at the second-order scale. These properties guide the construction of auxiliary output measures whose likelihood ratios admit the cancellations and normal approximation needed to evaluate an information spectrum converse.

</details>


### [75] [6G Uplink Waveform and Rank Selection under Distributed Reception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01590)
*Yewen Cao,Fen Hou,Yulin Shao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究分布式接收6G上行链路中CP-OFDM与DFT-s-OFDM在rank 1与非相干rank 2下的联合选择问题，推导波形切换阈值与秩选择裕度，并分析端口增益与相关性形成的聚合空间分离对模式选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G上行链路扩展了波形与空间传输选项，分布式接收使各方案的相对优劣取决于聚合信道的空间与频率结构，因此需要明确在何种条件下选择何种波形与秩。

Method: 在统一UE功率约束与集中式线性MMSE接收下，对rank 1及非相干rank 2的CP-OFDM/DFT-s-OFDM进行联合选择建模；推导两种秩下的波形切换阈值与秩选择裕度，并刻画各AP端口增益与相关性如何构成聚合空间分离并影响模式选择。

Result: 给出了基于可用发射功率、DFT解扩损失与空间复用增益的四模式选择条件；在固定端口增益与发射功率下，在不降低任一子载波空间分离的前提下提升分离度会增大秩选择裕度，而其对rank 2内波形偏好的影响取决于改进的频率分布；数值结果显示不同AP部署可偏好不同波形与秩，甚至在偏好秩不变时发生波形切换。

Conclusion: 波形与秩的最优选择由聚合信道的空间分离程度及其频率分布决定，而非仅由接收天线预算或聚合信道增益决定，需联合考虑功率、解扩损失与空间复用增益进行模式选择。

Abstract: The development of 6G uplink is expanding waveform and spatial transmission options, while distributed reception makes their relative merits depend on the spatial and frequency structure of the aggregate channel. We study the joint selection of cyclic prefix orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing and discrete Fourier transform spread OFDM at rank 1 and noncoherent rank 2 under common user equipment power constraints and centralized linear minimum mean-square error reception. We derive waveform switching thresholds at both ranks and a rank selection margin, establishing conditions for selecting among the four modes based on available transmit power, DFT despreading loss, and spatial multiplexing gain. We then characterize how port gains and correlations at individual access points (APs) form aggregate spatial separation and influence mode selection. At fixed port gains and available transmit powers, improving spatial separation without reducing it on any subcarrier increases the rank selection margin, while its effect on the preferred waveform within rank 2 depends on the frequency distribution of the improvement. Numerical results show that different AP deployments can favor different waveforms and ranks, even with the same receive antenna budget and comparable aggregate channel gains, including waveform switches at a fixed preferred rank.

</details>


### [76] [Quantum Private Information Retrieval on Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03558)
*Mohamed Nomeir,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究量子设定下的图私有信息检索（graph PIR），针对森林图（不相交树的并）设计基于GHZ纠缠态的量子方案，达到速率3/N；对特例G=3K₂专门设计达到4/6速率的方案并给出匹配上界，从而确定其量子容量为4/6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中仅有少量工作涉及量子图PIR，已有方案效率有限；作者希望在图结构类问题上利用服务器间共享纠缠提升检索速率，并给出可证明最优的容量结果。

Method: 在服务器共享纠缠态、每轮每服务器仅发送一个qudit的约束下，以GHZ态作为初始纠缠资源，为森林图构造量子PIR方案；并针对特殊图G=3K₂设计专用方案，同时推导相应的上界。

Result: 森林图方案速率达到3/N；与文献中唯一的量子图PIR工作相比效率更高；对G=3K₂设计出速率4/6的方案，并给出匹配上界，证明其量子容量为4/6。

Conclusion: 基于GHZ纠缠的量子方案可提升图PIR的检索速率，且在G=3K₂情形下容量被精确确定为4/6，为量子图PIR的容量刻画提供了范例。

Abstract: We consider the problem of graph private information retrieval (PIR) in the quantum setting. We focus on a specific type of graphs, namely the forest graphs. A forest graph is a union of disjoint trees. We consider the quantum setting where all the servers share an entangled state. Each server is allowed to send only one qudit per retrieval round. We design a scheme for the forest graphs that achieves the rate of $\frac{3}{N}$. The scheme uses a GHZ state as the initial entangled state between the servers prior to the scheme initiation. We compare our scheme with the only work done in the literature that considered the quantum setting of graph PIR. We show that our scheme is more efficient in this setting. We derive the quantum capacity for the case of $G=3K_2$, which is equal to $4/6$. $G=3K_2$ is a special case of the forest graph. We design a scheme that is specialized for this graph, achieving a rate equal to $4/6$ and provide the corresponding matching upper bound, establishing it as the capacity.

</details>


### [77] [Worst-User Outage Probability Minimization in Active RIS-Aided Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01650)
*Donghwan Shin,Hyeongtaek Lee,Junil Choi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究主动式可重构智能表面（active RIS）辅助系统中的最差用户中断概率最小化问题，通过概率单纯形用户加权、softplus 光滑替代与熵型尾部风险聚合将不可微目标转化为可优化形式，并用基于历史信道样本的数据驱动随机优化（投影 Adam）联合优化波束成形器与反射系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标函数对未知信道分布不连续（中断概率形式），难以直接优化；且面向可靠性传输需兼顾最差用户与罕见但严重的中断事件。

Method: 依次进行问题重构：概率单纯形用户加权处理最差用户（max-min 型）结构；softplus 替代函数实现可微化；熵型尾部风险聚合刻画罕见严重中断；在此基础上采用依赖历史信道样本的数据驱动随机优化，以投影自适应矩估计（projected Adam）联合优化发射波束成形与主动 RIS 反射系数。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提技术在面向可靠性的主动 RIS 传输设计中具有优势。

Conclusion: 所提概率重构与数据驱动随机优化框架可有效求解未知信道分布下的最差用户中断概率最小化问题，为主动 RIS 可靠性传输设计提供可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, a worst-user outage probability minimization problem in active reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-aided systems is addressed. Since the resulting problem involves a discontinuous objective function over an unknown channel distribution, a sequence of reformulations is applied by integrating a probability-simplex user weighting, a softplus surrogate for differentiability, and entropic tail-risk aggregation for rare but severe outage events. Based on the reformulated problem, a data-driven stochastic optimization technique that relies on historical channel samples is adopted to jointly optimize the beamformer and reflection coefficients with projected adaptive moment estimation. Simulation results show advantages of the proposed technique for reliability-oriented active RIS transmission design.

</details>


### [78] [How Much Does Random Rotation Help GPTQ? Sharp Worst-Case Rate Gaps in the High-Rate Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01669)
*Runguo Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文将GPTQ解释为输入二阶矩Σ的Cholesky因子格上的Babai最近平面算法，证明Haar随机旋转后第k个Cholesky主元几乎必然收敛到Lyapunov指数的经典公式1/S_ν(-k/n)，并用其刻画旋转GPTQ与WaterSIC的最坏情形差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ordentlich与Polyanskiy指出GPTQ在高率熵编码下需比WaterSIC多约½log₂(AM/GM)比特，且WaterSIC距率失真极限不超过0.255比特；实践中随机旋转可弥补大部分差距，但旋转GPTQ与WaterSIC的最坏情形差距未知，作者旨在回答这一开放问题。

Method: 把GPTQ视为Cholesky因子格上的Babai最近平面算法；对Σ施加Haar随机旋转，证明在k≥δn一致、谱ν条件数有界的条件下，第k个主元几乎必然收敛到1/S_ν(-k/n)（Lyapunov指数理论中的经典公式）；据此在条件数κ的谱类上求最坏旋转差距，并分析离群特征值、非高斯权重（标准高率假设下的权重侧速率开销）等情形，另在Qwen3与Llama-3.1的96个Hessian上做数值验证。

Result: 条件数κ下最坏旋转差距为½log₂κ−log₂lnκ+½−1/ln2+o(1)比特，仅由两点谱达到，比无旋转最坏情形仅低½log₂lnκ+O(1)比特；旋转将η比例、幅度μ的离群特征值代价从约ημ降至η²μ/2（首阶），但无法在对数尺度消除谱展宽，非高斯权重还引入可精确刻画的权重侧速率开销；实测96个Hessian上旋转差距中位数0.04比特、最大0.36比特（同条件数最坏情形为2–5比特），极限公式误差在0.009比特以内。

Conclusion: Haar旋转下GPTQ主元的极限行为可由Lyapunov指数公式精确刻画，从而给出最坏旋转差距的闭式解；结论是旋转一般不能替代waterfilling（最优谱整形），能缓解离群特征值但无法消除对数尺度的谱展宽，在真实层上差距极小、理论与实测高度吻合。

Abstract: GPTQ is Babai's nearest-plane algorithm on the lattice of the Cholesky factor of the layer's input second moment $Σ$. In the high-rate, entropy-coded regime it needs $\tfrac12\log_2(\AM/\GM)$ of its Cholesky pivots more bits than WaterSIC, which is within $0.255$ bit of the rate-distortion limit for every $Σ$; random rotations close most of this gap in practice, and Ordentlich and Polyanskiy asked for the worst-case gap between rotated GPTQ and WaterSIC. We show that after a Haar rotation the $k$-th pivot converges almost surely, uniformly in $k\geδn$ and over spectra $ν$ of bounded condition number, to $1/S_ν(-k/n)$, a classical formula from the theory of Lyapunov exponents, and use it to answer the question. Over spectra with condition number $κ$ the worst rotated gap is $\tfrac12\log_2κ-\log_2\lnκ+\tfrac12-\tfrac1{\ln2}+o(1)$ bits, attained only by two-point spectra, just $\tfrac12\log_2\lnκ+O(1)$ bits below the worst case without rotation, so rotation cannot replace waterfilling in general. It nearly can on real layers: rotation cuts the cost of an $η$-fraction of outlier eigenvalues of size $μ$ from about $ημ$ to $η^2μ/2$ to leading order, but cannot remove spectral spread on a logarithmic scale; for non-Gaussian weights it also adds a weight-side rate cost, which we characterize exactly under a standard high-rate assumption. On $96$ Hessians of Qwen3 and Llama-3.1 the rotated gap has median $0.04$ bit and maximum $0.36$ bit, against $2$--$5$ bits in the worst case for the same condition numbers, and the limit matches it to within $0.009$ bit.

</details>


### [79] [Communication with Adversarial Users: Byzantine Models in Multi-User Information Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01678)
*Minglai Cai,Christian Deppe,Neha Sangwan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，从统一的信息论视角系统梳理多用户场景下含对抗（拜占庭）用户的通信问题，涵盖拜占庭多址信道的可靠通信、对抗者识别、分布式函数计算，以及向经典-量子信道的推广，并指出开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际通信环境中部分参与用户不可信，可能采取对抗性行为，传统假设所有用户诚实协作的多用户信息论模型不再适用，因此需要系统研究拜占庭通信模型。

Method: 采用信息论方法，综述并统一整理拜占庭多址信道（multiple access channel）相关研究，包括可靠通信的可达性与容量刻画、对抗者识别（adversary identification）、分布式函数计算；并将框架推广到经典-量子信道，比较经典与量子模型的本质差异。

Result: 总结了拜占庭多址信道在上述三类问题上的近期进展，揭示经典-量子模型之间的根本性差异，并归纳出通信、计算与安全交叉领域的若干关键开放问题与未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 拜占庭通信模型为研究不可信多用户通信提供了统一的信息论框架，其经典与量子版本存在本质区别，通信—计算—安全交叉方向仍有大量待解决的理论问题。

Abstract: We consider communication systems operating in environments where some participating users cannot be trusted. This motivates the study of Byzantine communication models, in which users may behave adversarially. In this survey, we provide a unified information-theoretic perspective on communication with adversarial users in multi-user settings. We review recent advances in Byzantine multiple access channels, including reliable communication, adversary identification, and distributed function computation. Furthermore, we discuss extensions to classical-quantum channels and highlight fundamental differences between classical and quantum models. Finally, we outline key open problems and future research directions at the intersection of communication, computation, and security.

</details>


### [80] [Instance-Specific Sharp Convergence Rate of the Add-$λ$ Estimator via Feature Space Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01828)
*Zong Shang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文在Feature Space Decomposition框架下，对离散分布Add-λ估计量的期望KL估计误差给出了非渐近的匹配上下界，适用于任意信号、任意维数、任意样本量与任意λ>0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Add-λ（加性平滑）估计量广泛用于离散分布估计，但其期望KL误差在有限样本、高维与小样本情形下的精确刻画尚不完整，尤其缺乏覆盖任意信号与任意λ的非渐近匹配界。

Method: 采用Feature Space Decomposition框架，将估计误差分解到特征空间上逐项分析，从而推导期望KL估计误差的上界与下界。

Result: 对任意信号、任意维数、任意样本量和任意λ>0，得到期望KL误差的非渐近上界与下界，且二者在常数意义下相互匹配。

Conclusion: Add-λ估计量的KL误差可由所给界精确刻画；Feature Space Decomposition是分析离散分布估计量KL误差的有效工具，结论具广泛适用性。

Abstract: Using the Feature Space Decomposition framework, we establish non-asymptotic matching upper and lower bounds for the expected Kullback-Leibler estimation error of the Add-$λ$ estimator of a discrete probability distribution, for any signal, any dimension, any sample size, and any $λ>0$.

</details>


### [81] [Wideband Microwave Linear Analog Computer (MiLAC) for Frequency-Flat and Frequency-Selective Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02109)
*Juan Vidal Alegría,Matteo Nerini,Bruno Clerckx*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究基于可调LC谐振腔的宽带微波线性模拟计算机（MiLAC），刻画参考频率最优MISO预编码与电容配置向量间的映射，推导其剩余自由度并用于调控频率响应，提出频平与多频两种技术，首次证明实际MiLAC可在不同频率执行独立运算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MiLAC能以可重构阻抗网络在微波域高效计算，无需昂贵数字处理即可获得大规模多天线增益，但其宽带行为（支撑高速率传输所需）尚不清楚，且阻抗元件频率响应复杂，需特定实现来刻画。

Method: 采用电感与变容管构成的LC谐振腔实现MiLAC，在单频上合成近任意无源无损阻抗矩阵；刻画参考频率下最优MISO预编码与电容向量之间的映射，推导固定该最优预编码后剩余的自由度，并提出两种利用方式：用于频平MiLAC处理的闭式方法，以及优化非参考频率处理的多频数值方法。

Result: 频平方法在仿真的频平毫米波场景中性能令人满意，接近数字处理；多频方法能适应仿真sub-6GHz场景的频率选择性，相较频平数字处理获得增益。

Conclusion: 首次证明实际MiLAC实现可被配置为在不同频率执行独立计算，为宽带微波域模拟处理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Microwave linear analog computer (MiLAC) has been proposed as an analog processing architecture to perform efficient computation in the microwave domain. Consisting of reconfigurable impedance networks, a MiLAC can extract many of the gains of large multi-antenna systems without costly digital processing, e.g., achieving capacity in narrowband multiple-input single-output (MISO). However, the behavior of wideband MiLAC, required to support high data-rate transmissions, is widely unknown. The impedance components of MiLACs have non-trivial frequency-dependent response, requiring specific implementations to characterize it. In this work, we consider MiLAC implementations based on LC resonators with tunable capacitance, which allow synthesizing near-arbitrary passive lossless impedance matrices at individual frequencies using inductors and varactors. We characterize the mapping between optimal MISO precoding at a reference frequency, and the vector of capacitances configuring the MiLAC. We derive the degrees of freedom (DoFs) remaining after fixing such optimum precoding at the reference frequency, and show that these DoFs can be exploited to tune the frequency response of the MiLAC. We propose two techniques to exploit these DoFs: a closed-form approach for frequency-flat MiLAC processing, and a numerical approach for multi-frequency MiLAC processing, optimizing processing at frequencies other than the reference one. The frequency-flat approach shows satisfactory performance in a simulated frequency-flat millimeter-wave scenario, approaching the performance of digital processing. The multi-frequency approach can adapt to the frequency-selectivity of a simulated sub-6GHz scenario, attaining gains over frequency-flat digital processing. This constitutes the first demonstration that realistic MiLAC implementations can be configured to perform independent computations at different frequencies.

</details>


### [82] [Concatenation Locality under Successive Adjacent Transpositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02192)
*Adelin Ataba,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文研究纠正至多 t 次连续相邻换位的分组拼接码，证明校正性可由 2t+1 块的有限测试判定，并给出达到最优每块冗余的二进制显式构造及线性时间编解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次相邻换位码难以支持任意长度拼接下的块独立编解码；作者希望用块拼接方式实现块级编码解码，并厘清"在任意拼接长度下均能纠错"的判定条件与冗余代价。

Method: 构造固定长度 b 的词集 P，码字为 m 个 P 中词的拼接（长度 mb）。证明纠错性由 2t+1 块的单一有限测试认证，且该测试在 q 元字母表上当且仅当 C(q,2)≥2t 时紧。对二进制及 b≥2t+1 给出显式构造，编码器解码器仅依赖块参数并在各拼接长度复用；并分析块内信息恢复范围。

Result: 二进制构造总冗余至多 mt·log2 b + O_t(m)，匹配块集最优每块冗余 t·log2 b + O_t(1)；而长度 mb 的最优 t-纠错码总冗余为 t·log2(mb)+O_t(1)。编解码在固定 b,t 下关于 mb 线性时间，且可由前 jb 个接收符号恢复第 j 个源块；并证明第 j 块可在 jb 之后 2tb 个符号内被确定，且渐近率达到1的二进制块集在最坏情况下可达此界；此外任意普通纠错码中常数比例码字的任意拼接仍保持纠错性。

Conclusion: 块拼接码以总冗余随 m 线性增长为代价，换取块独立编解码与统一编解码器，并以 2t+1 块的有限测试刻画全部拼接长度下的纠错性；其冗余与恢复延迟界在相应模型下均为最优或可达，同时普通码向拼接稳健码的转化存在常数比例的结构性保证。

Abstract: We study codes correcting up to $t\geq1$ successive adjacent transpositions, using block concatenation to support blockwise encoding and decoding. A fixed set $P$ of words of length $b$ over a $q$-symbol alphabet generates, for every $m$, the length-$mb$ code of all concatenations of $m$ words from $P$. For our explicit constructions, the prepared encoder and decoder depend only on the block parameters and are reused at every concatenation length. We first prove that correction at every concatenation length is certified by a single finite test at $2t+1$ blocks, and this test is tight over a $q$-symbol alphabet exactly when $\binom q2\geq2t$. For binary words and $b\geq2t+1$, we give an explicit construction with total redundancy at most $mt\log_2 b+O_t(m)$, matching the optimal per-block redundancy $t\log_2 b+O_t(1)$ among block sets correcting at every concatenation length. Among all binary $t$-correcting codes of length $mb$, the optimal total redundancy is $t\log_2(mb)+O_t(1)$. Using all concatenations of one block set makes our total redundancy grow linearly in $m$ and buys independent block encoding, with one encoder and decoder at every length. The construction encodes and decodes in time linear in $mb$ for fixed $b,t$, and recovers the $j$-th source block from the first $jb$ received symbols. We also prove that, whenever the length-$mb$ code corrects $t$ transpositions, the $j$-th source block is determined within $2tb$ received symbols beyond position $jb$, and that explicit binary block sets with rates tending to one as $b$ grows with $t$ fixed attain this bound in the worst case. Finally, we prove that every ordinary correcting code contains a constant fraction of its words whose arbitrary concatenations retain correction, for fixed $q,t$.

</details>


### [83] [Achievability of CSS codes performance limits over Pauli channels under separate ML decoding with bounded graphical complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02339)
*Gulnihal Mercanoglu,Achilleas Anastasopoulos,S. Sandeep Pradhan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文构造了在无记忆Pauli信道下、最大似然译码时速率逼近CSS容量的CSS码集，同时保持稀疏、有界度的因子图表示，从而分离了校验矩阵密度与图复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容量逼近的量子纠错存在根本矛盾：所需校验矩阵日益稠密，而实用译码与校验子提取偏好稀疏、有界度结构。调和这一矛盾是量子码设计的重要开放问题。

Method: 先研究LDPC与LDGM分量码组合的构造并数值分析平均重量分布；进而提出基于级联LDPC/LDGM码与广义不规则重复累积(GIRA)码的第二构造，推导求和码集平均重量分布的界，并用Miller-Burshtein框架证明两个CSS译码分量的同时可靠性。

Result: 所得码集在分离的比特与相位纠错下，速率可任意接近CSS哈希界，且保持有界图复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作为迭代译码器与间接式校验子提取架构提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Capacity-approaching quantum error correction faces a fundamental tension: the required parity-check matrices need to become increasingly dense, while practical decoding and syndrome extraction favor sparse, bounded-degree structures. Reconciling these competing requirements remains an important open problem in quantum code design. We construct Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) code ensembles that approach the CSS rate over memoryless Pauli channels under maximum-likelihood decoding while retaining sparse, bounded-degree graphical representations. The construction separates parity-check density from graphical complexity: although capacity-approaching components may require dense parity-check matrices, they are represented by sparse concatenated factor graphs. We first study a construction combining low-density parity-check (LDPC) and low-density generator-matrix (LDGM) component codes and numerically analyze the average weight distributions of the resulting codes. Motivated by these results, we introduce a second construction based on concatenated LDPC and LDGM codes and generalized irregular repeat-accumulate (GIRA) codes. We derive bounds on the average weight distribution of the resulting sum ensemble and use the Miller-Burshtein framework to prove simultaneous reliability of the two CSS decoding components. Consequently, the proposed ensemble achieves rates arbitrarily close to the CSS hashing bound under separate bit- and phase-error correction, while maintaining bounded graphical complexity, thus providing a foundation for future iterative decoders and indirect syndrome-extraction architectures.

</details>


### [84] [A Sharp Mutual Information Bound for Binary Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02392)
*Qianmian Guo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了以分布依赖的KL收缩系数为参量的二元随机变量互信息的最优上界，解决了Anantharam-Gohari-Kamath-Nair二元对猜想，并刻画了取等条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元对猜想要求给出互信息与KL收缩系数之间关系的精确（sharp）上界；此前只有不紧的界，取等结构未知。

Method: 利用熵曲线的支撑切线（supporting tangents），在保持收缩系数不变的前提下同时变动输入分布与信道；并结合张量化（tensorization）与数据处理不等式推广到相关观测序列的二元输出。

Result: 得到关于KL收缩系数的二元互信息最优上界；取等当且仅当两变量独立或构成双对称二元信源（DSBS）；对有限字母独立同分布观测对，界仅依赖单对分布而与观测数无关。

Conclusion: 二元对猜想成立且为sharp，取等情形完全刻画；该界可经张化与数据处理推广到多观测情形，具有与观测数无关的稳定性。

Abstract: We prove a sharp upper bound on the mutual information between two binary random variables in terms of their distribution-dependent KL contraction coefficient. This resolves the binary-pair conjecture of Anantharam, Gohari, Kamath, and Nair. Equality holds if and only if the variables are independent or form a doubly symmetric binary source. The proof uses supporting tangents of an entropy curve to vary the input distribution and the channel while preserving the contraction coefficient. Together with tensorization and data processing, the result also bounds the mutual information between binary outputs obtained separately from two correlated observation sequences. When the observation pairs are independent copies of a finite-alphabet pair, this bound depends only on the law of one pair and not on the number of observations.

</details>


### [85] [Inverse Design of Finite-State Channels by Dynamic Measure Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02400)
*Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文将生物学的优化视角引入信道编码，提出"带记忆通信的逆问题"：构造一个有限状态信道，使其给定信令行为恰好支持达到容量的码。通过动态测度匹配，把有向信息逆定理转化为对信道动力学的约束，进而提出"动态惊奇"概念并给出相关容量、零差错反馈与强逆结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者受"生物学可视为优化问题的求解"启发，反向思考通信问题：不是给定信道求最优码，而是在给定信令行为（受约束图刻画）下构造信道，使该行为成为容量可达的最优通信策略；目标是理解记忆、约束与信息代价之间的结构性关系。

Method: 1) 建立带记忆通信的逆问题形式化；2) 引入动态测度匹配，把有向信息逆定理转化为信道动力学的匹配不等式；3) 定义动态惊奇（物理/测试输出概率的对数比，含未来信令机会的延续修正），并对其条件均值给出界；4) 对固定结构、测试律与势函数，证明在凸物理约束下的行综合问题为凸；5) 对任意有限不可约正熵约束图构造信道律，使非期望输入仍可用且行为可区分但付出严格信息惩罚；6) 提出含噪不应期（refractory）信道族，用Gibbs选择、精确轨迹似然恒等式与残差-散度分解进行分析。

Result: 构造出满足约束的信道律并给出严格信息惩罚；含噪不应期族存在精确容量平台，其后出现Shannon容量与零差错反馈容量不相等的区间；显式零差错码在图上构造及平台上达到容量；Gibbs选择使惩罚最大且动态惊奇等于其条件均值，导出精确轨迹似然恒等式、含提前请求的有限块译码界和指数强逆；给出行为残差与输出律散度的精确间隙分解；严格平台内对未用动力学获得正判别指数需付出正速率损失，而稀疏探测可在渐近容量下一致估计；化学信道示例显示提前刺激可改变未来响应性而不改变即时输出律。

Conclusion: 论文给出了一套"由行为反向构造信道"的统一框架：动态惊奇刻画了约束行为与容量可达性之间的张力，容量平台、反馈零差错差异、强逆与间隙分解均可在此框架内精确刻画，并暗示记忆型信道（如化学系统）中即时输出与未来响应性可解耦。

Abstract: Inspired by the optimization approach to biology, we formulate an inverse problem for communication with memory: construct a finite-state channel for which a given signaling behavior supports capacity-achieving codes. Dynamic measure matching is introduced to turn a directed-information converse into conditions on the channel dynamics. These conditions lead to a new notion of dynamic surprise: the log ratio of physical to test output probabilities with a continuation correction for future signaling opportunities. The matching inequalities bound its conditional mean. For fixed architecture, test law, and potential, channel row synthesis under convex physical restrictions is convex. For every signaling constraint represented by a finite irreducible constrained graph of positive entropy, we construct a channel law under which undesired inputs remain available and behaviorally distinct while incurring a strict information penalty. A noisy refractory family has an exact capacity plateau, followed by an interval in which Shannon and zero-error feedback capacities differ. Explicit zero-error codes attain capacity in the graph construction and on the plateau. In this family, a Gibbs choice maximizes the penalty and makes dynamic surprise equal its conditional mean on every transition, yielding an exact trajectory-wise likelihood identity, a finite-block bound on successful decoding with premature requests, and an exponential strong converse. An exact gap decomposition into action residuals and output-law divergence constrains every reliable capacity-approaching code. Within the strict plateau, positive discrimination exponents for unused dynamics require a positive rate loss, while sparse probing permits consistent estimation at asymptotic capacity. A chemical channel illustrates how premature stimulation can alter future responsiveness while leaving the immediate output law unchanged.

</details>


### [86] [Finding optimal measurement sequences for broken or constrained measuring devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02470)
*Peter N. Loxley*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究"受限/损坏测量设备"下的最优测量序列问题，用信息论与动态规划证明贪心策略次优，指出最优策略使测量结果非等概率并使序列"解压"其源，并给出基于最优二叉决策树的高效算法及复杂度上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量设备因疲劳、损坏或故障而无法完美工作，当无法维修或更换设备时，需要从"损坏的"设备中获取尽可能优的测量。因此需要一个在剩余可用测量上优化测量序列的序贯决策框架。

Method: 将测量过程建模为序贯决策过程，以信息论为优化准则，在剩余可用测量的约束下优化测量序列；用动态规划分析简单约束类下贪心序列的次优性；推导与最优二叉决策树相关的替代算法，并给出该任务复杂度的上界与下界。

Result: 对一类简单的测量约束，动态规划表明贪心测量序列总是次优的；贪心序列通常导致等概率测量结果，而最优策略倾向于选择使"触发约束条件的测量结果"概率很低的测量，从而产生非等概率结果；该策略生成的测量序列可"解压"其源，即期望测量结果数严格大于其编码的比特数，这构成受限测量的关键性质；动态规划效率低下，替代算法更高效。

Conclusion: 受限测量下贪心不可取，最优策略通过压低触发约束的结果概率来获得更好的信息效率，并表现出"解压"特性；所提出的类最优二叉决策树算法在给定复杂度界内为损坏或受限测量设备寻找最优测量提供了高效手段。

Abstract: Measuring devices do not always function perfectly due to fatigue, damage, or malfunction. When device repair or replacement is not possible, it becomes necessary to seek optimal measurements from a broken device. We show this is possible via a sequential decision making process where we optimize measurement sequences over the remaining available measurements using information theory. With dynamic programming, we show greedy measurement sequences are always suboptimal for a simple class of measurement constraints. Greedy measurement sequences generally lead to equiprobable measurement outcomes, while the optimal policy is found to select each measurement in such a way as to assign a low probability to any measurement outcome that triggers a constraint condition; leading to measurement outcomes that are not equiprobable. A measurement sequence generated under this policy is shown to decompress its source: that is, the expected number of measurement outcomes is strictly greater than the number of bits it encodes, defining a key property of constrained measurements. Dynamic programming is inefficient for finding optimal measurement sequences, so we develop an alternative algorithm related to optimal binary decision trees. By deriving upper and lower complexity bounds for the task, we propose this algorithm as an efficient means to find optimal measurements for a broken or constrained measuring device.

</details>


### [87] [Information Complexity of Inner Product and Matrix Multiplication via Shannon-to-Min-Entropy Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02684)
*Xiaohong Cai,Mingyang Zhu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文在独立均匀输入模型下刻画了有限域上矩阵乘法的信息复杂度，确定到通用加性常数（<9.544比特）：内积情形解决了Braverman等人的公开问题，矩阵乘法情形给出精确表达式，并证明了一个离散群卷积的Shannon到min-熵定理作为核心工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵乘法的通信与信息复杂度是通信复杂度理论的核心问题；此前仅有内积的上界，且信息复杂度对错误概率ε的最优依赖关系（Braverman, Garg, Pankratov, Weinstein提出的公开问题）未知，矩阵乘法的一般情形亦缺乏紧的信息下界。

Method: 证明新的离散群卷积Shannon到min-熵定理：对独立随机变量分布Q_1,…,Q_k（支撑大小最大为s），若k≥⌈⌊log_2 s⌋·ln2⌉，则有序乘积的min-熵至少为各分布平均Shannon熵减c_0比特；该定理适用于任意离散群（含无限与非交换），并由此导出内积与矩阵乘法的信息下界。

Result: 内积情形：输入于F_2^m、错误概率≤ε∈[0,1/2]时，输出在Bob处的信息复杂度介于(1-2ε)m-c_0与(1-2ε)m之间，c_0<4.772；矩阵乘法：X∈F_q^{t×m}、Y∈F_q^{s×m}（t≤s），计算XY^T的信息复杂度为(1-q^{ts}ε/(q^{ts}-1))^+·mt·log_2 q，加性误差<9.544比特，与维度、域大小、错误无关；零错误时恰为mt·log_2 q。该对数阈值的主导系数最优。

Conclusion: 在独立均匀输入下，矩阵乘法的信息复杂度被完全确定到通用加性常数；内积情形回答了关于信息复杂度对错误概率最优依赖的公开问题，零错误时达到精确值；所提出的离散群Shannon到min-熵定理是更通用的独立工具。

Abstract: We determine the information complexity of matrix multiplication over finite fields under independent uniform inputs, up to a universal additive constant. The binary inner-product case resolves the open question of Braverman, Garg, Pankratov, and Weinstein on the optimal dependence of information complexity on the error probability. For inputs in $\mathbb F_2^m$ and error at most $\varepsilon\in[0,1/2]$, the information complexity with output at Bob lies between $(1-2\varepsilon)m-c_0$ and $(1-2\varepsilon)m$, where $c_0<4.772$ is a universal constant.
  For independent uniform matrices $X\in\mathbb F_q^{t\times m}$ and $Y\in\mathbb F_q^{s\times m}$, where $t\le s$, computing $XY^{\mathsf T}$ with output at Bob and error at most $\varepsilon\in[0,1]$ has information complexity $(1-q^{ts}\varepsilon/(q^{ts}-1))^+mt\log_2q$, up to an additive gap of less than $9.544$ bits. This gap is independent of the matrix dimensions, field size, and error. At zero error, the information complexity is exactly $mt\log_2q$.
  The information lower bounds for both inner product and matrix multiplication follow from a new Shannon-to-min-entropy theorem for convolution on discrete groups. Let $Q_1,\ldots,Q_k$ be the finitely supported distributions of independent random variables taking values in a discrete group, and let $s$ be the largest support size. We show that if $k\ge\lceil\lfloor\log_2s\rfloor\ln2\rceil$, then the min-entropy of their ordered product is at least the average Shannon entropy of the $Q_i$ minus $c_0$ bits. This holds for arbitrary discrete groups, including infinite and noncommutative groups, even when the input distributions differ. The leading coefficient of the logarithmic threshold is optimal.

</details>


### [88] [Design and Analysis of Pinching-Antenna Systems With Radially Deployed Waveguides (X-PASS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02687)
*Wei Jiang,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出X-PASS：由中心hub径向馈电多根波导臂、每臂一根pinching天线，支持臂选择/聚合/复用三种模式，分析覆盖性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PASS系统受单根波导限制，天线只能沿一条线排布，实现区域良好覆盖需多根波导或带独立馈电的多段波导，结构复杂、成本高。

Method: 设计hub径向部署波导臂的X-PASS架构，每臂仅一根pinching天线，通过臂选择、聚合、复用驱动；对hub建模并计入插入损耗；推导三种模式统一的信噪比表达式；闭式推导聚合优于选择的半径门限。

Result: 数值结果验证理论分析；在相同发射功率下比较X-PASS与其他布局的覆盖性能，并给出聚合模式占优的半径范围。

Conclusion: X-PASS以单hub径向多臂结构突破单波导一维限制，为区域覆盖提供更简洁的PASS方案。

Abstract: Pinching-antenna systems (PASS) reconfigure the wireless channel by positioning dielectric antennas along a waveguide. A single waveguide, however, confines its antennas to one line, so that good coverage of an area has relied on several waveguides or waveguide segments with separate feeds. This paper proposes X-PASS, in which a hub feeds radially deployed waveguide arms, one pinching antenna on each, and drives them by arm selection, aggregation or multiplexing. It models the hub with its insertion losses, derives a unified signal-to-noise ratio expression for these three operating modes, and derives in closed form a radius inside which aggregation outperforms selection. Numerical results validate the analysis and compare the coverage of X-PASS with that of the other layouts at equal transmit power.

</details>


### [89] [Movable Antenna-Assisted Secret Key Generation over Temporally Correlated Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02761)
*Zhiyu Huang,Chen Sun,Guyue Li,Guanghui Wen,Xianbin Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文研究时间相关信道下的可移动天线（MA）辅助物理层密钥生成（PLKG），以一步条件互信息为密钥信息度量，联合优化天线位置与逐时隙波束成形，在高时间相关系数下相比固定天线获得约51%速率提升，联合优化再提升106%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢衰落环境下信道强时间相关性导致密钥新鲜随机性不足、密钥生成速率低；MA通过天线位置重构引入空间自由度，为解决该瓶颈提供新途径。

Method: 用一阶Gauss-Markov过程刻画多径增益的时间相关性，分析全序列互信息并给出随有效SNR增大而趋于零的有限时域近似误差界；推导一步条件互信息的闭式表达作为可处理的密钥信息度量；在发射功率与最小间距约束下构建非凸联合优化问题，采用交替优化算法，结合精确主特征向量波束成形解与基于SCA的可行投影梯度位置更新。

Result: 仿真表明MA方案优于固定位置天线基线；高时间相关系数下，位置优化较固定天线速率提升约51%，位置与波束成形联合优化较仅位置优化再提升106%，且强时间相关下增益更显著。

Conclusion: MA的空间自由度可有效缓解时间相关信道对PLKG速率的限制，位置与波束成形的联合设计在强时间相关场景中收益尤为突出。

Abstract: Physical-layer key generation (PLKG) exploits reciprocal channel dynamics to generate symmetric keys, addressing the key distribution problem in wireless communications.
