However, strong temporal correlation in slow-fading environments limits fresh randomness and results in low key generation rates. Movable antennas (MAs) introduce spatial degrees of freedom through antenna repositioning, providing a new approach to addressing the limitation. This paper investigates MA-assisted PLKG over temporally correlated channels. A first-order Gauss-Markov process is utilized to characterize the temporal correlation of multipath gains. We characterize the full-sequence mutual information (MI) over temporally correlated channels. For fixed antenna positions and beamforming, we establish a finite-horizon approximation error bound that vanishes as both effective signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs) increase. We derive a closed-form expression for the one-step conditional MI and adopt it as a tractable key-information metric. A nonconvex problem is formulated to jointly optimize antenna positions and per-slot beamforming under transmit-power and minimum-spacing constraints. We develop an alternating optimization algorithm, combining an exact principal-eigenvector beamforming solution with feasible SCA-based projected gradient position updates. Simulation results demonstrate that the MA-assisted scheme outperforms the fixed-position antenna baseline. At a high temporal correlation coefficient, position optimization achieves approximately $51\%$ higher rates than fixed antennas. Joint position and beamforming optimization provides a further $106\%$ gain over position optimization alone, demonstrating more pronounced gains under strong temporal correlation.

</details>


### [90] [Localization MSE Analysis for Cooperative OFDM-ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02783)
*Kaitao Meng,Kawon Han,Christos Masouros*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向合作式通感一体化（ISAC）定位的接收机特定MSE框架，基于随机OFDM载荷，建模匹配滤波（MF）与倒数滤波（RF）两类接收处理的局部峰值扰动与模糊峰误捕获，并通过多站融合误差近似位置MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ISAC定位性能分析未针对接收机处理方案（MF/RF）在随机载荷条件下的差异化误差进行建模，忽略符号能量波动、频域扩展及模糊峰误捕获对延迟估计与定位精度的影响。

Method: 对延迟充分分离的散射体，量化符号能量波动引起的频域扩展与干扰对MF局部延迟误差的影响；分析RF去除符号相关加权但放大低能量符号噪声的机制；构建以载荷为条件的联合高斯延迟分布，导出峰值选择概率，并与非线性多站融合误差结合近似位置MSE，同时计入网络几何。

Result: 数值结果表明估计误差具有接收机依赖性，且频率相关星座分配可带来性能增益。

Conclusion: 该框架可在随机载荷下统一刻画MF与RF的延迟估计误差及多站融合定位精度，为接收机方案与星座分配的联合设计提供依据。

Abstract: This paper develops a receiver-specific mean-squared-error (MSE) framework for cooperative integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) localization using random orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) payloads. It captures local peak perturbations and incorrect ambiguity-peak acquisition for two classical receiver processing schemes: matched filtering (MF) and reciprocal filtering (RF). For scatterers well separated in delay, we quantify how frequency spread and interference induced by symbol-energy fluctuations affect local delay errors under MF. RF removes symbol-dependent weighting but amplifies noise for low-energy symbols. Payload-conditioned joint Gaussian delay profiles yield peak-selection probabilities, which combine with nonlinear multistatic fusion errors to approximate position MSE while accounting for network geometry. Numerical results illustrate receiver-dependent estimation errors and the benefits of frequency-dependent constellation allocation.

</details>


### [91] [Customizing Wireless Channels via Intelligent Reflecting Surfaces: Feasibility, Fundamental Limits, and Constructive Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02894)
*Xiyuan Liu,Qingqing Wu,Guangji Chen,Yuxuan Chen,Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究仅相位IRS的信道定制可达性问题，建立与点对点MIMO信号MSE同构的等价问题刻画单奇异坐标增益极限，并通过主贡献区(PCA)重叠分析与平行阵列Fresnel模型给出阵列尺寸充分/必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IRS研究多直接优化相移以服务通信指标，忽视"所设定的IRS反射信道在物理上是否可实现"这一前提，缺乏对仅相位约束下信道定制可达性极限的刻画。

Method: 在三类逐级放宽的定制准则下建模；利用定制MSE与点对点MIMO信号MSE数学同构构造对偶问题，刻画个体奇异坐标增益极限及达到幅度匹配上界的条件；针对多流相位冲突分析PCA重叠，在并行均匀线阵与聚焦矢量构造下推导PCA不相交的阵列尺寸充分条件；并由两段传播链奇异值与平行阵列Fresnel模型推导必要条件。

Result: 给出单奇异坐标增益极限与上界可达条件；得到基于BS–IRS阵列尺寸乘积的充分与必要条件；数值结果显示联合选择右奇异向量与IRS相移可降低平均定制MSE，但无法精确实现超出传播谱极限的增益。

Conclusion: 结果为仅相位IRS信道定制提供设计指导，并揭示其受传播谱极限约束的本质限制；需指出结论依赖并行阵列与Fresnel近似等特定假设。

Abstract: By adjusting their reflection coefficients, intelligent reflecting surfaces (IRSs) can reshape the channels between a base station (BS) and user equipment (UE). Existing studies mainly optimize IRS phase-shifts for communication objectives, leaving the physical attainability of prescribed IRS-reflected channels largely unexplored. This paper investigates channel customization with a phase-only IRS under three progressively relaxed criteria. Observing that the customization mean-squared error (MSE) has the same mathematical form as the signal MSE of a point-to-point multiple-input multiple-output system, we construct a counterpart problem to characterize individual singular-coordinate gain limits and the condition for attaining the amplitude-matching upper bound. To address multi-stream phase conflicts, we analyze principal contribution area (PCA) overlap and derive sufficient array-size conditions for disjoint PCAs under a specified focused-vector construction with parallel uniform linear arrays. We also derive necessary conditions from the two propagation links' singular values and an array-size restriction under the parallel-array Fresnel model. Both the restriction and the construction involve the BS--IRS array-size product. Numerical results show that jointly selecting right singular vectors and IRS phase-shifts reduces mean customization MSE, whereas gains exceeding propagation spectral limits cannot be realized exactly. These results provide design guidance and highlight spectral limits of phase-only IRS channel customization.

</details>


### [92] [Fluid Antenna Array-Enabled Near-Field ISAC: Joint Port Position Optimization and Transmit Beamforming Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03000)
*Wangqi Shi,Zhentian Zhang,Hao Jiang,Zaichen Zhang,Kin-Fai Tong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出平面流体天线阵列（FAA）辅助的近场ISAC框架，用于多用户通信与目标感知；推导了目标距离、天顶角、方位角与径向速度联合估计的FIM与CRB，并基于CRB联合优化FAA端口位置与发射波束成形，采用两阶段交替优化（SDR+离散搜索与连续细化）求解非凸问题，仿真验证其在合理计算代价下取得良好感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体天线系统（FAS）表明在密集阵列中激活单个端口即可获得媲美数十根独立天线的空间分集，显著提升通信可靠性与效率；流体天线阵列（FAA）将该概念扩展到多端口配置，避免复杂多维空间相关性，并在有限孔径与端口预算下提供几何灵活性。这种灵活性可支撑环境自适应阵列几何，用于ISAC中利用近场距离-角度信息，但在通信与部署约束下改善多参数目标估计仍具挑战。

Method: 构建平面FAA辅助的近场ISAC框架，同时服务多用户通信与目标感知；推导距离、天顶角、方位角、径向速度联合估计的FIM及CRB；以CRB为目标，在通信SINR、发射功率与物理部署约束下联合优化FAA端口位置与发射波束成形；针对非凸问题设计两阶段交替优化算法：波束成形用半定松弛（SDR）求解，端口位置先离散搜索再连续细化。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提算法在保持合理计算复杂度的前提下取得了良好的感知性能。

Conclusion: 基于CRB的FAA端口位置与波束成形联合设计可有效提升近场ISAC的多参数目标估计精度，两阶段交替优化在性能与计算成本间取得良好折中。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FASs) have shown that activating a single port from a densely packed antenna array can yield spatial diversity comparable to that of tens of independent antennas, a property that has substantially improved communication reliability and efficiency. The fluid antenna array (FAA) extends this concept to multi-port configurations while avoiding complex multi-dimensional spatial correlation, and offers significant geometrical flexibility under a finite aperture and a limited port budget. These attributes motivate environment-adaptive array geometry for sensing in integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems. Although this flexibility enables adaptive array geometries that exploit near-field distance-angle information, improving multi-parameter target estimation under communication and deployment constraints remains challenging. In this paper, we propose a planar FAA-assisted near-field ISAC framework for multiuser communication and target sensing. Specifically, we derive the Fisher information matrix (FIM) and the corresponding Cramér-Rao bound (CRB) for the joint estimation of target range, zenith angle, azimuth angle, and radial velocity. Based on the derived CRB, we jointly optimize the FAA port positions and transmit beamforming to improve sensing accuracy while satisfying communication signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR), transmit power, and physical deployment constraints. To solve the resulting non-convex problem, we develop a two-stage alternating optimization algorithm, in which transmit beamforming is optimized through semidefinite relaxation (SDR), while FAA port positions are successively optimized through discrete search and continuous refinement. Simulation results indicate that the proposed algorithm achieves favorable sensing performance while maintaining a reasonable computational cost.

</details>


### [93] [Hotplug Coded Caching: Uncoded versus Row-Wise Coded Placements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03045)
*Daniela Tuninetti*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究hotplug编码缓存模型中负载与K是否无关的开放问题，证明在非编码放置下,YMA+MAN方案最优且存在严格负载惩罚；在逐行放置(RWP)下,可将经典方案嵌入Reed-Solomon码消除负载惩罚(但放置依赖K),并给出RWP逆定理确定GV与YMA的最优参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: hotplug编码缓存中活跃用户数A未知,已知最优的各类情形下最优tradeoff仅依赖A而不依赖K,该性质是否普遍成立是公开问题;同时需厘清放置策略(MAN vs 行内)对是否出现离线用户负载惩罚的影响。

Method: (1)非编码放置下,将离线用户的需求分配给活跃用户,采用YMA传输配MAN放置,证明其最优性;(2)在RWP(每个缓存仅是其自身行向量函数)下,用Reed-Solomon码将任意经典方案嵌入,构造对每个A≤K具有A用户经典tradeoff的hotplug方案;(3)推导RWP逆定理,对GV方案与YMA的最优性区间进行刻画。

Result: (i)非编码放置下YMA+MAN为最优:中间存储容量区间存在严格负载惩罚,且MAN放置对任意活跃用户数均最优;(ii)RS嵌入方案对每个A≤K达到A用户经典tradeoff,无负载惩罚但放置依赖K;RWP逆定理给出:(a)2≤N≤A时,GV在AM≤N/(N-1)为RWP最优,解决N=2的RWP tradeoff;(b)YMA在AM=N时RWP最优;(c)N=A=3时GV在M≤1为RWP最优。

Conclusion: 负载对K的依赖并非普遍存在,而取决于放置约束:非编码放置下离线用户必然带来负载惩罚;RWP下通过RS嵌入可完全消除惩罚但需牺牲放置对K的独立性。所证逆定理表明上述方案的RS嵌入在嵌入式RWP方案中为最优,并解决了N=2等情形的RWP tradeoff。

Abstract: In the hotplug coded caching model, $K$ users fill their caches of size $M$ as a function of $N$ files, but only an unknown subset of $A\leq K$ users is active in the delivery phase. In all regimes where the optimal hotplug tradeoff is known, it depends only on $A$, not on $K$; whether this holds in general is open. We show that: (i) Under uncoded placement, YMA delivery with MAN placement, where offline users are assigned the demands of active users, is optimal. Hence, offline users cause a strict load penalty at intermediate memory sizes, while the MAN placement is optimal for every number of active users; (ii) Under row-wise placement (RWP), where each cache is a function of the user's own row of each file, every classical scheme can be embedded in a Reed-Solomon code. The resulting hotplug scheme has the tradeoff of the classical model with $A$ users, for every $A\leq K$: there is no load penalty, but the placement depends on $K$. We then derive a converse for RWP, which shows that: (a) for $2\leq N\leq A$, the scheme of Gomez-Vilardebo (GV) is RWP optimal for $AM\leq N/(N-1)$, which settles the RWP tradeoff for $N=2$; (b) YMA is RWP optimal at $AM=N$; and (c) for $N=A=3$, GV is RWP optimal for $M\leq 1$. Hence, the Reed-Solomon embeddings of these schemes are optimal among embedded RWP schemes.

</details>


### [94] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for BD-RIS-Assisted Multi-UAV RSMA Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03047)
*Aamer Mohamed Huroon,Ali A. Nasir,Daniel B. da Costa*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文研究BD-RIS辅助的多无人机RSMA下行网络，以DDPG联合优化轨迹、波束成形、BD-RIS散射矩阵与关联，获得与SAC相当的总速率并大幅降低时延，增益主要来自RSMA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机信道快速变化且功率受限，传统对角RIS难以充分挖掘空间自由度；BD-RIS允许多单元耦合，且需要高效处理混合整数非凸优化。

Method: 构建混合BD-RIS辅助多UAV RSMA下行模型（覆盖内UAV使用直连+BD-RIS链路，其余仅直连）；以网络总速率最大化为目标联合优化UAV轨迹、RSMA公共速率分配、波束成形、BD-RIS散射矩阵和UAV-BD-RIS关联；采用DDPG，并设计约束保持动作层，以闭式生成互易无损BD-RIS响应，将二进制关联嵌入环境转移。

Result: 在物理一致性信道模型（BD-RIS附近直连被阻塞）下，三个训练种子平均结果：总速率与SAC相当、高于TD3、PPO和DQN；超过固定轨迹逐时隙交替优化方案，且每时隙运行时间约为后者的1/65；增益主要源于RSMA，BD-RIS额外增益有限。

Conclusion: DDPG结合约束保持动作层可高效求解BD-RIS辅助多UAV RSMA联合优化问题，实现高谱效与低时延；BD-RIS相对RSMA的边际收益较小，需进一步审视其在该场景的部署价值。

Abstract: Beyond-diagonal reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (BD-RISs) extend conventional diagonal RISs by allowing controllable coupling among reflecting elements. This flexibility is attractive for aerial networks, where unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) face rapidly varying channels and strict power limits. In this paper, we study a hybrid BD-RIS-assisted multi-UAV rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) downlink, in which UAVs within favourable BD-RIS coverage use both direct and BD-RIS-aided links, whereas the remaining UAVs are served through direct links only. We formulate a network sum-rate maximization problem that jointly optimizes UAV trajectories, RSMA common-rate allocation, beamforming, BD-RIS scattering matrices, and UAV-BD-RIS association. The resulting mixed-integer non-convex problem is solved by using deep deterministic policy gradient (DDPG) algorithm, equipped with a constraint-preserving action layer that generates reciprocal and lossless BD-RIS responses in closed form and handles the binary association inside the environment transition. Simulation results, averaged over three training seeds under a physically consistent channel model with blocked direct links near the BD-RISs, show that the proposed design attains a sum rate comparable to SAC and higher than twin delayed deep deterministic policy gradient ( TD3), proximal policy optimization (PPO), and deep Q-network (DQN), exceeds per-slot alternating optimization with fixed trajectories at about 1/65 of its per-slot runtime, and benefits mainly from RSMA, while the additional BD-RIS gain is modest.

</details>


### [95] [Reed-Muller Codes are Optimal Binary Linear 3-Query LCCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03145)
*Oliver Janzer,Peter Manohar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文证明了任意常纠正半径的线性3查询局部可纠码满足 n ≥ 2^{Ω(√k)}，改进了先前 2^{Ω(√(k/log k))} 的下界，并说明由 Reed-Muller 码构造的 3-LCC 在下界指数上是常数因子最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前最好的下界为 [AG24] 的 2^{Ω(√(k/log k))}，与 Reed-Muller 构造达到的 n = 2^{O(√k)} 之间仍存在 log k 的指数差距。本文旨在消除该差距，确定线性3-LCC长度的真实指数级下界。

Method: 沿用 [KM24a]（并经 [Yan24, KM24b] 精化）的"链式导出方程"结合 Kikuchi 图的方法。核心是证明某个边染色 Kikuchi 图具有唯一的颜色环（即某颜色恰好使用一次的环）。证明手段为：利用随机游走混合论证控制每个顶点处颜色的分布，并基于超图"展平"为图后的边扩展性提出一种新的超图分解。

Result: 证明任意线性3查询局部可纠码（常纠正半径）长度满足 n ≥ 2^{Ω(√k)}；相较 [AG24] 的 2^{Ω(√(k/log k))} 有指数级改进，并使 Reed-Muller 类构造 n = 2^{O(√k)} 在指数意义下达到常数因子最优。

Conclusion: 线性3-LCC 的最优长度随维数 k 呈 2^{Θ(√k)} 增长，folklore 的 Reed-Muller 构造本质最优；技术贡献在于颜色环唯一性的证明，借助随机游走混合与基于展平图边扩展性的超图分解。

Abstract: We prove that any linear $3$-query locally correctable code $C \colon \mathbb{F}_2^k \to \mathbb{F}_2^n$ with constant correction radius must have length $n \geq 2^{Ω(\sqrt{k})}$. This improves on the prior best lower bound of $2^{Ω(\sqrt{k/\log k})}$, due to [AG24], and shows that the folklore construction of linear $3$-LCCs from Reed-Muller codes, which achieve length $n = 2^{O(\sqrt{k})}$, is optimal up to constant factors in the exponent.
  Our proof builds on the approach of [KM24a], further refined in [Yan24,KM24b], that uses "chain derived equations" in combination with Kikuchi graphs. The crux of our argument is to show that a certain edge-coloured Kikuchi graph has a unique colour cycle: a cycle in which some colour is used exactly once. We do this by controlling the distribution of colours at each vertex in the graph using a random walk mixing argument and a novel hypergraph decomposition based on edge expansion of the hypergraph's "flattening" into a graph.

</details>


### [96] [The Covering Radius of the Reed--Muller Code \texorpdfstring{$\RM(3,8)$}{RM(3,8)} is 56](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03186)
*Zhihang Zhao,Yansheng Wu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文证明八变量三阶Reed–Muller码的覆盖半径为56，从而精确确定八变量次数为8的布尔函数三阶非线性度的最大值（55）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定Reed–Muller码 RM(3,8) 的覆盖半径等价于确定八变量三次函数的最大三阶非线性度，这是编码理论与密码学中非线性度刻画的关键基准问题；已有四阶相对半径结果可直接给出下界，但上界证明长期缺失。

Method: 按输入奇偶重量做矩控制的切片分离：偶数侧用二次标号星上的传输不变量与单点限制的相容性构造相容三次修正；奇数侧利用三阶矩的对称性导出协方差秩障碍，秩二例外化为四次函数加二平面三点，零二阶矩情形化为五次函数的指定点扰动；随后用带标记导数恒等式与完整限制码覆盖证明此类扰动的三阶非线性度不超过55，并用完整有限证书核验局部断言。

Result: RM(3,8) 覆盖半径为56；任意八变量精确次数8函数的三阶非线性度不超过55，结合已发表的四阶相对半径得到匹配下界，故最大值为55。

Conclusion: 该工作以解析结构归约加有限证书验证的混合范式，闭合了 RM(3,8) 覆盖半径问题，确认八变量三阶非线性度上界为55，并为高维覆盖半径的结构化证明提供了可复用的切片与矩方法框架。

Abstract: We prove that the covering radius of the third-order Reed--Muller code in eight variables is fifty-six. The proof separates even- and odd-weight inputs by moment-controlled slicing. On the even side, a transport invariant on quadratic-label stars and consistency of singleton restrictions give a compatible cubic correction. On the odd side, symmetry of the third moment yields a covariance-rank obstruction. Its rank-two exception is a quartic plus three points of a two-plane; the zero-second-moment case is a prescribed-point perturbation of a quintic. We prove that every such point perturbation has third-order nonlinearity at most fifty-five by a marked derivative identity and complete restriction-code covers. Consequently the maximum among exact-degree-eight functions is fifty-five. The structural reductions are proved analytically, and complete finite certificates verify the explicitly stated local covering and compatibility assertions. The matching lower bound follows from the published quartic relative radius.

</details>


### [97] [Harnessing Mutual Coupling in Cell-Free mMIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03193)
*Erind Tufa,Antonio A. D'Amico,Luca Sanguinetti*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文利用多端口通信理论研究无蜂窝大规模MIMO中紧密排列天线阵列的互耦效应，分析全匹配网络下互耦对上下行SE的影响，指出在小阵列分布式AP场景中互耦可带来增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无蜂窝大规模MIMO的AP采用小尺寸固定形状ULA，天线间距可能很近，互耦不可避免；而集中式大规模阵列中全匹配网络规模不可控，因此需评估分布式小阵列中互耦与匹配策略对SE的实际影响。

Method: 基于多端口通信理论建模含互耦的L个AP、每AP含N单元ULA的无蜂窝大规模MIMO系统，覆盖多种阻抗匹配策略，对比固定L增大N与固定总天线数M=NL增大N两种部署方式，计算上下行SE。

Result: 全匹配网络下，当单个AP服务多个UE（以用户为中心无蜂窝网络的典型工况）时互耦本身变为有益；且全匹配条件下天线致密化可提升SE，或在无性能损失的前提下减少AP数量。

Conclusion: 分布式小阵列中全匹配网络规模可控，因此无蜂窝网络是充分利用互耦效应的天然场景。

Abstract: This letter investigates a cell-free massive MIMO system with $L$ access points (APs), each employing a small fixed-form-factor, uniform linear array of $N$ potentially closely spaced antennas. Utilizing multiport communication theory, we analyze the impact of mutual coupling on uplink and downlink spectral efficiency (SE) under various impedance-matching strategies. We compare two deployment regimes: ($i$) increasing $N$ while $L$ is kept fixed, and ($ii$) increasing $N$ while fixing the total antenna count $M = NL$. Numerical results show that, with a full matching network, mutual coupling itself becomes beneficial when an AP serves several user equipments, which is the typical operating regime of user-centric cell-free networks. Furthermore, with full matching, antenna densification can enhance SE or allow for a reduction in the number of APs without performance loss. Since full matching networks remain of manageable size in the small arrays of distributed APs, unlike in large centralized arrays, cell-free networks offer a natural setting for exploiting mutual coupling.

</details>


### [98] [Good rational functions up to degree six](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03368)
*Anuvrat Jaindungarwal,Divyesh Vaghasiya*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该文在特征2下完成了次数不超过6的"好有理函数"分类，并证明全分裂位点个数服从线性递推序列，从而可精确计算由此构造的最优LRC的长度与维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Tamo-Barg构造依赖好多项式，但其Galois群过大，限制了全分裂有理位点数量与码长；虽有工作推广到好有理函数以获得最小Galois群，但低次情形缺乏系统分类，且对有限扩域上全分裂位点数的估计仅停留在渐近的Hasse-Weil界。

Method: 将问题嵌入代数函数域理论，对特征2、次数≤6的好有理函数进行完全分类，显式确定其最小算术Galois群与几何Galois群；并证明扩域上全分裂位点总数满足线性递推序列（LRS）。

Result: 给出次数≤6的好有理函数的完整分类及对应最小算术/几何Galois群；证明全分裂位点数服从LRS，提供计算相应最优LRC精确长度与维数的高效方法，并在诸多情形下给出超越好多项式可得参数的新码参数。

Conclusion: 好有理函数框架在特征2低次情形下被完整刻画，LRS方法替代渐近估计实现参数的精确计算，显著扩展了最优LRC的参数范围。

Abstract: Locally recoverable codes (LRCs) are fundamental to the architecture of modern distributed storage systems. The Tamo-Barg construction of optimal LRCs was revolutionized by the introduction of good polynomials, which group evaluation points into local recovery sets. However, polynomial constructions are constrained by their inherently large Galois groups, which limit the number of totally split rational places. Recent advances have generalized this paradigm to good rational functions, which leverage the geometry of the projective line to achieve minimal Galois groups and subsequently codes with greater length. In this paper, we complete the classification of good rational functions of degree up to $6$ in characteristic $2$. By embedding the problem into algebraic function field theory, we explicitly determine the minimal arithmetic and geometric Galois groups for these low degrees. Furthermore, we move beyond asymptotic Hasse-Weil estimates by proving that the exact number of totally split places over extension fields obeys a linear recurring sequence (LRS). This gives an efficient method for computing the exact lengths and dimensions of the corresponding optimal LRCs and, in many cases, yields parameters beyond those obtainable from good polynomials.

</details>


### [99] [Non-Coherent Over-the-Air Distributed Consensus: Algorithm Design and Power Control Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03378)
*Yuhang Deng,Zheng Chen,Erik G. Larsson*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出容纳行随机混合的NCOTA共识框架，给出DPGD-RS算法及其均方收敛性与误差上界，并基于此设计谱重塑功率控制（SR-PC）方案加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCOTA聚合无需瞬时CSI与相位对齐，具可扩展、通信高效优势，但持续聚合误差使名义分布式平均协议难以实现精确平均共识，且通常依赖双随机（保平均）混合矩阵假设。

Method: 基于平均共识的分布式优化建模，提出带行随机混合的分散投影梯度下降算法（DPGD-RS）；推导均方共识误差闭式上界，刻画聚合不确定性、网络连通性与混合不平衡的影响；据此设计SR-PC，联合优化发射功率与接收缩放因子，在聚合精度与网络混合间取得平衡。

Result: 理论上证明DPGD-RS以均方意义收敛到初始状态的精确平均；给出共识误差闭式上界；数值实验验证收敛性保证，并表明SR-PC相比全功率发射与谱半径最小化基准显著加速收敛。

Conclusion: 通过放宽双随机假设、以误差上界指导功率控制，可在NCOTA框架下实现精确平均共识并显著提升收敛速度。

Abstract: Non-coherent over-the-air (NCOTA) aggregation offers a scalable and communication-efficient approach to distributed average consensus in wireless networks, without requiring instantaneous channel state information (CSI) or phase alignment. However, persistent aggregation errors pose fundamental challenges in achieving exact average consensus under nominal distributed averaging protocols. In this paper, we develop an NCOTA consensus framework that accommodates row-stochastic mixing, without requiring the conventional average-preservation property of doubly stochastic mixing matrices. Building on a distributed optimization formulation of average consensus, we propose a decentralized projected gradient descent with row-stochastic mixing (DPGD-RS) algorithm and establish its mean-square convergence to the exact average of the agents' initial states. We further derive a closed-form upper bound on the mean-square consensus error, characterizing the effects of aggregation uncertainty, network connectivity, and mixing imbalance. Guided by this analysis, we propose a spectral-reshaping power control (SR-PC) scheme that jointly optimizes transmit powers and receive-scaling factors to accelerate convergence by balancing aggregation accuracy and network mixing. Numerical results validate the theoretical convergence guarantees and demonstrate that the proposed power control scheme significantly accelerates convergence compared with full-power transmission and spectral-radius-minimization benchmarks.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond GNSS: Leveraging LEO Satellite Signals of Opportunity for Doppler Positioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03440)
*Md. Ali Hasan,M. Humayun Kabir,Sangmin Han,Wonjae Shin,H. Vincent Poor*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文综述LEO卫星多普勒频移定位的原理、挑战与关键技术，并提出基于Sionna的射线追踪框架SionnaNTN-RT用于可复现的性能评估，仿真表明其在城市与开阔天空场景均具高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LEO星座虽提供全球连接，但其高速运动带来的多普勒效应给通信造成困难；同时，在GNSS拒止环境下传统卫星导航失效，而多数LEO卫星缺乏专用导航信号，需借助机会信号（SOPs）与公开TLE星历实现定位，但星历误差会限制精度。因此有必要系统梳理LEO多普勒定位的基础原理与挑战。

Method: 文章采用综述+框架构建的方式：系统阐述LEO多普勒频移定位的基本原理、挑战与使能技术，对比其相对传统非多普勒方法的优势；并基于Sionna构建面向非地面网络（NTN）的射线追踪框架SionnaNTN-RT，实现多普勒定位的端到端仿真，以超越随机建模的真实可复现评估。

Result: 仿真结果表明，多普勒定位在城市与开阔天空环境下均可达到较高的定位精度，验证了其作为下一代定位系统的潜力。

Conclusion: LEO多普勒定位在GNSS拒止场景下具有显著优势与应用前景；SionnaNTN-RT为真实、可复现的性能评估提供了有效工具；未来需进一步研究方向（如缓解星历误差等）以提升实用性与精度。

Abstract: Large constellations of low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites provide ubiquitous global connectivity; yet, their rapid motion introduces significant Doppler shift effects. While challenging for communication, these effects can be leveraged for precise user terminal (UT) positioning, particularly in GNSS-denied environments where traditional satellite-based navigation struggles. Unlike GNSS, most LEO satellites lack dedicated navigation signals, motivating signals-of-opportunity (SOPs) approaches that repurpose existing LEO signals and use publicly available two-line element (TLE) files for orbital information, although ephemeris errors can limit accuracy. This article explores the fundamental principles, challenges, and enabling technologies for LEO-based Doppler shift positioning, highlighting its advantages over conventional non-Doppler methods, and discussing future research directions. Furthermore, to facilitate realistic and reproducible performance evaluation beyond stochastic modeling, we propose SionnaNTN-RT, a ray tracing framework for non-terrestrial network (NTN) built upon Sionna, enabling end-to-end simulation of Doppler-based positioning. Simulation results demonstrate that Doppler-based positioning can achieve high positioning accuracy in both urban and open-sky environments, highlighting its promise for next-generation positioning systems.

</details>


### [101] [An information-geometric formulation of distributed computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03545)
*Alberto Liardi,George Blackburne,Pedro A. M. Mediano*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出 ΦID^{IG}——基于信息几何的信息动力学理论，可将多输入多输出运算完整分解为可解释的计算模式，并给出系统的完整信息画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画跨多个输入输出的、不可归约为单个组件贡献的真正"集体计算"仍是开放问题，缺乏能够定量描述不可约集体现象并给出完整信息剖面的理论工具。

Method: 构建几何化的信息动力学理论 ΦID^{IG}，基于系统底层概率分布的几何结构，将多输入多输出运算分解为若干可解释的计算模式；先在具有已知基准真值的离散系统与高斯系统中验证该框架，再用于合成与真实复杂系统。

Result: 在离散/高斯系统中成功识别并量化不同计算模式；混沌映射中不同普适类对应特征性计算模式；100 余种哺乳动物脑解剖数据中，人类新皮层呈现向更分布式计算的演化偏移；金融市场中集体计算可恢复市场板块的模块化组织。

Conclusion: 复杂系统可借助由其概率分布几何导出的多维"计算画像"进行定量研究，该框架为跨学科刻画集体计算提供了统一、可解释的分析工具。

Abstract: Understanding how complex systems perform computations, i.e. how they store, transmit, and transform information across their components, is a major goal across the natural sciences. However, characterising genuinely collective computations across multiple inputs and outputs that cannot be reduced to contributions from individual components remains an open problem. In this work, we introduce $Φ\text{ID}^{IG}$, a geometric theory of information dynamics that captures irreducible collective phenomena and enables a complete decomposition of multi-input multi-output operations into interpretable computational modes, providing a complete informational profile of a system. We first validate our framework to identify and quantify distinct computational modes in discrete and Gaussian systems with known ground truth. We then apply it to uncover the computational architecture of complex systems across synthetic and real-world scenarios, including chaotic maps, where distinct universality classes are associated with characteristic modes of computation; brain anatomy of over 100 mammalian species, revealing an evolutionary shift towards more distributed computation in the human neocortex; and financial markets, where collective computations recover the modular organisation of market sectors. Overall, our results show how complex systems can be quantitatively investigated through multidimensional computational portraits derived from the geometry of their underlying probability distributions.

</details>


### [102] [Uniform Sampling of Linear Codes with Prescribed Hull Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03578)
*Keita Ishizuka*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 给出欧几里得、埃尔米特与辛内积下、任意有限域上具有指定 hull 维数的线性码的精确均匀采样器，期望代价为 O(k²N + k log q) 次域运算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指定 hull 维数的线性码在编码理论（如纠缠辅助量子码、自正交码、LCD 码）中具有重要作用，但此前缺乏对任意指定 hull 维数的精确均匀采样算法，仅少数特例（如奇特征域上的欧几里得型）已知。

Method: 针对欧几里得、埃尔米特和辛三种内积，在任意特征的有限域 F_q 或 F_{q²} 上构造精确均匀采样器；对维数 k、环境维数 N 的码，采样一次期望需 O(k²N + k log q) 次域运算。

Result: 除奇特征域上的欧几里得型外，这是首个对任意指定 hull 维数的均匀采样器；采样器精确均匀，且期望复杂度为 O(k²N + k log q) 次域运算。

Conclusion: 统一覆盖三种内积与所有特征的有限域，实现了任意指定 hull 维数线性码的精确均匀、高效采样，填补了该方向此前的方法空白。

Abstract: We give exactly uniform samplers for linear codes with prescribed hull dimension, for the Euclidean, Hermitian, and symplectic inner products and over finite fields of every characteristic. Except for the Euclidean form over fields of odd characteristic, these are the first uniform samplers for arbitrary prescribed hull dimension. Each sample costs an expected $O(k^2 N + k \log q)$ field operations for codes of dimension $k$ in a space of dimension $N$ over $\mathbb{F}_q$ or $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [mmWave UE Positioning Under Blockage: Limits for ISAC-Assisted Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00453)
*Seyed Bagher Hashemi Natanzi,Bo Tang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该文研究服务用户的无线单元能否仅凭上行探测参考信号实现足够精度的定位，以支撑毫米波主动切换；推导含同步与阵列校准误差的定位误差界，并在射线追踪街谷场景中比较两种估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波链路在行人遮挡前可通过主动切换得以保全，但其前提是网络知道用户位置，因此需要评估上行探测参考信号定位精度是否满足这一需求。

Method: 推导包含同步误差与阵列校准误差的定位误差下界，分别在已知数字孪生反射路径与未知反射路径两种情形下给出界；在射线追踪街谷场景的留出场景上测试两种估计器（简单主径估计器与地图辅助多径跟踪器）。

Result: 三点主要发现：(1) 双基站视距条件下，限制误差界的是阵列校准而非带宽；(2) 遮挡会使纯视距误差界急剧恶化，而已知反射路径可将地图辅助误差界维持在较低水平；(3) 简单主径估计器在遮挡附近误差急剧增大，使主动切换劣于3GPP基线；地图辅助多径跟踪器将中位误差由12 cm降至3.7 cm，并在15 dB及以上链路余量下消除该显著损失，但仍未超越基线。

Conclusion: 上行探测参考信号定位在利用已知反射路径与多径跟踪时可显著改善遮挡下的定位精度，但仍不足以全面优于3GPP基线；阵列校准是视距场景下的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Proactive handover can save a millimeter-wave (mmWave) link before a passing body blocks it, but only if the network knows where the user is. We ask whether the radio units that serve a user can localize it accurately enough from its uplink sounding reference signals. We derive position error bounds that include synchronization and array calibration errors, with and without reflected paths known from a digital twin, and test two estimators in a ray-traced street canyon on held-out scenarios. Three findings stand out. First, with line of sight to both radio units, array calibration, not bandwidth, limits the bound. Second, blockage degrades the line-of-sight-only bound sharply, whereas known reflections keep the map-aided bound low. Third, a simple dominant-path estimator fails where it matters most: its errors grow sharply around blockage, and proactive handover becomes worse than the 3GPP baseline. A map-aided multipath tracker cuts the median error from 12 to 3.7 cm and removes this significant penalty at link margins of 15 dB and above, but does not yet beat the baseline.

</details>


### [104] [From Confounding to Counterfactuals: A Cross-Layer Causal Model for RAN Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00635)
*Artin Elhamirad,Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Ali Tajer*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出跨PHY/MAC/网络层的分层结构因果模型(H-SCM)，分离小区负载混杂与UE异质性响应，并用反事实goodput进行PRB调度，仿真中反事实调度总小区goodput相对损失<0.5%，优于神经网络基线的2.0–2.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动RAN控制依赖控制动作与KPI的关联学习，但共享无线环境造成混杂，使关联高估分配效果并将资源误配给无法获益的UE；同时UE响应的异质性使校正后的平均效应仍无法识别哪些UE能获益。现有因果推断方法未建模从分配到结果的跨层机制。

Method: 构建覆盖PHY、MAC与网络层的分层结构因果模型(H-SCM)，将小区负载混杂与UE特定响应异质性分离；形式化以每UE反事实goodput（而非观测条件均值）为选择依据的PRB反事实调度问题；求解流水线包括约束式因果发现、结构方程拟合以及abduction-action-prediction三步反事实推断。

Result: 朴素观测估计将全PRB响应高估73.3%；校正小区负载混杂后相对误差降至9.8%；对留出干预的预测决定系数R²=0.988；反事实调度的总小区goodput平均相对损失<0.5%，神经网络基线为2.0–2.9%。

Conclusion: 显式建模跨层结构因果机制并采用反事实准则，可有效消除混杂偏差与异质性误导，实现接近真值最优的RAN资源分配。

Abstract: Data-driven control in radio access networks (RANs) selects resource allocations using associations between control actions and key performance indicators (KPIs) learned from network telemetry. Because the shared radio environment influences both the scheduler's decisions and the resulting performance, a bias known as confounding, such associations overstate the effect of an allocation and misdirect resources toward user equipment (UEs) that cannot benefit. Moreover, individual UEs respond heterogeneously to the same decision, so even a corrected average effect does not identify which UEs gain from additional resources. While causal inference has recently been applied to RANs, existing approaches do not model the cross-layer mechanisms that connect an allocation to its outcome. In this paper, a hierarchical structural causal model (H-SCM) spanning the PHY, MAC, and network layers is proposed that separates cell-load confounding from UE-specific response heterogeneity. In particular, a counterfactual scheduling problem is formulated in which physical resource block (PRB) allocations are selected using per-UE counterfactual goodput rather than observational conditional averages. This problem is solved via a pipeline combining constrained causal discovery, structural-equation fitting, and abduction-action-prediction. Simulations demonstrate that naive observational estimation overstates the full-PRB response by 73.3%, whereas adjustment for cell-load confounding reduces the relative error to 9.8%. Held-out interventions are predicted with a coefficient of determination of 0.988. Counterfactual scheduling achieves a mean relative loss in total cell goodput below 0.5% against the ground-truth optimum, compared with 2.0-2.9% for a neural-network baseline.

</details>


### [105] [Joint Trajectory, Sensing, and Computation Co-Design for UAV-Assisted Federated Edge Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00790)
*Kaichen Liu,Yueling Che,Sheng Luo,Jianhua Tang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究面向计算密集型目标检测模型训练的无人机辅助联邦边缘视觉系统，提出四阶段联合轨迹、感知与计算协同设计，并开发双层交替优化算法以最小化电池约束下的期望训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将训练数据视为静态，忽视了动态视觉感知与大规模就地计算负担之间的紧密耦合，导致在无人机辅助联邦边缘视觉场景下训练效率受限。

Method: 提出四阶段联合轨迹、感知与计算协同设计框架；采用均匀感知概率将联邦收敛需求转化为无人机移动性约束以缓解数据异构；开发双层交替优化（AO）算法自适应确定本地数据工作量，在所需训练轮次与就地计算时延之间取得最优平衡。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在模型收敛性和精度上均优于基线方案。

Conclusion: 通过将动态感知与就地计算纳入统一协同设计，并结合收敛需求到移动性约束的转化与双层AO优化，可有效降低电池受限条件下的期望训练时间并提升联邦训练性能。

Abstract: This paper investigates UAV-assisted federated edge vision for training computationally heavy object detection models. Existing studies treat training data as static, ignoring the tight coupling between dynamic visual sensing and massive in place computational burdens. To minimize the expected training time under battery constraints, we propose a four-phase joint trajectory, sensing, and computation co-design. To mitigate data heterogeneity, we use a uniform sensing probability to translate federated convergence requirements into UAV mobility constraints. Furthermore, a two-layer alternating optimization (AO) algorithm is developed to adaptively determine the local data workload, optimally balancing the required training rounds against in-place computation delays. Simulations validate the superiority of our approach in model convergence and accuracy over baselines.

</details>


### [106] [Energy-Efficient Geographic Multicast Routing in Cognitive Wireless Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01212)
*Farzan Heidari,Farzaneh Heidari*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出面向认知无线传感网的两种地理组播路由协议CCast与PCast，以局部信息实现节能组播转发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线传感网中能耗是关键设计约束；认知无线电可机会式接入授权频谱，组播路由能让多个目的节点共享转发路径，因此需在认知传感网场景下设计兼顾频谱选择与能耗的组播路由方案。

Method: 提出CCast与PCast两种地理组播路由协议：CCast将组播树构建与时延导向的频谱选择度量相结合；PCast采用归一化链路功率代价选路，并可规避主用户占用低频段的区域。两者均仅依赖局部邻居信息与目的节点坐标，无需完整网络拓扑图。以地理组播路由（GMP）与多单播路由为对照进行仿真。

Result: 仿真中CCast与PCast相对GMP分别降低能耗约21%与26%，代价是单目的路径变长、投递时延升高。

Conclusion: 基于局部信息的地理组播路由在认知无线传感网中可显著节能，能耗与路径长度、时延之间存在权衡；CCast侧重时延导向的频谱选择，PCast侧重功率代价与主用户规避，二者适用场景不同。

Abstract: Energy consumption is a major design concern in wireless sensor networks. Cognitive radio provides opportunistic access to licensed spectrum, while multicast routing allows several destinations to share forwarding paths. In this paper, we propose two geographic multicast routing protocols for cognitive wireless sensor networks, CCast and PCast. CCast combines multicast-tree construction with a latency-oriented spectrum-selection metric. PCast uses a normalized link-power cost to select routes and can avoid regions where primary users occupy lower-frequency bands. Both protocols use local neighbor information and destination coordinates, without requiring a complete network-topology map. Simulations compare the proposed protocols with geographic multicast routing (GMP) and multiple unicast routing. In simulations, CCast and PCast reduce energy consumption by 21\% and 26\% relative to GMP, at the cost of longer per-destination routes and higher delivery delay.

</details>


### [107] [Vehicle and UAV Detection and Tracking with LTE Downlink Reference Signals: A Ray-Traced Evaluation on a Mapped Suburban Site with Licensed Base-Station Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01459)
*Arun K. Gurung,Satha K. Sathananthan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究LTE下行被动感知（UE侧接收、不改动网络），基于射线追踪信道对band-3的CRS参考信号"感知臂"进行仿真，并与同频SRS上行感知臂对比，随后在实测场地（OSM建筑轮廓+授权站址记录）中评估多接收机的覆盖与跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动感知可利用现有蜂窝下行信号，在终端侧完成探测而无需改动网络，具备低成本、易部署的潜力；但下行CRS作为感知波形相对上行SRS的性能与覆盖能力尚缺乏定量评估。

Method: 在射线追踪信道上仿真LTE band-3小区特定参考信号（CRS）感知臂；在合成街道峡谷场景中，与同频段、同目标、同评分器的上行探测参考信号（SRS）感知臂进行对比，以漏检概率为指标；再在由OSM建筑footprint与授权站址记录构建的实测场地中，沿街道布设9个接收机位置，考察对车辆的覆盖比例、不同轨迹/目标高度分离下的跟踪，以及8个接收机以135 m间距单照射源覆盖1140 m街道的可行性。

Result: 合成街道峡谷中，CRS感知臂漏检概率0.667，SRS感知臂为0.772；实测场地中9个沿街接收机对车辆的跟踪覆盖率为29.9%–61.7%；283 m环路可将目标高度与轨迹分离于1.6–30 m；137 m环路在建成街道上实现对车辆与15 m空中目标的全小区跟踪；8个间距135 m的接收机可用单一照射源覆盖1140 m街道。

Conclusion: 结果表明下行被动感知在终端侧不改动网络的前提下具备可行性与一定覆盖能力。未来工作包括扩展到多照射源、改变接收机配置，并通过空口实测加以验证。

Abstract: Passive sensing on the cellular downlink places the receiver at the user equipment and changes nothing in the network. We report simulation results for an LTE band-3 cell-specific reference signal arm on a ray-traced channel. On a synthetic street canyon the arm is compared with an uplink sounding reference signal arm on the same band, target and scorer: missed-detection probabilities of 0.667 and 0.772. On a surveyed site built from OpenStreetMap footprints and the licensed site record, nine receiver positions along the street track the vehicle at coverage between 29.9 % and 61.7 %; a 283 m lap separates target height from trajectory at 1.6 to 30 m; a 137 m lap on the built-up street tracks a vehicle and a 15 m airborne target on every cell, and 8 receivers at 135 m spacing carry one illuminator's coverage over 1140 m of the street. Potential future work includes extending the analysis to multiple illuminators and varying receiver configurations, and confirmation through over-the-air measurements.

</details>


### [108] [MATE: Multi-Agent Traffic Engineering for Topology-Aware Distributed SRv6 Scheduling under Bursty Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01831)
*Yuang Chen,Shuyi Liu,Chengdi Lu,Fengqian Guo,Hancheng Lu,Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出MATE——一种面向部分部署SRv6的混合网络的多智能体主动分布式流量调度方法，采用离线集中训练、在线分布执行的架构，通过区分化主动调度与反事实差异奖励实现近最优的分布式TE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络流量日益动态化和突发化，传统集中式TE因状态聚合、集中计算与策略下发导致控制回路时延过长，难以快速响应突发流量。

Method: 利用历史流量稳定性刻画各流量需求的突发风险，用路径敏感度量化候选路径承接额外流量的能力，实现面向异构流量特征的区分化主动调度；采用离线集中训练+在线分布执行：训练时共享全局critic在由MLU与方差加权路径敏感度构成的奖励下评估联合动作，并用反事实差异奖励为各actor提供个体信用；在线时边缘智能体基于局部观测生成流量拆分策略并经SRv6下发，避免每个调度周期的集中优化。

Result: 在CENI测试床与Sanet拓扑仿真中，MATE在多数场景优于无个体信用分配的多智能体强化学习消融基线、COPE和TEAL，并在实测流量轨迹上接近集中式FIGRET；CENI-Abilene上最多90.1%的测试实例MLU在LP最优值的10%以内；多数故障场景下中位归一化MLU低于FIGRET，神经推理平均耗时2.05–3.14 ms。

Conclusion: MATE验证了在多智能体强化学习框架下以分布式在线推理替代集中式周期优化，可在保证低时延的同时获得接近集中式最优的流量工程性能，且具备故障鲁棒性。

Abstract: As network traffic becomes increasingly dynamic and bursty, conventional centralized traffic engineering (TE) suffers from long control-loop delays caused by network-state aggregation, centralized computation, and policy distribution. In this paper, we propose MATE, a multi-agent TE method for proactive distributed scheduling in hybrid networks with partial deployment of Segment Routing over IPv6 (SRv6). MATE uses historical traffic stability to characterize the burst risk of each traffic demand and path sensitivity to quantify the ability of candidate paths to accommodate additional traffic, thereby enabling differentiated proactive scheduling tailored to heterogeneous traffic characteristics. MATE adopts an offline-centralized training and online-distributed execution architecture. During training, a shared global critic evaluates joint actions under a reward combining maximum link utilization (MLU) with variability-weighted path sensitivity, while counterfactual difference rewards provide agent-specific credit for actor learning. During online execution, edge agents generate traffic-splitting policies from local observations and apply them through SRv6, avoiding centralized optimization in each scheduling interval. Experiments on the China Environment for Network Innovations (CENI) testbed and simulations on the Sanet topology show that MATE outperforms a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) ablation baseline without agent-specific credit assignment, COPE, and TEAL in most evaluated scenarios and approaches centralized FIGRET on measured traffic traces. On CENI-Abilene, MATE achieves MLU within 10% of the linear-programming optimum in up to 90.1% of test instances. MATE also achieves lower median normalized MLU than FIGRET in most tested failure scenarios, with mean neural-inference times of 2.05--3.14~ms.

</details>


### [109] [Interoperability Assessment of Fronthaul and E2 Interfaces for Multi-Vendor Open RAN Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01862)
*Farhad Mehran,Charles Turyagyenda,Dritan Kaleshi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文总结了在SONIC实验室对13套多厂商Open RAN系统的Open Fronthaul与E2接口进行互操作性评估的最新发现与经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Open RAN旨在通过开放接口、解耦与虚拟化RAN组件、数据驱动控制来变革无线网络架构；但Open Fronthaul与E2接口集成困难（如Fronthaul IOT profile需对齐、Near-RT RIC为新型组件带来未预见问题），因此需要依据O-RAN Alliance IOT规范进行严格的互操作性评估以加速可互操作方案的发展。

Method: 基于O-RAN Alliance互操作性测试（IOT）规范，对部署于SmartRAN Open Network Interoperability Centre（SONIC）Labs的13套多厂商Open RAN系统进行Open Fronthaul与E2接口的互操作性评估，并选取其中的发现与经验进行总结。

Result: 本文呈现了针对13套多厂商Open RAN系统在Open Fronthaul与E2接口互操作性评估中获得的若干最新发现与经验教训（摘要未给出具体量化结果）。

Conclusion: 对Open Fronthaul与E2接口开展严格的互操作性评估是推动Open RAN多厂商集成落地的关键环节，其实践经验可为接口profile对齐与Near-RT RIC集成的后续工作提供指导。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Networks (RANs) aim to transform the existing wireless network architectures by adopting open interfaces, disaggregating and virtualising RAN components, and enabling dynamic control of the system via data-driven methods. To accelerate the development of interoperable RAN solutions, rigorous assessment of Open Fronthaul and E2 interfaces based on O-RAN Alliance Interoperability Testing (IOT) specifications plays a key role as the integration of these interfaces seems to be very challenging, e.g. due to the need for alignment of Fronthaul IOT profiles and unforeseen challenges for E2 interface integration as Near-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controller (Near-RT RIC) is relatively a new component introduced within the RAN architecture. In this paper, selected parts of the latest findings and lessons learned from interoperability assessment of Open Fronthaul and E2 interfaces for 13 multi-vendor Open RAN systems deployed in SmartRAN Open Network Interoperability Centre (SONIC) Labs are presented.

</details>


### [110] [PRISM: RL-Based Rate Control for Low Latency and High Data Rate in Mobile Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02032)
*Hamid Hassani,Staji Wilson,Kishor C. Joshi,Sonia M. Heemstra,Ignas G. Niemegeers,George Exarchakos*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: PRISM 是一种基于强化学习的拥塞控制算法，利用峰值 PRB 利用率作为早期拥塞信号来调节发送速率，在移动与衰落场景下可兼顾高吞吐与低时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展现实、远程操作等时延敏感交互式业务需要在高数据速率下保持低时延，而现有 CCA 依赖丢包、时延或 ECN 等拥塞信号，这些信号存在滞后、不精确或需大规模网络升级的问题；PRB 利用率则被提出为移动网络中可靠的早期拥塞信号。

Method: 提出 PRISM：一种基于强化学习的 CCA，采用峰值 PRB 利用率作为拥塞信号，在移动性和衰落条件下调节发送速率；通过 NS3 仿真评估，场景为两个 PRISM 流与两个 TCP Cubic 流、一个 TCP BBR 流共存。

Result: 在 20 分钟高竞争区间内，PRISM 保持每包平均单向时延 6.3ms、p99 单向时延 44.6ms，丢包率可忽略（0.02%）；同时段共存 TCP 流的平均单向时延高达 371–390ms（约 60 倍），p99 为 563–578ms，丢包率 0.1%。PRISM 的"吞吐/时延"性能提升 31 倍。

Conclusion: 以 PRB 利用率为信号的强化学习 CCA 能在高竞争移动场景中显著降低时延与丢包，实现有利于时延敏感应用的良好吞吐-时延权衡。

Abstract: Emerging delay-sensitive interactive services, such as extended reality and remote operation, demand advanced congestion control algorithms (CCAs) capable of maintaining high data rates while keeping latency low in mobile networks. Existing CCAs typically rely on congestion signals such as packet loss, delay, or explicit congestion notification (ECN) marks, which can be delayed, imprecise, or require extensive upgrades to the network infrastructure. To address these limitations, physical resource block (PRB) utilization has recently been proposed as a reliable early congestion signal in mobile networks [1]. This paper introduces PRISM, a reinforcement-learning-based CCA that uses peak PRB utilization to regulate the send rate under challenging mobility and fading conditions. NS3 simulation results show that, in a mobility scenario where two PRISM flows coexist with two TCP Cubic flows and one TCP BBR flow, PRISM maintains a per-packet mean one-way delay (OWD) of 6.3ms and a $p_{99}$ OWD of 44.6ms, with negligible (0.02%) packet loss, over a 20min high-contention interval. However, over the same interval, the coexisting TCP flows experience 60x higher mean OWDs of 371-390ms and $p_{99}$ OWDs of 563-578ms, with 0.1% packet loss. PRISM achieves a 31x improvement in goodput-over-latency performance, indicating a favorable tradeoff for delay-sensitive applications.

</details>


### [111] [QueueTrack: Framework for Detecting the Onset of Queueing in Mobile Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02038)
*Hamid Hassani,Anudeep Karnam,Kishor Joshi,Sonia M. Heemstra,Ignas G. Niemegeers,George Exarchakos*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出端主机框架QueueTrack，利用端主机可观测的包级时序统计量（无需精确同步或网络改造）计算队头堆积指示量QBI，并用机器学习分类器检测移动网络路径瓶颈处队列起始。在OAI 5G测试床（含竞争TCP流量）上验证，逻辑回归等低复杂度模型即可可靠检测，混合TCP/UDP场景F1约80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络中端主机难以观测瓶颈队列状态；丢包、RTT、OWD等传统拥塞信号对早期队列建立检测存在已知局限，导致端到端CCA难以及时准确感知排队起始。

Method: 设计QueueTrack端主机框架：从端主机可观测的包级时序统计中构建队列堆积指示量QBI；构建完整ML流水线（数据采集、标注、训练与测试）；在基于OpenAirInterface的5G测试床上，设置多组竞争TCP流量的场景进行评估。

Result: 低复杂度分类模型（如逻辑回归）即可可靠检测5G基站处队列起始；在具有挑战性的混合TCP/UDP场景中F1分数约80%。

Conclusion: QueueTrack可作为面向低时延CCA开发的实用框架，亦适用于移动网络遥测与监控；其优势在于无需端间紧同步、无需改动现有网络基础设施。

Abstract: In mobile networks, timely and accurate detection of the onset of queueing at path bottlenecks remains challenging for end-to-end congestion control algorithms (CCAs), where end hosts have limited visibility into queue status. Traditional congestion signals, such as packet loss, round-trip time (RTT), and one-way delay (OWD), have well-known limitations for early queue buildup detection. This paper presents QueueTrack, a practical end-host framework to detect the onset of queueing in mobile networks. QueueTrack computes a queue buildup indicator, denoted QBI, from endpoint-observable packet-level timing statistics, without requiring tight synchronization between end hosts or modifications to the existing network infrastructure. We develop and assess the complete QueueTrack machine learning (ML) pipeline, including data collection, labeling, model training, and testing, on a 5G testbed built on the OpenAirInterface platform, under a range of scenarios with competing TCP traffic. The experimental results show that QueueTrack can reliably detect the onset of queueing at the 5G base station using low-complexity classification models such as logistic regression. For example, QueueTrack achieves an F1 score of approximately 80% in a challenging mixed TCP/UDP scenario. These findings confirm that QueueTrack can serve as a practical framework for developing CCAs that target low delay, as well as for network telemetry and network monitoring in mobile networks.

</details>


### [112] [Band-Separated Redundant Communication for a Mars-Analog Rover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02131)
*Zamiul Rashid,Akid Anis,Tanzim Ahmed,Md. Golam Rabiul Alam,Md. Khalilur Rhaman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文描述并实测了BRACU Mongol-Tori火星车（URC参赛）的通信栈：视觉与控制分离在不同无线电和频段，视频/RTK走5.2 GHz主链路+2.4 GHz备份并交由Babel路由选择，指令同时经两个亚GHz电台发送、主路静默时使用备份。台架断电与丢包注入实验及>1 km野外行驶验证了备份切换，同时暴露了Babel仅凭Hello丢包判断链路质量、导致在故障接收机链路和量程边缘振荡的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: URC中队伍远距离、视距外操控漫游车，组委会将通信故障列为队伍失利最常见原因。因此漫游车需要能承载多路视频带宽、且单条无线电失效后仍可存活的指令通路，而现有方案缺乏对真实赛场条件下通信栈的系统验证。

Method: 1）架构：视频与RTK改正数走5.2 GHz桥接（2.4 GHz备份），由Babel路由协议在两链路间切换；指令同时由两个亚GHz电台并行发送，仅当主路静默时使用备份副本。2）台架实验：断电与注入丢包。3）外场实验：漫游车驶离基站超过1 km，视距约在中途中断。

Result: 台架上流量在数秒内切换至备份；通过metric offset设置使流量保持在10%丢包的主链路上。外场中主链路在771 m处失效，备份链路将视频与RTK改正数承载至略超1 km。同时暴露Babel局限：仅依据Hello消息丢失判断链路，即使备份链路报告两倍容量仍将流量留在故障接收机的主链路上，并在量程边缘发生路由振荡（flapping）。

Conclusion: 分离视觉/控制信道加双发指令的通信栈可在单链路失效下保持可用，并在1 km级距离上得到验证；但基于Hello丢包的链路度量不足以反映真实链路容量与硬件故障，需改进链路度量、天线对准与日志记录方法（作为经验教训给出）。

Abstract: Teams in the University Rover Challenge (URC) drive their rovers remotely and often out of sight. The organizers name communication failure as the most common reason a team falls short. A rover therefore needs bandwidth for several camera views and a command path that survives the loss of one radio. We describe and field test the communication stack of the BRACU Mongol-Tori rover, which keeps vision and control on separate radios and bands. Video and RTK corrections travel over a 5.2 GHz bridge with a 2.4 GHz backup, and the Babel routing protocol chooses between them. Every command goes out on two sub-gigahertz radios at once, and the rover uses the backup copy only when the primary falls silent. We tested the backhaul on the bench with a power cut and injected packet loss. We also drove the rover more than a kilometer from the base, and line of sight ended about halfway. On the bench, traffic reached the backup within a few seconds. A metric offset kept it on a primary that lost one packet in ten. In the field, the primary failed at 771 m and the backup carried video and corrections to just over a kilometer. The drive also exposed a limit of Babel, which judges a link only by lost Hello messages. It kept traffic on a primary with a faulty receiver, although the backup reported twice the capacity, and it flapped at the edge of range. We close with lessons on antenna aiming, link metrics and logging.

</details>


### [113] [Wireless Networks That See: Integrated Sensing and Communication in Complex Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02157)
*Xingqin Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本教程阐述如何利用已知蜂窝信号进行传播估计、静止背景抑制、反射路径解读与多站观测融合，实现ISAC感知；并通过UAV信道模型与射线追踪案例说明机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信中的无线电交互会干扰通信，但同时也蕴含环境信息。ISAC将这些交互转化为观测UAV、道路车辆和工业移动机器人的机会。然而强回波不等于可靠观测：静止背景会掩盖运动、反射会产生虚假目标、单一视角会导致部分运动不可观测，因此需要系统性的方法指导。

Method: 教程性方法框架：利用已知蜂窝信号估计传播特性；抑制静止背景杂波；解读反射路径以区分虚假目标；融合不同站点的观测。并以3GPP随机信道模型下的UAV示例及旧金山城市场景射线追踪为例，通过空间布局、信道剖面和距离—多普勒图前后对比说明机制；采用本地校验降低误输出。

Result: 本地校验可减少虚假输出，但会带来一定检测损失；多站协作可提升被单站与协作站点同时检测到的UAV的定位精度。案例展示了信号资源、测量上报与应用需求如何共同决定感知性能。

Conclusion: 信号资源、测量报告机制与应用需求共同塑造感知性能；该教程为复杂环境下的感知服务提供了实用指导。

Abstract: The same radio interactions that complicate wireless communication can reveal what is happening around a network. Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) turns these interactions into opportunities to observe unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), road vehicles, and industrial mobile robots. Yet a strong echo is not necessarily a reliable object observation: stationary background can conceal motion, reflections can create virtual objects, and one viewpoint can leave part of an object's motion unobserved. This tutorial explains how known cellular signals are used to estimate propagation, suppress stationary background, interpret reflected paths, and combine observations from different sites. UAV examples under a Third Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) stochastic channel model and ray tracing in a San Francisco urban scene illustrate the mechanisms through spatial layouts, channel profiles, and before/after range--Doppler maps. Local checks reduce false outputs, with some loss of detection. Cooperation improves position accuracy for UAVs detected by both a single site and cooperating sites. The examples show how signal resources, measurement reporting, and application needs shape sensing performance, providing practical guidance for sensing services in complex environments.

</details>


### [114] [S-R3: Secure and Robust RIS-Assisted RSMA under Finite-Resolution Phase Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02662)
*Ibrahima Sory Diallo,Elhadj Moustapha Diallo,Wang Mi,Mamadou Aliou Diallo,Amayika Kakati*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 研究CSI不完美与有限分辨率RIS相位下的RIS辅助RSMA下行安全传输：公共流承载公开信息、私有流承载机密信息，对窃听者采取保守假设（可消除公共流并将其他私有流作为边信息），构建稳健的预编码、各向同性人工噪声、公共速率分配与RIS相位的联合设计，在速率与功率约束下折中机密与公共吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RSMA的流分层结构天然利于安全设计（公共流可吸收窃听者能力、私有流保护机密），但实际系统中窃听者CSI存在有界误差、RIS相位量化有限，直接优化会高估安全性能；因此需要一个对信道不确定性与离散相位均有效的稳健设计框架。

Method: 直接与级联窃听信道的范数有界误差被转化为在RIS相位更新下依然成立的有效信道不确定性上界；构造保守的泄漏上界与凸的内部发射端更新（预编码、人工噪声、公共速率分配、RIS相位），通过可行性初始化流程启动，并对人工噪声功率与RIS相位做有限候选搜索并显式检验可行性，从而以内-外迭代求解非凸问题。

Result: 理论保证：固定人工噪声功率与RIS配置时，可行内层迭代的目标值单调不降并收敛，相邻迭代差趋于零；外层被接受的更新保持可行且数量有限（算法终止性得到保证）。数值结果未在摘要中给出。

Conclusion: 所提框架将不完美窃听CSI与有限分辨率RIS相位纳入统一的稳健安全优化，并具备可证明的收敛与可行性保证，适用于RIS辅助RSMA的保密与公开吞吐折中设计。

Abstract: This paper investigates a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) downlink with imperfect eavesdropper channel state information and finite-resolution RIS phases. The common stream carries public information, while user-specific private streams carry confidential information. For a conservative security assessment, each eavesdropper is allowed to remove the public stream and is given the other private streams as side information. We formulate a robust design of t he transmit precoders, isotropicartificial noise, common-rate allocations, and RIS phases that balances confidential and public throughput under rate and power constraints. Bounded errors in the direct and cascaded eavesdropper channels yield an effective-channel uncertainty bound valid across RIS phase updates. To address the resulting nonconvex problem, we construct conservative leakage bounds and convex inner transmit updates, initialize through a feasibility procedure, and search finite artificial-noise and RIS-phase candidates with explicit feasibility checks. For a fixed artificialnoise power and RIS configuration, the feasible inner iterates have a nondecreasing objective that converges in value, and their successive changes vanish. Accepted outer updates remain feasible and are finite in number. Our code is available at https://github.com/ABM-ADAM/RIS RSMA-secure.

</details>


### [115] [Multipartite FSO/QKD with N-GHZ States for Scalable Secure Group Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02913)
*Yuma Kaminaga,Hoang D. Le,Cuong T. Nguyen,Anh T. Pham*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出HAP辅助的多方QKD网络架构，结合N-GHZ态协议与TDMA/FSO，通过Qiskit仿真验证其组密钥率与延迟性能优于传统两方方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G需抗量子攻击的组密钥生成机制，而传统QKD仅适用于点对点通信，无法直接用于组密钥建立。

Method: 设计可扩展的高空平台（HAP）辅助多方QKD网络架构，将N-GHZ态协议与TDMA方案集成于自由空间光（FSO）信道；基于Qiskit构建仿真框架，纳入FSO链路真实物理层损伤。

Result: 相较传统两方方案，所提架构显著提升组密钥率（SKR），并有效降低整体网络执行时延；同时揭示了信道损伤的影响，给出最优束腰控制等设计见解。

Conclusion: HAP辅助多方QKD可作为未来大规模6G无线网络安全组通信的有效方案。

Abstract: The emergence of sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks is driving the need for secure group key generation mechanisms resilient to quantum-computing attacks. Conventional quantum key distribution (QKD) protocols are designed for point-to-point communication and are therefore not directly applicable to group key establishment. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a scalable High-Altitude Platform (HAP)-assisted multipartite QKD network architecture that integrates $N$-GHZ-state protocols with a Time Division Multiple Access (TDMA) scheme over free-space optical (FSO) channels. The performance of the proposed system is evaluated using a Qiskit-based simulation framework that incorporates realistic physical-layer impairments of FSO links. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed architecture significantly improves the group secret key rate (SKR) compared to conventional bipartite approaches, while effectively reducing overall network execution delay. In addition, the analysis reveals the impact of channel impairments and provides practical design insights, such as optimal beam-waist control. The proposed framework demonstrates the potential of HAP-assisted multipartite QKD as an effective security solution for future large-scale 6G wireless networks supporting secure group communications.

</details>


### [116] [DisC: Incentive Design for Green, Robust, and Fair Client Self-Participation in OTA Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03024)
*Driss Choukri,Essaid Sabir,Abdelkrim Haqiq*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出DisC——一种用于公平感知、信道感知与能量高效OTA-FL的博弈论分布式客户端选择框架。客户端通过最大化兼顾Pareto公平重要性、学习相关性、OTA失真、能耗与时延的效用函数来选择参与水平；作者证明DisC存在纳什均衡且可建模为精确势博弈，其稳定均衡对应OTA-FL的局部最优工作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTA-FL利用无线多址信道波形叠加提升通信效率，但客户端异构、信道衰落、能量约束与参与不平衡会同时损害公平性与聚合精度。现有面向Pareto公平的OTA-FL方案在"高权重客户端遭遇差信道"时仍会产生较大聚合失真，因此需要兼顾公平、信道与能量的选择机制。

Method: 将客户端选择建模为分布式博弈：每个客户端自主选择参与水平，以最大化综合效用（含Pareto公平重要性、学习相关性、OTA失真、能耗、时延五项）。理论分析上证明该博弈存在纳什均衡，并进一步构造精确势函数，将其表述为精确势博弈。

Result: 证明DisC存在纳什均衡；可形式化为精确势博弈，博弈的稳定均衡点与OTA-FL的局部最优运行点相对应。

Conclusion: DisC为无线与资源约束下的客户端选择提供了分布式、激励相容的替代方案，可替代集中式选择以缓解高权重客户端信道恶化导致的聚合失真。

Abstract: Over-the-Air Federated Learning (OTA-FL) improves communication efficiency by exploiting waveform superposition over wireless Multiple-Access Channels (MAC). However, client heterogeneity, fading, energy constraints, and participation imbalance can degrade both fairness and aggregation accuracy. Existing Pareto-oriented fair OTA-FL schemes may still suffer from large aggregation distortion when highly weighted clients experience poor channel conditions. This paper proposes DisC, a game-theoretic distributed client selection framework for fairness-aware, channel-aware, and energy-efficient OTA-FL. Each client selects its participation level by maximizing a utility that captures Pareto fairness importance, learning relevance, OTA distortion, energy consumption, and delay. We show that DisC admits a Nash equilibrium and can be formulated as an exact potential game, where stable equilibria correspond to locally optimal OTA-FL operating points. DisC therefore offers a distributed, incentive-compatible alternative to centralized client selection under wireless and resource constraints.

</details>


### [117] [Pod-wise Multimodal Federated Learning for Heterogeneous IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03273)
*Joy Okolo,Zihao Ding,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出TIDE框架，为每个传感器单独设编码器与辅助头、仅在持有该传感器的设备“pod”内聚合参数，融合层与任务头全局共享，解决IoT联邦学习中传感器异构与动态在线导致的参数覆盖与跨传感器关系缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习适配IoT感知，但设备搭载传感器不同且周期性上线，参与方传感器集合逐轮变化。朴素平均会让缺少某传感器的设备覆盖其参数，且单传感器设备永远学不到自身传感器与其他传感器的关系；现有方法或假设设备常在线而改造本地训练，或选客户端时不考虑其传感器配置。

Method: Tier-aware Intra-pod Decoupled Encoders (TIDE)：为每个传感器配置独立编码器与辅助头，其参数仅在同一"pod"（持有该传感器的设备集合）内部聚合；融合层与任务头由所有设备共享。

Result: 在三个多模态活动识别数据集上与八个基线对比：ActionSense达78.06%，较次优方法高7.63个百分点；在三个数据集上均以自身一半通信量取得最高精度；等通信量下ActionSense领先，另两个数据集在随机种子波动范围内领先。

Conclusion: 按传感器分层的解耦编码器+pod内部分层聚合可在传感器异构且动态在线的IoT场景下避免参数覆盖，并使单传感器设备间接习得跨传感器关系，同时具备通信效率优势。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is well suited to Internet of Things (IoT) sensing. However, devices carry different sensors and come online in periodic patterns, and as such, the sensors among the participants change from round to round. Under naive averaging, devices without a sensor overwrite its parameters, and a single-sensor device never learns how its sensor relates to the others. Existing methods either redesign local training for mismatched sensors (assuming always-available devices) or select clients without regard to their sensors. We propose Tier-aware Intra-pod Decoupled Encoders (TIDE). Specifically, each sensor has its own encoder and auxiliary head, whose parameters are merged only within the "pod" (i.e., the devices that hold the sensor), while the fusion layer and the task head remain shared by all devices. On three multimodal activity recognition datasets against eight baselines, TIDE reaches 78.06% on ActionSense, 7.63 points above the next method, is the most accurate on all three datasets at half its own communication, and at equal communication leads on ActionSense and is ahead within seed variation on the other two.

</details>


### [118] [From Reactive to Agentic Resilience: Multi-Timescale AI-RAN Orchestration Under Signaling Storms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03406)
*Sotiris Chatzimiltis,Mahdi Boloursaz Mashhadi,Mohammad Shojafar,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 面向Open RAN/AI-RAN的非一致性、计算感知平台，论文将韧性视为覆盖预测、吸收、适应、恢复与演化的全生命周期，提出以智能体AI作为编排层，把慢速大模型推理置于快控制环路之外，用三层架构协调控制层级。以大规模接入信令风暴为例，仿真表明智能体控制器仅用四分之一容量即可逼近全量配置的韧性，自由文本意图到控制指令的落地准确率达94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Open RAN与AI-RAN使RAN成为解耦、计算感知的平台，业务连续性依赖多时间尺度的控制环路，而现有韧性机制多为单阶段、缺乏跨控制层级的协调；同时语言模型推理代价高，不能置于快速控制路径上。

Method: 构建离散事件Open RAN附着仿真器，刻画重试放大、共享计算竞争与非瞬时资源配置；提出三层框架：SMO层编排器设定常驻策略，站点级非实时智能体判决风暴并预判计划性人群，近实时确定性环路执行决策；并提供关于预测时域定理的解析证明及反应式配置极限的推论。

Result: 智能体控制器读取日程事件并提前配置，以约四分之一容量逼近全量服务器池的韧性；一旦洪峰到来，反应式权值调整无法恢复该性能；自由文本意图在未见意图上以94%精确匹配转为控制指令，单条指令可端到端改变实时行为；并量化了延迟、成本与竞争对其的约束边界。

Conclusion: 智能体AI可作为跨控制层级的编排层，实现韧性全生命周期协调，且通过预判式预配置优于纯反应式机制；但受延迟、成本与共享计算竞争限制，需以分层设计将慢推理与快闭环解耦。

Abstract: Next-generation open radio access network (Open RAN) and AI-RAN deployments turn the RAN into a disaggregated, compute-aware platform whose service continuity depends on many control loops acting at different timescales. Resilience in this setting is best seen as a lifecycle spanning anticipation, absorption, adaptation, recovery, and evolution. Yet, existing mechanisms remain stage-specific and are rarely coordinated across the control hierarchy. This paper argues that agentic AI offers an orchestration layer that coordinates this lifecycle, turning operator intent into coordinated action while keeping slow language-model reasoning off the fast control path. Using mass-attachment signaling storms as a running study, we build a discrete-event Open RAN attach simulator that captures retry amplification, shared-compute contention, and non-instantaneous provisioning, and we propose a three-tier framework in which an orchestrator at the service management and orchestration layer sets a standing policy, a per-site non-real-time agent judges storms and anticipates scheduled crowds, and a deterministic near-real-time loop enforces every decision. Across experiments, the agentic controller approaches the resilience of a fully provisioned pool of servers at a quarter of its capacity by reading a scheduled event from a calendar and provisioning ahead of it. No reactive weight setting recovers this once the surge arrives. Free-text operator intents ground into control directives at 94% exact match on previously unseen intents, and a single instruction reshapes live behavior end to end. We also quantify the latency, cost, and contention limits that bound it. The supplementary material provides analytical proof of the derived Theorem on anticipation horizon, as well as the subsequent Corollary results on reactive provisioning limits.

</details>
